maithilapaṇḍitaśrījīvanāthaśarmaviracito-

dīkṣāprakāśaḥ |

sāhitya- śāstri �

paṃ0 rāmatejapāṇḍeyena saṃskṛtaḥ |

sa ca

paṃ0 raghunandanaprasāda śukla,

saṃskṛtapustakālaya, kacauṇīgalī

banārasa siṭī

ityanena prakāśitaḥ |

(dvitīyāvṛttiḥ |)

saṃvat 1991




śrīgaṇeśāya namaḥ

atha dīkṣāprakāśaḥ |

sṛṣṭisthitilayakatrī nagapatiputrī pumarthasandātrī |
kācinnavajaladābhā vidhiharivandyā jayatyādyā || 1 ||

sādhakānāṃ hitārthāya jīvanāthena dhīmatā |
dīkṣāprakāśaḥ kriyate tantrasārānusārataḥ || 2 ||

atha dīkṣānityatā yāmale-

adīkṣitānāṃ martyānāṃ doṣaṃ śṛṇvantu sādhakāḥ |
annaṃ viṣṭhāsamaṃ jñeyaṃ jalaṃ mūtrasamaṃ tathā || 1 ||

adīkṣita kṛtaṃ śrāddhaṃ śrāddhaṃ cādīkṣitasya ca |
gṛhītvā pitarastasya narake cāśu dāruṇe || 2 ||

patantyeva na sandeho yāvadindrāścaturdaśa |
tathāpyadīkṣitasyārcāndevā gṛhṇanti naiva hi || 3 ||

tasmādadīkṣitaṃ dṛṣṭvā sacailaṃ snānamācaret |
dīkṣāgrahaṇakāle tu pāparāśiḥ pralīyate || 4 ||

nirvāṇapadasaṃyukto bahirgacchati tatparaḥ |
sarvvāśrameṣu dīkṣāyā nityatvaṃ paricakṣate |
anīśvarasya martyasya nāsti trātā sureśvari || 5 ||

tathā dīkṣāvihīnasya neha trātā paratra ca |
na tapobhirjjapairhīmairupacāraiśca sādhanaiḥ || 6 ||

divyaṃ jñānaṃ yato dadyātkuryyātpāpasya saṃkṣayam |
tasmāddīkṣeti sā proktā munibhistatravedibhiḥ || 7 ||

dīkṣāmūlaṃ jagatsarvaṃ dīkṣāmūlaṃ parantapaḥ |

p. 2) dīkṣāmāśritya nivasedyatra kutrāśrame janaḥ || 8 ||

adīkṣitaśca maraṇe rauravaṃ narakaṃ vrajet |
tasmāddīkṣāṃ prayatnena gṛhṇīyādāgamānmudā || 9 ||

tathā coktaṃ tārākalpe-

kalpe dṛṣṭvā tu mantraṃ vai yo gṛhṇāti narādhamaḥ |
mavantarasahasreṣu niṣkṛtirnnaiva jāyate || 1 ||

piturmmantraṃ na gṛhṇīyāttathā mātāmahādapi |
sodarasya kaniṣṭhasya vairipakṣātritasya ca || 2 ||

pramādādvā tathājñānātpitṛdīkṣāṃ samācaret |
prāyaścittaṃ tataḥ kuryātpunardīkṣāṃ samācaret || 3 ||

piturdīkṣā yaterdīkṣā dīkṣā ca vanavāsinaḥ |
anāśramāṇāṃ yā dīkṣā sā dīkṣā duḥkhadāyinī || 4 ||

anyatantrepi-

piturmantraṃ na gṛhṇīyāttathā vairāśritasya ca |
kadācidapi gṛhṇāti punaḥ saṃskāramarhati || 1 ||

nirvīryantu piturmmantraṃ śaive śākte na duṣyati |
iti kaulikamantradīkṣāparam |

tathā ca yoginī tantre-

śaktidīkṣāmadhikṛtya pitṛdīkṣāniṣedhāt | mahātīrthe uparāge sati 
sarvatra na doṣaḥ |

tathā ca viṣṇumantramadhikṛtya-

sādhu pṛṣṭantvayā brahman vakṣyāmi sakalaṃ tava |
brahmaṇā kathitaṃ pūrvaṃ vasiṣṭhāya mahātmane |
vasiṣṭhopi svapautrāya matpitre dattavānsvayam |
prasannahṛdayaḥ svacchaḥ pitā me karuṇānidhiḥ |
kurukṣetre mahātīrthe sūryaparvaṇi dattavān |

ityādi vaiśampāyanīyasaṃhitāyāṃ śaunakamprati vyāsavākyam |

p. 3) yoginītantre-

nirvīryantupiturmantraṃ tathā mātāmahasya ca |
svapnalabdhaṃ striyā dattaṃ saṃskāreṇaiva śuddhyati ||

yattu-

sādhvī caiva sadācārā gurubhaktā jitendriyā |
sarvamantrārthatattvājñā suśīlā pūjane ratā ||
guruyogyā bhavetsā hi vidhavā parivarjitā |
striyo dīkṣā śubhā proktā mātuścāṣṭaguṇā smṛtā ||
idantu gurorūpāsitamantraparam |

tathā ca bhairavītantre-

svīyamantropadeśe tu na kuryādgurucintanam | māturityupāsite 
aṣṭaguṇaṃ || anupāsite śubhaphalamityarthaḥ | siddhamantraviṣayaṃ veti 
kecit |

yoginītantre-

svapnalabdhe ca kalaśe guroḥ prāṇaṃ niveśayet |
vaṭapatre kuṃkumena likhitvā grahaṇaṃ śubham ||
tataḥ siddhimavāpnoti anyathā viphalaṃ bhavet |
idantu sadgurorabhāve, tatsambhave tasmādeva grahaṇaṃ kuryāt |
svapne tu niyamo na hīti nāradavacanāt ||

atha gurulakṣaṇam-

śānto dāntaḥ kulīnaśca vinītaḥ śuddhaveśavān |
śuddhācāraḥ supratiṣṭhaḥ śucirdakṣaḥ subuddhimān ||
āśramī dhyānaniṣṭhaśca tantramantraviśāradaḥ |
nigrahānugrahe śakto gururityabhidhīyate ||

atha nindyagurulakṣaṇam-

śvitrī caiva galatkuṣṭhī netrarogī ca vāmanaḥ |

p. 4) kunakhī śyāmadantaśca strījito hyadhikāṅkakaḥ ||
hīnāṅgaḥ kapaṭī rogī vahvāśī bahujalpakaḥ |
etairdoṣairvihīno yaḥ sa guruḥ śiṣyasammataḥ ||

atha śiṣyalakṣaṇam-

śāntaḥ sunītiḥ śuddhātmā śraddhāvāndhāraṇakṣamaḥ |
samarthaśca kulīnaśca prājñaḥ saccārito vratī ||
evamādiguṇairyuktaḥ śiṣyo bhavati nānyathā |
gurutā śiṣyatā vāpi tayorvatsaravāsataḥ ||

tathā coktaṃ sārasaṅgrahe-

sadguruḥ svāśritaṃ śiṣyaṃ varṣamekaṃ parīkṣayet |
rājñi rāṣṭrakṛtaṃ pāpaṃ patnīpāpaṃ svabhartari ||
tathā śiṣyārjitaṃ pāpaṃ guruḥ prāpnoti niścitam |
ataḥ sadgurorāptadīkṣaḥ san sarvakarmāṇi sādhayet ||
tatrāpyanukūlamantraṃ gṛhṇīyāt |

tathā ca-

sutārārāśikoṣṭhānāmanukūlānbhajenmanūn |

tathā ca vārāhītantre-

tārācakraṃ rāśicakraṃ nāmacakraṃ tathaiva ca |
atra cetsadguṇo maṃtro nānyacakraṃ vicārayet ||
idantu pradhānatayā bodhyam |

tathā ca-

dhanī maṃtraṃ na gṛhṇīyādakulañca tathaiva cetyādidarśanāttatta-
ccakravicārasyāvaśyakatvamiti |

nṛsiṃhārkavarāhāṇāntāraprāsādayorapi |
sapiṇḍākṣaramantrāṇāṃ siddhādīnnaiva śodhayet ||

tāraḥ praṇavaḥ | prāsādaḥ hoṃ | sapiṇḍākṣaraḥ kūṭamaṃtra iti |

p. 5) svapnalabdhe striyā datte mālāmantre ca tryakṣare ||
vaidikeṣu ca sarveṣu siddhādīnnaiva śodhayet |

mālamantrastu vārāhīye-

viṃśatyarṇādhikyamaṃtrā mālāmaṃtrāḥ prakīrtitā iti |
haṃsasyāṣṭākṣarasyāpi tathā pañcākṣarasya ca ||
ekadvitryādibījasya siddhādīnnaiva śodhayet ||

haṃsamantrastu-

hakāreṇa bahiryāti sakāreṇa viśetpunaḥ |
haṃsa haṃseti maṃtro'yaṃ jīvo japati sarvadeti ||
kālī tārā mahāvidyā ṣoḍaśī bhuvaneśvarī |
bhairavī chinnamastā ca vidyā dhūmāvatī tathā ||
bagalā siddhividyā ca mātaṅgī kamalātmikā |
ekādaśa mahāvidyāḥ siddhividyāḥ prakīrtitāḥ ||
nātra siddhādyapekṣāsti na nakṣatravicāraṇā |

kālādiśodhanaṃ nāsti na ca mitrādiśodhanamityādivacanāde-
teṣāṃ vicāro nāsti | vastutastu praśaṃsāparamidaṃ | sarvatra 
vicārasyāvaśyakatvamiti sāmpradāyikāḥ | tatrādau kulākulacakramāha |

nibandhe-

vāyyagnibhūjalākāśāḥ pañcāśallipayaḥ kramāt |
pañca hrasvāḥ pañca dīrghā vṛddhāntāḥ saṃdhisambhavāḥ ||
kādayaḥ pañca śaḥṣakṣalasahāntāḥ samīritā iti |

a ā e ka caṭatapayaṣā mārutāḥ | i � ī aikhachaṭhathapharakṣā 
āgneyāḥ |   u-ū- ogajaḍadabalalāḥ pārthivāḥ | ṛ-ṝ au 
ghajhaḍhadhabhavasā vāruṇāḥ | ḷ- ḹ aṅatraṇanamaśā nābhasā iti |

sādhakasyākṣaraṃ pūrvaṃ mantrasyāpi tadakṣaram |

p. 6) yadyaikabhūtaṃ daivatyaṃ jānīyātsvakulaṃ hi tat ||
bhaumasya vāruṇaṃ mitramāgneyasyāpi mārutam |
mārutaṃ pārthivānāñca āgneyaṃ cāmbhasāṃ ripuḥ ||
nābhasaṃ sarvamitraṃ syādviruddhaṃ naiva śīlayet |
pārthive vāruṇaṃ mitraṃ taijasaṃ śatrurucyata iti ||

athodāharaṇaṃ yathā jīvanāthaḥ-

etasyādyākṣarañjakāraḥ | sa ca pārthivaḥ | mantrastu oṃ namaḥ 
śivāyeti | asya prathamākṣaramokāraḥ | ayamapi pārthivaḥ | atra 
dvayorekabhūtatvāduttamo'yaṃ mantraḥ śubhaphalada iti ||
yadi āṃ hrīṃ kromiti mantraḥ | tarhi etasya vāyutattvam | jakārasya 
bhūtattvasya ripurato'yaṃ mantro na śubha iti | mārutaḥ pārthivānāṃ 
ripurityukteḥ |

atha rāśicakram |

āgamakalpadrume-

sūtradvayaṃ pūrvaparāyatañca tanmadhyato yāmyakuberabhedāt |
ekaikamīśānaniśācare tu hutāśavāyau vilikhettato'rṇān ||

vedā 4 gni 3 vahni 3 yugala 2 śrāvaṇā 2 kṣi 2 saṃkhyān
pañce 5 ṣu 5 bāṇa 5 śara 5 pañca 5 catuṣṭayā 4 rṇān |

meṣāditāṃstu 
gaṇayetsakalāṃstrivarṇānkanyāgatānpravilikhedatha śādyavarṇāniti ||

	a ā i ī meṣe | u ū ṛ vṛṣe | ṛ ḷ ḹ mithune | e ai karke | o au siṃhe | aṃ 
aḥ śa ṣa ha ḍha kṣāḥ kanyāyām | kavarga 5 stulāyāṃ | cavargo bṛścike | 
ṭavargo 5 dhanuṣi | tavargo makare | pavargaḥ kumbhe | yavargo mīne | evaṃ 
jñeyam ||

svarāśermantrarāśyantaṃ gaṇanīyaṃ vicakṣaṇaiḥ |
rāśīnāṃ śuddhatā jñeyā tyajecchatruṃ 6 mṛtiṃ 8 vyayam 12 ||

p. 7) yadā svarāśerajñānaṃ tadā sādhakaḥ 
prasiddhasvanāmādyakṣarasambandhinaṃ rāśiṃ gṛhītvā gaṇayet | 
sādhyākṣararāśyantaṃ gaṇayetsādhakākṣarāditi | 

eka 1 pañca 5 nava 9 bāndhavāḥ smṛtā
dvau 2 ca ṣaṭ ca 6 daśamaśca 10 sevakāḥ |
vahni 3 rudra 11 munaya 7śca poṣakāḥ
dvādaśā 12 ṣṭa 8 caturastu ghātakāḥ ||
iti viṣṇuviṣayam |

śaktyādau tu ṣaṣṭhāṣṭamadvādaśaṃ tyajet | tathā ca tantrarāje-

tena mantrādyavarṇo na nāmnā cādyākṣareṇa ca |
gaṇayedyadi ṣaṣṭhaṃ vāpyaṣṭamaṃ dvadaśaṃ tu vā |
ripurmantrādyavarṇaḥ syāttena tasyāhito bhavediti ||

asyodāharaṇam- jīvanāthasya prathamākṣaraṃ vṛścikarāśāvasti 
tena sādhakasya vṛścikarāśiḥ | mantrākṣaramomiti tasya siṃharāśiḥ | 
atra sādhakarāśerdaśamarāśau mantrarāśirato'yaṃ śubhaphalada iti | 
evamanyatrāpi | atha nakṣatracakrametaduktaṃ mantramahodadhau |

tadyathā-

uttarādakṣiṇāgrāstu rekhāstu caturaḥ kramāt |
daśa rekhāḥ paścimāgrāḥ kartavyāḥ sādhakottamaiḥ ||
netra 2 bhū?guṇa 3 veda 4 kṣm 1 dharāyugala2 bhū 1 bhujaḥ 2 |
dvi2 candra1 bhuja2 bahva2 kṣi2 bhūnetra2 tri3 dharā1 guṇāḥ |
ekai 1 ka 1 bhū 1 bhuje 2 ndva 1 kṣi 2 rāma 3 caṃdra 1 uḍuṣvatha |
aśvinyādiṣu vijñeyā ādivarṇāḥ kramādbudhaiḥ |
kṣāntā binduvisargau tu revatyaṃśe vyavasthitau iti ||

yadā maṃtrādyakṣaraṃ ā vā ā tadā tasya bhamaśvinī | 
ikarākṣaraṃ cedbharaṇī | ī u ū cetkṛttikā | eva magrepi | evaṃ 
prathamākṣaravaśānnakṣatranakṣatrāttārājñānaṃ gaṇajñānañca | 
tathaiva sādhakasyāpi prathamākṣaravaśānnakṣatraṃ

p. 8) natratrāttārāgaṇajñānaṃ kuryāt | atrodāharaṇam | oṃ namaḥ 
śivāyeti mantraḥ | asyā'dyakṣaramomiti || okārasya punarvasunakṣatraṃ 
devagaṇaḥ sādhakatārā siṃharāśiriti | jīvanātha iti sādhakaḥ || 
tasyādakṣaravaśāduttarāphālgunīnakṣatraṃ gaṇo naraḥ | tārā pratyariḥ 
| vṛścikarāśiriti |

atha nakṣatrāttārājñānamāha-

janmasampadvi patkṣemapratyariḥ sādhako vadhaḥ |
maitraṃ paramamaitrañca gaṇanīyaṃ svanāmabhāt ||
vipadvadhaḥ pratyariśca tyajedanyaduḍūttamamiti |

	atra sādhakasya uttarāphālgunīnakṣatraṃ 
tasmānmantranakṣatraparyantaṃ saṃkhyā 23 navabhi 9 śśeṣitā 5 
tasmātpratyaritāreti tasmādduṣṭā tārājñeyā |

atha gaṇavicāramāha-

pūrvottarātrayañcaiva bharaṇyārdrā ca rohiṇī |
imāni mānuṣānyāhurnakṣatrāṇi munīśvarāḥ ||
jyeṣṭhāśatabhiṣāmūlaṃ dhaniṣṭhāsarpyakṛttikāḥ |
citrā maghā viśākhā ca etā rākṣasadevatāḥ ||
aśvinī revatī puṣyaḥ svātī hastaḥ punarvasuḥ |
anurādhā mṛgaścaiva śravaṇo devatārakā iti ||
svajātau paramā pratirmadhyamā bhinnajātiṣu |
rakṣomānuṣayornāśo vairaṃ dānavadevayoriti ||

atranaradevagaṇayormadhyamā prītiritivacanānmadhyamatvaṃ | mantrasyeti 
1 
athākaḍamacakram | tadukta mantramahodadhau |
sādhakasya tu nāmādivarṇamārabhya śodhayet ||
mantrādyakṣaraparyantaṃ cakre siddhādike kramāt |

p. 9) janmarkṣottha prasiddhaṃ vā nāma grāhyaṃ praśodhayet |
ūrdhvagāḥ pañca rekhāḥ syuḥ pañca tiryaggatāḥ punaḥ |
koṣṭhāni tatra jāyante ṣoḍaśaivātra saṃlikhet ||

bhū 1 rāma 3 śiva nandā 9 kṣi 2 vedā 4 rka 12 digra 10 sā 6 ṣṭabhiḥ 8 |
kalā 13 manu 14 śarai5radri7 tithi 15 viśvai 13 rmiteṣu ca ||

koṣṭheṣu mātṛkā varṇāstatra nāmāditaḥ kramāt |
siddhaḥ sādhyaḥ susiddhorirjñeyo manvakṣarāvadhi ||
yasmin catuṣke nāmārṇaḥ tataḥ siddhacatuṣṭayam |
prādakṣiṇyāttaddvitīyaṃ sādhyākhyantattṛtīyakam ||
susiddhākhyaṃ caturthantu sapatnākhyaṃ smṛtaṃ budhaiḥ |
ekakoṣṭhe dvayorvarṇaḥ siddhasiddhaḥ prakīrtitaḥ ||
taddvitīye mantravarṇaḥ siddhasādhya udāhṛtaḥ |
tṛtīye siddhasuprasiddhaḥ siddhāriḥ syāccaturthake ||
nāmārṇayukcatuḥ koṣṭhānmanvarṇaśceddvitīyake |
catuṣke tatra pūrvantu yatra nāmākṣaraṃ sthitam ||
tatra tatkoṣṭhamārabhya gaṇayetpūrvavat kramāt |
sādhyasiddhaḥ sādhayasādhyastatsasiddhaśca tadripuḥ ||
evaṃ jñeyaṃ tṛtīye ceccatuṣke mantravarṇakaḥ |
tadā pūrvoktayā rītyā kramājjñeyā vicakṣaṇaiḥ ||
susiddhasiddhastatsādhyastatsusiddhaśca tadripuḥ |
caturthe tu catuṣke syādarisiddhorisādhakaḥ ||
tatsusiddho'ryariḥ paścādevaṃ mantraṃ vicārayet |
siddhasiddho yathoktena dviguṇātsiddhasādhakaḥ ||
siddhasusiddhodrdhajapātsiddhārirhanti bāndhavān |
sādhyasiddho dvighnajapātsādhyasādhyo nirarthakaḥ ||
dvighnajapāttatsusiddhaḥ sādhyārirhanti gotrajān |

p. 10) susiddhasiddhorddhajapāttatsādhyo dviguṇājjapāt ||
tatsusiddho grahādevasu siddhāriḥ kuṭumbahā |
arisiddhaḥ sutaṃ hanyādarisādhyastu kanyakām ||
tatsusiddhaśca patnīghnastadariḥ sādhakāpahaḥ |

anyatrāpi-

siddhaḥ sidhyati kālena sādhyastu japahomataḥ |
susiddho grahaṇādeva ripurmūlaṃ nikṛntati ||
athādau caturasraṃ vidhāya tatra ṣoḍaśakoṣṭeṣu | bhū 1 rāma 3 śiva 11 
naṃdā 9 kṣi 2 vedā 4 rka 12 daśa 10 ṣaḍa 6 ṣṭa 8 ṣoḍaśa 16 caturdaśa 14 
pañca 5 sapta 7 pañcadaśa 15 trayodaśa 13 pratimeṣu koṣṭheṣu 
kramādakārādi varṇānpunaḥ punaḥ saṃvilikhya | koṣṭhacatuṣkaṃ 
siddhasādhyādi bicintya punaścatuṣke siddhādigaṇanā kāryā | 
ayamarthaḥ | yasmiṃścatuṣke nāmādivarṇaḥ syāt | taccataṣkamārabhya 
pradakṣiṇyena siddhasādhyasusiddhārīn gaṇayet | atra 
svanāmādyakṣaramārabhya mantrādimākṣaraṃ yāvat caturbhiścaturbhiḥ 
koṣṭhakaiḥ siddhaḥ sādhyaḥ susiddhaḥ ariśca jñeyaḥ |

taduktaṃ viśvasāre-

indvagnirudranavanetrayugārkadikṣu
ṛtvaṣṭaṣoḍaśacaturdaśabhauti keṣu |
pātālapañcadaśaviśvamite hi koṣṭhe
varṇānlikhellipibhavānkramaśastu dhīmān ||
nāmādyakṣaramārabhya yāvanmantrādimākṣaram |
caturbhiḥ koṣṭhakairekaikamiti koṣṭhacatuṣṭayam ||
punaḥ koṣṭhagakoṣṭheṣu savyato nāmna ādita iti |

atrodāharaṇaṃ yathā | jīvanātha iti sādhakaḥ | oṃ namaḥ śivāya 
iti maṃtraḥ | atra caturthacatuṣke sādhakanāmādyakṣaramasti tadeva 
siddhākhyaṃ prathamaṃ tasmāddvitīyacatuṣke mantrādyakṣaramasti 
tasmātsādhyākhyo

p. 11) maṃtra iti | yatra nāmādyavarṇasthacatuṣke

| tasmāttṛtīye siddhasusiddhaḥ | tasmāccaturthe mantravarṇastadā siddhāriḥ 
| atha yadi nāmādyakṣarayuktacatuṣkoṣṭhānmaṃtrādyavarṇo

prathame susiddhasiddhaḥ dvitīye susiddhasādhyaḥ | tṛtīye susiddhasusiddhaḥ 
| caturthe susiddhāriḥ | evaṃ caturthacatuṣke arisiddhaḥ |  arisiddhaḥ |

evamanyat |

atha prakārāntareṇa siddhādiśodhanamāha-

nāmno mantrasya varṇāśca likhitvā prativarṇakam |
siddhādigaṇanā kāryā yāvanmantrasamāpanam ||
nāmno yadi samāptiḥ syātpunarnāma likhetsudhīḥ |

p. 12) evaṃ saṃsodhiteṣu syurbhūraya sādhyavairiṇaḥ ||
alpāḥ siddhasusiddhāścedaśubhaṃ vyutkramācchubham |
matamitthaṃ tu keṣāñcittadapi prājñasammatam ||

	nāmna iti vyutkramāt | siddhasādhyasusiddhānāṃ bahutve 
rīṇāmalpatve śubhamiti |

atha prakārāntareṇa siddhādiśodhanaṃ mantramahodadhau-

dvādaśāre likheccakre varṇānpūrvāditaḥ kramāt |
īśānāntamakārādyān hāntān bukṣavivarjitān ||
tatra nāmārṇamārabhya mantrādyarṇāvadhikramāt |
gaṇayetsiddhasādhyādi phalanteṣāntu pūrvavat ||

siddho navaikabāṇeṣu 9|1|5 sādhyo rasadigakṣiṣu 6|10|2 susiddhaḥ

gopālaviṣayam |

taduktaṃ vārāhīye-

rāśiśuddhistraipure ca gopāle'kraḍamaḥ smṛta iti |

atha prakārāntareṇa siddhādiśodhanamāha-

catuṣkoṣṭheṣu vilikhedādīnvarṇānpunaḥ punaḥ |
nāmārṇātsiddhasādhyādi jñeyaṃ manvakṣarāvadhi ||

atha prakārāntareṇa siddhādiśodhanam-

nāmno mantrasya varṇāḍhyaṃ caturbhirvibhajetsudhīḥ |
ekādiśeṣe siddhādi jānīyātpūrvavatphalam ||

atha prakārāntareṇādhamaṇaṃmāha-

nāmādyakṣaramārabhya yāvanmantrādimākṣaram |
gaṇayenmātṛkāvarṇakrameṇa guṇayettribhiḥ ||
vibhaktaḥ saptabhiḥ śiṣṭo nāmarāśirudāhṛtaḥ |
evaṃ mantrārṇamārabhya yāvannāmādimākṣaram ||
gaṇayitvā tribhirhatvā vibhajetsaptabhiḥ sudhīḥ |
mantrarāśiḥ smṛtaḥ śiṣṭaḥ pūrvavaddhanitarṇiteti ||

	udāharaṇam | atra jakāramārabhya omityantaṃ saṃkhyā x 37

p. 16) athaprakārāntaramāha-

yadvā mantrākṣarāṇīha svaravyañjanarūpataḥ |
pṛthakkṛtya dviguṇayedyojayetsādhakākṣaraiḥ ||
tādṛśairaṣṭabhistaṣṭairmantrarāśirudāhṛtaḥ |
evaṃ nāmarṇasaṃghopi dviguṇīkṛtya yojitaḥ ||
mantravarṇairaṣṭataṣṭo nāmarāśiḥ smṛto budhaiḥ |
ṛṇitā dhanitā cātra pūrvavatparikīrtiteti ||
uktānyatamamārgeṇa śodhanīyamṛṇaṃ dhanamiti ||

	atrodāharaṇam | oṃ namaḥ śivāya | a u m nmśvy| a a (visargaḥ) i ā a

haratulyaṃ śeṣam |

yo mantraḥ punarjanuṣi sevito nādadātphalam |
paścātpāpakṣaye jāte phalavāptiranehasi ||
āyuḥkṣayādgato nāśaṃ sādhakasya bhavāntare |
ṛṇatvātprāptimātreṇa mantro'bhīṣṭaṃ prayacchati ||
samāṅkau yadyubhau rāśī tadā saṃsevanātphalam |
dhanī mantrastu samprāptaḥ phalatyadhikasevayā ||
mantrāṇāṃ śodhanaṃ bhūyaḥ prakārāntaramucyate |
ṣaṭkoṇe vilikhetpūrvakoṇādyekaikavarṇakān |
akārādihakārāntān napuṃsakavivarjitān |
nāmādyakṣaramārabhya mantrārṇāvadhi śodhayet ||

p. 17) prathame sampadāṃ prāptirdvitīye dhanasaṃkṣayaḥ |
tṛtīye dhanasamprāptiścaturthe bandhuvigrahaḥ ||
pañcame tu bhavedvyādhiḥ ṣaṣṭhe sarvasya saṃkṣayaḥ |
evaṃ saṃśodhitaṃ mantraṃ dadyācchiṣyāya māntrika iti ||

athārimantratyāgaprakāraḥ |

arimantro gṛhītaścedajñānavaśatastadā |
tasya tyāgaḥ prakartavyastatprakāro'dhunocyate ||
sudine sthāpayetkumbhaṃ sarvatobhadramaṇḍale |
vilomaṃ sañjapanmantraṃ pūrayettaṃ supāthasā ||
tatra devaṃ samāvāhya yajedāvaruṇādikam |
tadagre sthaṇḍilaṃ kṛtvā pratiṣṭhāpyānalaṃ tataḥ ||
juhuyānmūlamantreṇa vilomena śataṃ ghṛtaiḥ |
dikpatibhyo balindadyātpāyasānnairghatānvitaiḥ ||
punaḥ sampūjya deveśaṃ prārthayenmanunā'munā |
ānukūlyamanā''locya mayā taralabuddhinā |
yadupāstaṃ pūjitañca prabho mantrasvarūpakam |
tena me manasaḥ kṣobhamaśeṣaṃ vinivartaya ||
pāpaṃ pratihatañcāstu bhūyāchreyaḥ sanātanam |
tato'nu mama kalyāṇaṃ pāvanī bhaktirastu me |
evaṃ samprārthya deveśaṃ karpūrāgurucandanaiḥ |
vilomaṃ vilikhenmantrantālapatre tadarcayet ||
prabudhya nijamaste tatsnāyātkumbhasthitaijjalaiḥ |
punaḥ sampūrya tantoyaistatrāsye mantrapatrakam ||
sampūjya kumbhaṃ nadyāṃ vai taḍāge vā vinikṣipet |
viprānsambhojya mucyeta pīḍayā'sau manūtthayā |
anekadhā śodhane cecchuddhiṃ ca prāpyate manuḥ ||

p. 18) māyāṃ kāmaṃ śriyañcā''dau dadyāttaddoṣamuktaye |
yadvā duṣṭo manurjaptaḥ siddhyetpraṇavasampuṭaḥ |
yadvā kramotkramagayā prajapto varṇamālayā ||
yatra yasya bhavedbhaktirviśeṣaḥ sa manūttamaḥ |
vairikoṣṭhamapi prāpto'bhīṣṭadastasya jāyate ||
		iti ṣaṭcakraśodhanaprakaraṇam |
		atha dīkṣākālavyavasthā |
mantrārambhastu caitre syātsamastapuruṣārthadaḥ |
vaiśākhe ratnalābhaḥ syājjyeṣṭhe tu maraṇaṃ bhavet ||
āṣāḍhe bandhunāśaḥ syācchrāvaṇe vittavarddhanam |
bhādre prajāvināśaḥ syādāśvine ratnasañcayaḥ ||
kārtike mantrasiddhiḥ syānmārgaśīrṣe tathā bhavet |
pauṣe tu śatrupīḍā syānmāghe medhāvivarddhanam ||
phālgune sarvakāmāḥ syurmalamāsaṃ vivarjayet |

caitre tu dīkṣāphalaṃ gopālaviṣayaṃ gautamyuktatvāt | madhumāse 
bhaveddīkṣā duḥkhāyeti vacanānnānyatra | āṣāḍhaviṣedhopi 
śrīvidyātiriktaviṣaye | jyeṣṭhe mṛtyupradā caiva āṣāḍhe dhanasampadeti 
yoginīhṛdayāt | 

atha vāraniyaḥ |

ravivāre bhavedvittaṃ some śāntiḥ prakīrtitā |
āyuraṅgārake hanti tatra dīkṣāṃ vivarjayet ||
budhe saundaryamāpnoti jñānaṃ syāttu bṛhaspatau |
śukre saubhāgyamāpnoti yaśohāniḥ śanaiścare ||

p. 19) viśeṣo gāruḍatantre-

guruvāre sukhaṃ vidyācchaktau cārkaḥ praśasyate |
śive ca guruvāraḥ syātsaure cāndraḥ praśasyate ||
aphalā budhavāre tu dīkṣā jñānavināśinī |

atha tithiniyamaḥ |

pratipadi kṛtā dīkṣā jñānanāśakarī matā |
pratipattirdvitīyāyāṃ tṛtīyāyāṃ śucirbhavet ||
caturthyāṃ vittanāśaḥ syātpañcamyāṃ vittavardhanam |
ṣaṣṭhyāṃ jñānakṣayaṃ proktaṃ saukhyaṃ syātsaptamītithau ||
aṣṭamyāṃ buddhināśaḥ syānnavamyāṃ vapuṣaḥ kṣayaḥ |
daśamyāṃ rājyasaubhāgyamekādaśyāṃ śucirbhavet ||
dvādaśyāṃ sarvasiddhiḥ syāttrayodaśyāṃ daridratā |
tiryagyoniścaturdaśyāṃ hānirmāsāvasānake ||
pakṣānte dharmabuddhiḥ syādasvādhyāyaṃ vivarjayet |
sandhyāgarjitanirghoṣabhūkampolkānipātanam ||
etānaśyāṃśca divasān śrutyuktānparivarjayet |
dvādaśyāmapi kartavyaṃ trayodaśyāmathāpi vā ||
yatra trayodaśīvidhānaṃ tadviṣṇuviṣayaṃ rāmārccanacandrikāghṛtatvāt 
|
trayodaśī ca daśamī praśastāsarvakāmadetisanatkumāravacanācca ||

atha nakṣatranirṇayaḥ |

aśvinyāṃ sukhamāpnoti bharaṇyāṃ maraṇaṃ dhruvam |
kṛttikāyāṃ bhavedduḥkhī rohiṇyāṃ vākpatirbhavet ||
mṛgaśīrṣe sukhāvāptirārdrāyāṃ bandhunāśanam |
punarvasau dhanāptiḥ syātpuṣye śatruvināśanam ||
āśleṣāyāṃ bhavenmṛtyurmaghāyāṃ duḥkhamocanam |
saundaryaṃ pūrvaphalgunyāṃ prāpnotyatra na saṃśayaḥ ||
jñānañcottaraphalgunyāṃ haste caiva dhanī bhavet |

p. 20) citrāyāṃ jñānasiddhiḥ syātsvātyāṃ śatruvināśanam ||
viśākhāyāṃ sukhaṃ cānurādhāyāṃ bandhuvardhanam |
jyeṣṭhāyāṃ putrahāniḥ syānmūlabhe kīrtivarddhanam ||
pūrvāṣāḍhottarāṣāḍhe bhavetāṃ kīrtidāyike |
śravaṇāyāṃ bhavedduḥkhaṃ dhaniṣṭhāyāṃ daridratā ||
buddhiḥ śatabhiṣāyāntu pūrvabhādre sukhī bhavet |
saukhyaṃ cottarabhādre tu revatyāṃ kīrtivardhanam ||

atha yoganirṇayo viśvasāre-

śubhaḥ siddhiśca āyuṣmān dhruvayogastataḥ param |
prītiḥ saubhāgyayogaśca vṛddhiyogastataḥ param ||
harṣaṇāśca tathā yogaḥ sarvatantre sukhāvahaḥ |

atha karaṇanirṇayaḥ |

bavaśca bālavaścaiva kaulavantaitilantathā |
karaṇāni śubhānyeva sarvatantreṣu bhāmini ||

atha lagnanirṇayaḥ |

vṛṣe siṃhe ca kanyāyāṃ dhanurmīnākhyalagnake |
candratārānukūlye ca kuryāddīkṣāpravartanam ||
sthiralagnaṃ viṣṇumantre śivamantre carantathā |
dvisvabhāvagataṃ lagnaṃ śaktimantreṣu śasyate ||

atha pakṣanirṇayaḥ |

śuklapakṣe śubhā dīkṣā kṛṣṇepyāpañcamī tathā |
niṣiddhamāse tu tattu viśeṣo dinabhedataḥ ||

āgamakalpadrume-

ṣaṣṭhī bhādrapade māse tviṣe kṛṣṇā caturdaśī |
kārtike navamī śuklā mārge śuklā tṛtīyakā ||
pauṣe ca navamī śuklā mārge śuklā caturthikā |
phālgune navamī śuklā caitre kāmacaturdaśī ||

p. 21) vaiśākhe cākṣayā caiva jyeṣṭhe daśaharā tithiḥ |
āṣāḍhe pañcamī śuklā śrāvaṇe kṛṣṇapañcamī ||
etāni devaparvāṇi tīrthakoṭiphalaṃ labhet |

anyamatañca-

caitre trayodaśī śuklā vaiśākhe tu haripriyā |
jyeṣṭhe caturdaśī kṛṣṇā āṣāḍhe nāgapañcamī |
śrāvaṇaikādaśī bhādre rohiṇīsaṃyutā'ṣṭamī |
āśvine ca mahāpuṇyā siddhidā syānmahāṣṭamī ||
kārtike navamī śuklā mārgaśīrṣe tathāsitā |
phālgune ca sitā ṣaṣṭhī ceti kālanirṇayaḥ ||

yoginītantre-

anaye viṣuve caiva grahaṇe candrasūryayoḥ |
sūryasaṅkrāntidivase yugādyāyāṃ sureśvari ||
manvantarāsu sarvāsu mahāpūjādine tathā |
sūryagrahaṇakālasya samāno nāsti bhūtale ||
tatra yadyatkṛtaṃ sarvamanantaphaladambhavet |
na vāratithimāsādiśodhanaṃ sūryaparvaṇi ||

evaṃ candragrahaṇepi taduktaṃ matsyasūkte-

na kuryācchaktikīṃ dīkṣāṃ sūryaparvaṇi sādhakaḥ |
na kuryādvaiṣṇavīṃ tāntu yadi candramaso grahaḥ ||
etacca śrīgopālaśrīvidyetaraviṣayam |
amāvāsyā somavāre bhaumavāre caturdaśī |
saptamī ravivāre ca sūryaparvaśataiḥ samā ||
śiṣyamāhūya guruṇā kṛpayā dīyate yadā |
tatra lagnādikaṃ kiñcinna vicāryaṃ kathañcana ||
sarve vārā grahāḥ sarve nakṣatrāṇi ca rāśayaḥ |
yasmi hani santuṣṭo guruḥ sarve śubhāvahāḥ ||

p. 22) yoginītantre-

grahaṇe ca mahātīrthe nāsti kālavinirṇayaḥ |
gayāyāṃ bhāskarakṣetre viraje candraparvate ||
catvare ca mataṅge ca tathā śūnyāśrameṣu ca |
na gṛhṇīyāttato dīkṣāṃ tīrtheṣveteṣu pārvati ||

iti dīkṣākālanirṇayaḥ |

atha mantrāṇāṃ śāradātilakoktadaśasaṃskāraprakāraḥ |
jananaṃ jīvanaṃ paścāttāḍanaṃ bodhanaṃ tathā |
athābhiṣeko vimalīkaraṇāpyāyane punaḥ ||
tarpaṇaṃ dīpanaṃ guptirdaśaitā mantrasaṃskriyā iti ||
mantrāṇāṃ mātṛkāmadhyāduddhāro jananaṃ smṛtam |
praṇavāṃtaritaṃ kṛtvā mantravarṇāñjapetsudhīḥ ||
etajjīvanamityāhurmantratantraviśāradāḥ |
mantravarṇānsamālikhya tāḍayeccandanāmbhasā ||
pratyekaṃ vāyunā mantrī tāḍanaṃ tadudāhṛtam |
vilikhya mantraṃ tanmantrī prasūnaiḥ karavīrajaiḥ ||
tanmantrākṣarasaṃkhyākairhanyādrephena bodhanam | 
svatantroktavidhānena mantrī mantrārṇasaṃkhyayā ||
aśvatthapallavairmantramabhiṣiñcedviśuddhaye |
sañcintya manasā mantraṃ jyotirmantreṇa nirdahet ||
mantre malatrayaṃ mantrī vimalīkaraṇantvidam |

malatraya yathā-

ānabdhaṃ 1 māyikaṃ 2 kārmaṇaṃ 3 ca |
māyikaṃ māyayā cotthaṃ pauruṣaṃ kārmaṇaṃ malam ||
ānabdhaṃ mānasaṃ proktaṃ niṣiddhaṃ tanmalatrayam |
tāravyomātminuyugdaṇḍī jyotirmanuḥ smṛtaḥ ||

tāraḥ praṇavaḥ oṃ daṇḍī anusvāraḥ | vyoma hakāraḥ agniḥ

p. 23) rakāraḥ manuḥ aukāraḥ tena oṃ hauṃ iti jyotirmantraḥ |

kuśodakena japtena pratyarṇaṃ prokṣaṇaṃ manoḥ |
tena mantreṇa vidhivadetadāpyāyanaṃ matam ||
mantreṇa vāriṇā mantre tarpaṇantarpaṇaṃ smṛtam |
madhunā tarpaṇaṃ śaktau śaive dugdhājyakena ca ||
pañcāmṛtena deveśi tarpaṇaṃ vaiṣṇave tathā |
amukamantraṃ tarpayāmīti prayogaḥ |
tāramāyāramāyogo manordīpanamucyate ||

oṃ hrīṃ śrīṃ tato mantrasyaiakākṣaramiti |

japyamānasya mantrasya gopanantvaprakāśanam |

mantragrahaṇavidhiḥ ||

atha dhāraṇayantrasaṃskāravidhiḥ |

svasvakalpoktadravyeṇa yantraṃ vilikhya paṭṭasūreṇa tantunā vā

tannityatā yāmale-

grāhyam |

triguṇaṃ triguṇīkṛtya granthayecchilpaśāstrataḥ |
