atha mālānirṇayaḥ |

padmabījādibhirmālā vacmi jāye śṛṇuṣva tāḥ |
rudrākṣaśaṅkhapadmākṣapūtrañjīva kamauktikaiḥ ||
sphāṭikairmaṇiratnaiśca sauvarṇairvidrumaistathā |
rājataiḥ kuśamūlaiśca gṛhasthasyākṣamālikā ||
aṃgulīgaṇanādekaṃ parvaṇyaṣṭaguṇaṃ bhavet |
putrañjīvaiḥ śataguṇaṃ śataṃ śaṃkhaiḥ sahasrakam |
prabālairmaṇiratnaiśca daśasāhasrakaṃ smṛtam |
tadeva sphāṭikaiḥ proktaṃ mauktikairlakṣamucyate ||
padmākṣairdaśalakṣaṃ syātsauvarṇaiḥ koṭirucyate |
kuśagranthyā koṭiśataṃ rudrākṣaiḥ syādanantakam ||
padmākṣai racitā mālā śatrūṇāṃ nāśakāriṇī |
kuśagranthimayī mālā sarvapāpapraṇāśinī ||

p. 49) putrañjīvaphalakḷptā kurute putrasampadam |
prabālairvihitā mālā prayacchedvipulaṃ dhanam ||

	tripurāmantrajapādau raktacandanabījādibhiratiśastā | gaṇeśajapepi 
raktacandanamālā praśastā ||

vaiṣṇave tulasīmālā gajadantairgaṇeśvare |
tripurāyā jape śastā rudrākṣaistu ca candanaiḥ ||
akārādikṣakārāntā akṣamālā prakīrtitā |
kṣārṇaṃ merumukhaṃ tatra kalpayenmunisammatam ||
pañcāśanmaṇibhirmālā vihitā sarvakarmasu |
kāmyāmapi kare kuryānmālābhāve tu sundari ||

atha karamālā |

anāmyā madhyamārabhya kaniṣṭhādita eva tu |
tarjanīmūlaparyantaṃ daśaparvasu sañjapet |
etacca gopālaviṣayam |

atha śakteḥ karamālā |

parvadvayamanāmāyāḥ parivartena vai kramāt |
parvatrayaṃ madhyamāyāstarjanyekaṃ samāharet ||
śaktimālā samākhyātā sarvatantrapradīpikā |
śrīvidyāyāṃ viśeṣaḥ ||
anāmāyā madhyamāyāśca mūlāgrācca dvayaṃ dvayam |

p. 50) kaniṣṭhāyāśca tarjanyāstrayaṃ parva sureśvari ||
anāmāyā madhyamāyāśca meruḥ syāddvitayaṃ śubhe |
pradakṣiṇyakrameṇaiva japet tripurasundari ||
iti yāmalīyāt |
aṅgulyagre ca yajjaptaṃ yajjaptaṃ merulaṅghane |
parvasandhiṣu yajjaptaṃ tatsarvaṃ niṣphalaṃ bhavet ||
japasaṃkhyā tu kartavyā nāsaṃkhyātaṃ japetsudhīḥ |
prasaṃkhyākārakasyāsya japo bhavati niṣphalaḥ ||
hṛdaye hastamāropya tiryakkṛtvā karāṅgulīḥ |
ācchādya vāsasā hastaṃ dakṣiṇena sadā japet ||
aṅgulīrna vimuñcīta kiñcidākuñcitena ca |
aṅgulīnāṃ viyogācca cchidre ca sravate japaḥ ||
kāmanābhede tvaṅgulīniyamaḥ |
tarjanyaṅguṣṭhayogena śatrūccāṭanakārakam |
aṅguṣṭhamadhyamāyogānmantrasiddhiḥ suniścalā ||
aṅguṣṭhānāmikāyogāduccāṭotsādane mate |
jyeṣṭhākaniṣṭhayogena śatrūṇāṃ nāśanaṃ matam ||
jīrṇe sūtre punaḥ sūtre granthayitvā śataṃ japet |
pramādātpatite hastācchatamaṣṭottaraṃ japet ||
japenniṣiddhasaṃsparśe kṣālayitvā yathocitam |
cchinnepyaṣṭottaraśatajapaḥ | chinnakarabhraṣṭayostulyatvāt |

athāsananiyamaḥ |

kāmyārthaṃ kambalaṃ caiva śreṣṭhaṃ tadraktakambalam |
kṛṣṇājine jñānasiddhirmokṣaḥ śrīrvyāghracarmaṇi ||
kuśāsane mantrasiddhirnnātra kāryā vicāraṇā |
dharaṇyāṃ duḥkhasambhūtirdaurbhāgyaṃ dārujāsane ||
vaṃśāsane tu dāridryaṃ pāṣāṇe vyādhipīḍanam |

p. 51) tṛṇāsane yaśohāniḥ pallave cittavibhramaḥ ||
japadhyānatapohāniṃ vastrāsanaṃ karoti ca |

	atra vastrāsanaṃ kevalavastraṃ niṣiddham | vastrāsanaṃ rogaharamiti 
viśiṣṭaphalajanakatvavidhānāt | cailājinakuśottaramiti bhagavadvacanācca |
	itiśrīmanmaithilajīvanāthakṛtaṣaṭcakravivaraṇadīkṣāvidhāna-
mantrasaṃgrahamālāsaṃskārādividhānaṃ sampūrṇam |

pañcadevatāḥ |

gaṇeśaviṣṇusūryadurgāśivāḥ | pañca ca lokapālāḥ |
gaṇeśaścāmbikāvāyurākāśaṃ cāśvinau tathā |
grahāṇāmuttare pañca lokapālāḥ prakīrtitāḥ ||
indro'gniśca yamaścaiva nir-ṛtirvaruṇastathā |
vāyuḥ somaśca giriśaḥ pannago navamaḥ smṛtaḥ ||
brahmā pūrvāditaḥ sarve dikpālāḥ samudīritāḥ |
vajraśaktirdaṇḍakhaḍgau pāśo'ṅkuśagade api ||
triśūlapadmacakrāṇi tadastrāṇi vidurbudhāḥ | ṣoḍaśa mātṛkāḥ |
gaurī 1 padmā 2 śacī 3 medhā 4 sāvitrī 5 vijayā 6 jayā 7 |
dedhasenā 8 sudhā 9 svāhā 10 mātaro 11 lokamātaraḥ ||
hṛṣṭi 13 puṣṭiḥ 14 tathā tuṣṭi 15 stathātmakuladevatāḥ 16 |

atha puraścaraṇavidhiḥ |

yoginīhṛdaye-

gurorājñāṃ samādāya śuddhāntaḥkaraṇo naraḥ |
mantrasya siddhikāmena puraścaraṇamārabhet ||
jīvahīno yathā dehī sarvakarmasu na kṣamaḥ |
puraścaraṇahīnopi tathā mantraḥ prakīrtitaḥ ||
tasmādādau svayaṃ kuryādguruṃ vā kārayedbudhaḥ |

p. 52) gurorabhāve vipraṃ vā sarvaprāṇihite ratam ||
striyaṃ ca suguṇopetāṃ suputrāṃ viniyojayet |

	tata uttarāyaṇe śuklapakṣe candratārānukūle'hni puraskriyāṃ katu 
sthānaṃ cintayet |

puṇyakṣetraṃ nadītīraṃ guhāṃ parvatamastakam |
tīrtha pradeśaṃ sindhūnāṃ saṅgamaṃ pāvanaṃ vanam ||
udyānaṃ ramyadeśaṃ ca bilvamūlaṃ taṭaṃ gireḥ |
tulasīkānanaṃ goṣṭhaṃ vṛṣaśūnyaṃ śivālayam ||
aśvatthāmalakīmūlaṃ gośālāṃ jalamadhyataḥ |
devatāyatanaṃ kūlaṃ samudrasya nijaṃ gṛham ||
gṛhe śataguṇaṃ vidyādnoṣṭe lakṣaguṇaṃ bhavet |
koṭirdevālaye puṇyamanantaṃ śivasannidhau ||
mlecchaduṣṭamṛgavyāghraśaṅkātaṅkavivarjite |
ekānte pāvane nindārahite bhaktisaṃyute ||
svadeśe dhārmike deśe subhikṣe nirupadrave |
ramye bhaktajanasthāne nivasettāpasaḥ priye ||
eṣāmanyatamaṃ sthānamāśritya japamācaret |
yatra grāme japenmantrī tatra kūrmaṃ vicintayet ||

kūrmasthānamāha gautamīye-

	japasthānabhūmiṃ navadhā kṛtvā pūrvādiṣu koṣṭheṣu 
kacaṭatapayaśavarṇānante lakṣāviti vilikhya madhyakoṣṭhamapi navadhā 
vidhāya tatra pūrvādiṣu svaradvandvaṃ vilikhet | kṣetranāmādivarṇo yatra 
koṣṭhe dṛśyate tadeva dīpasthānamiti | tadabhāvasthānamapi tatraiva |

parvate sindhutīre vā puṇyāraṇye nadītaṭe |
yadi kuryātpuraścaryāṃ tatra kūrmaṃ na cintayet |

devīyāmalepi-

kurukṣetre prayāge ca gaṅgāsāgarasaṅgame |

p. 53) mahākāle ca kāśyāñca dīpasthānaṃ na cintayet |
grāme vā yadi vā vāstau gṛhe ca tacca vicintayet |

	tataḥ parigṛhītakṣetramadhye kūrmacakramavalokya tasya mukhe 
japamaṇḍapaṃ kṛtvā puraścaraṇaṃ kuryāditi |

atha puraścaraṇe bhakṣyābhakṣyaniyamo gautamīye-

puraścaraṇakṛnmantrī bhakṣyābhakṣyaṃ vicintayet |
anyathā bhojanāddevi siddhihāniśca jāyate ||
bhuñjānopi haviṣyānna śākaṃ yāvakameva ca |
payo mūlaṃ phalaṃ vāpi yatra yatropalabhyate ||

haviṣyamāhāgastyasaṃhitāyām-

dadhi kṣīraṃ dhṛtaṃ gavyamaikṣavaṃ guḍavarjitam |
tilāścaiva sitā mudrāḥ kandaṃ kaimukavarjitam ||
nārikelaphalañcaiva kadalī kavalī tathā |
āmramālakañcaiva panasañca harītakīm ||
vratāntare praśastañca haviṣyaṃ manyate budhaiḥ ||

vratāntara iti || haimantikaṃ sitāsvinnamityādi ||

atha varjyāni |

vivarjayenmadhu kṣāraṃ lavaṇantailameva ca |
tāmbūlaṃ kāṃsyapātrañca divābhojanameva ca ||

anyacca-

kṣārañca lavaṇaṃ māṃsaṃ gṛñjanaṃ kāṃsyabhojanam |
māṣāḍhakīmasūrañca kodravāṃścaṇakānapi ||
kauṭilyaṃ kṣauramabhyaṅgamaniveditabhojanam |
asaṃgakalpitakṛtyañca varjayenmardanādikam ||
snāyācca pañcagavyena kevalāmalakena vā |
mantrajaptānnapānīyaiḥ snānācamanabhojanam ||

p. 54) kuryādyathoktavidhinā trisandhyaṃ devatārcanam |
hikkāṃ kāsaṃ tathā jṛmbhāṃ nidrāñca parivarjayet ||

atha bhojananiyamamāha-

jaṭharaṃ pūrayedarddhamannairbhāgaṃ jalena ca |
vāyoḥ sañcaraṇārthāya caturthamavaśeṣayet ||

atha parānnaniṣedhaḥ kulārṇave-

yasyānnapānapuṣṭāṅgaḥ kurute dharmasaṃcayam |
annadātuḥ phalasyārddhaṃ kartuścārddhaṃ na saṃśayaḥ ||
tasmātsarvaprayatnena parānna parivarjayet |
puraścaraṇakāle tu sarvakarmasu śāmbhavi ||
jihvā dagdhā parānnena karau dagdhau pratigrahāt |
mano dagdhaṃ parastrībhiḥ kathaṃ siddhirvarānane ||
atra parānnaṃ bhikṣetaraviṣayam ||

atha jape niyamaḥ |

uktasaṃkhyājapaṃ kuryātpuraścaraṇasiddhaye ||

p. 55) devatāgurumantrāṇāmaikyaṃ sambhāvayandhiyā ||
japedekamanāḥ prātaḥ kālaṃ madhyandināvadhiḥ |
yatsaṃkhyayā samārabdhaṃ tatkartavyamaharniśam ||
yadi nyūnādhikaṃ kuryādvratabhraṣṭo bhavennaraḥ ||

kulārṇavepi-

nyūnātiriktakarmāṇi na phalanti kadācana |
yathāvidhi kṛtānyeva satkarmāṇi phalanti hi ||

muṇḍamālātantrepi-

yatsaṃkhyayā samārabdhaṃ tajjaptavyaṃ dine dine |
nyūnādhikaṃ na kartavyamāsamāptaṃ sadā japediti ||

p. 56) atha jape dvādaśa dharmāḥ kulārṇave-

bhūśayyā brahmacaryatvaṃ maunamācāryasevanam |
nityapūjā nityadānaṃ devatāstutikīrtanam ||
naimittikārcanañcaiva viśvāso gurudevayoḥ |
jape niṣṭhā dvādaśaite dharmāḥ syurmantrasiddhidāḥ ||
strīśūdrapatitabrātyanāstikocchiṣṭabhāṣaṇam | 
asatyabhāṣaṇañcaiva japakāle vivarjayet ||

yoginītantre-

asatyabhāṣaṇaṃ vācaṃ kuṭilāṃ parivarjayet |
satyenāpi na bhāṣeta japahomārcanādiṣu ||
varjayedgītavādyādi śravaṇaṃ nṛtyadarśanam |
abhyaṅgaṅgandhalepañca puṣpadhāraṇameva ca ||
tyajeduṣṇodakasnānamanyadevaprapūjanam |
anyadapi tatraiva |
naikavāsā japenmantraṃ bahuvastrākulopi vā |
patitānāmantyajānāṃ darśane bhāṣaṇe śrute ||
kṣute'dhovāyugamane jṛmbhaṇe japamutsṛjet |
tathā tasya ca tatprāptau prāṇāyāmaṃ ṣaḍaṅgakam ||
kṛtvā samyagjapeccheṣaṃ yadvā sūryādidarśanam | 
ādiśabdādagniṃ brāhmaṇamapi avalokayet ||

athānyattantrāntare-

manaḥ saṃharaṇaṃ śaucaṃ maunaṃ mantrārthacintanam |
avyagratvamanirvedo japasampattihetavaḥ | iti ||

anyadapi-

uṣṇīṣī kañcukī nagno muktakeśo gaṇāvṛtaḥ |
apavitrakarośuddhaḥ pralapanna japetkvacit ||
anāsanaḥ śayāno vā gacchan bhuñjāna eva vā |
rathyāyāmaśivasthāne na japettimirālaye ||

p. 57) mārjāraṃ kukkuṭaṃ śvānaṃ krauñcaṃ śūdraṃ kharaṃ kapim ||
dṛṣṭvācamya japetkarṇaṃ spṛṣṭvā snānaṃ vidhīyate |
evaṃ japaniyamaḥ sarvatra jñeyaḥ | mānase na doṣakṛditi ||

atha mānasajape niyamābhāvamupadiśati tatraiva-

aśucirvā śucirvāpi gacchaṃstiṣṭhan svapannapi |
mantraikaśaraṇo vidvānmanasaivaṃ sadābhyaset ||
na doṣo mānase jāpye sarvadeśepi sarvadā |
japaniṣṭho dvijaśreṣṭho khilayajñaphalaṃ labhet ||
śrīvidyādimantrajape tu tattanmantre viśeṣojñeyaḥ ||

atha japaphalamāha sarvadharme-

japaniṣṭho dvijaśrebheṣṭhokhilayajñaphalaṃ labhet |
sarveṣāmeva yajñānāṃ jāyate'sau mahāphalaḥ ||
japena devatā nityaṃ stūyamānā prasīdati |
prasannā vipulānkāmān dadyānmuktiñca śāśvatīm |
yakṣarakṣaḥpiśācāśca grahāḥ sarpāśca bhīṣaṇāḥ ||
japinaṃ nopasarpanti bhayabhītāḥ samantataḥ ||
yāvantaḥ karmayajñāḥ syuḥ pavitrāṇi tapāṃsi ca |
sarve te japayajñasya kalāṃ nārhanti ṣoḍaśīm ||
māhātmyaṃ vācikasyaitajjapayajñasya kīrtitam |
tasmācchataguṇopāṃśuḥ sahasro mānasaḥ smṛtaḥ ||
mānasaḥ siddhikāmānāṃ puṣṭikāma upāṃśukaḥ |
vāciko māraṇe caiva praśasto japa īritaḥ |

atha mānasādijapalakṣaṇam |

dhiyā yadakṣaraśreṇī varṇasvarapadātmikām |
uccaredarthamuddiśya mānasaḥ + sa japaḥ smṛtaḥ ||

p. 58) jihnoṣṭhaucālayetkiñciddevatāgatamānasaḥ |
kiñcicchravaṇayogyaḥ syādupāṃśuḥ sa japaḥ smṛtaḥ ||
mantramuccārayedvācā + vācikaḥ sa japaḥ smṛtaḥ |

viśuddheśvaratantrepi-

nijakarṇāgocaro yo mānasaḥ sa japaḥ smṛtaḥ |
upāṃśurnijakarṇasya gocaraḥ parikīrtitaḥ ||
vācikastu janairvedyastrividhoṃ japa īritaḥ |
uccairjapādviśiṣṭaḥ syādupāṃśurdaśabhirguṇaiḥ ||
jihvājapācchataguṇaḥ sahasro mānasaḥ smṛtaḥ |

tantrāntare ca-

uccairjapo'dhamaḥ prokta upāṃśurmadhyamaḥ smṛtaḥ |
uttamomānaso devi trividhaḥ kathito japaḥ ||
atihrasvo vyādhiheturatidīrgho vasukṣayaḥ |
akṣarākṣarasaṃyuktaṃ japenmauktikahāravat ||
manasā yaḥ smaretstotraṃ vacasā vā manuṃ japet |
ubhayaṃ niṣphalaṃ yāti bhinnabhāṇḍodakaṃ yathā ||

atha mantrasya prabhutvakāraṇamāha gautamīye-

paśubhāve sthitā mantrāḥ kevalā varṇarūpiṇaḥ |
sauṣumṇādhvanimantrāste prabhutvaṃ yānti tajjapāt ||
mantrākṣarāṇi cicchaktau proktāni paribhāvayet |
tāmeva paramavyomni paramānandavṛṃhite ||
darśayatyātmasadbhāvaṃ pūjāhomādibhivinā |

p. 59) 					kulavarṇe tu viśeṣaḥ |

mano'nyatra śivonyatra śaktiranyatra mārutaḥ |
na siddhyati varārohe kalpakoṭiśatairapi ||

atha mantrasya sūtakadvayamāha tatraiva-

jātasūtakamādau syādante ca mṛtasūtakam |
sūtakadvayasaṃyukto yo mantraḥ sa na siddhyati ||
brahmabījaṃ manau datvāṃ cādyante parameśvari |
saptavāraṃ japenmantraṃ sūtakadvayamuktaye ||

	brahmabījamoṅkāraḥ saptadhā praṇavamādau prajapya tato 
maṃtraṃ japtvā cānte punaḥ saptadhā praṇavaṃ japediti ||
athānyat-

mantrārthaṃ mantracaitanyaṃ yonimudrāṃ na vetti yaḥ
śatakoṭijapenāpi tasya siddhirna jāyate ||
luptabījāśca ye mantrā na dāsyanti phalaṃ priye |
mantrāścaitanyasahitāḥ sarvasiddhikarāḥ smṛtāḥ ||
caitanyarahitā mantrāḥ proktā varṇāstu kevalam |
phalaṃ naiva prayacchanti kalpakoṭiśatairapi ||
mantroccāre kṛte yādṛk mvarūpaṃ prathamaṃ bhavet |
śate sahasre lakṣe vā koṭijapte na tatphalam ||
hṛdaye granthibhedaśca sarvāvayavavarddhanam |
ānandāśrūṇi pulako dehāveśaḥ kuleśvari ||
gadnadoktiśca sahasā jāyate nātra saṃśayaḥ |
sakṛduccaritepyevaṃ mantre caitanyasaṃyute ||
dṛśyante pratyayā yatra pāramparyantaducyate ||

atha mantrasamarpaṇaprakāro gautamīye-

evaṃ japaṃ purā kṛtvā tejorūpaṃ samarpayet |
devasya dakṣiṇe haste kuśapuṣpārghavāribhiḥ ||

p. 60) saphalaṃ tadvibhāvyaivaṃ prāṇāyāmaṃ samācaret |
japasyādau japānte ca tritayaṃ tu samācaret ||

śaktipakṣe vāmahaste japaṃ samarpayet |

evaṃ japaṃ purā kṛtvā gandhākṣatakuśodakaiḥ |
japaṃ samarpayeddevyā vāmahaste vicakṣaṇaḥ ||
japānte homayenmantrī pratyahaṃ taddaśāṃśataḥ |
tarpaṇaṃ cābhiṣekaṃ ca taddaśāṃśaṃ tato muneḥ |
pratyahaṃ bhojayedviprān nyūnādhikapraśāntaye |
athavā sarvapūrtau ca homādikamathācaret ||

homādyaśaktau sanatkumārīye |

yadyadaṅgaṃ bhavedbhaṃgaṃ tatsaṅkhyādviguṇo japaḥ |
homābhāve japaḥ kāryo homasaṃkhyā caturguṇaḥ ||
viprāṇāṃ kṣatriyāṇāñca rasasaṃkhyāguṇaḥ smṛtaḥ |
vaiśyānāṃ vasusaṃkhyātameṣāṃ strīṇāmayaṃ vidhiḥ |

atrāpyaśaktaḥ |

tatprakāro yoginīhṛdaye-

homakarmaṇyaśaktānāṃ viprāṇāndviguṇo japaḥ |
itareṣañca varṇanāntriguṇādisamanvitaḥ ||
ityanena strīśūdrayorhomādhikāraḥ | tacca brāhmaṇadvāraiva ||

tathā ca tantrāntare-

oṃ kāroccāraṇāddhomācchālagrāmaśilārcanāt | 
brāhmaṇīgamanāccaiva śūdraścāṃḍālatāmbrajet |
iti sākṣānniṣedhāt | tathā praṇavādiśca yo mantro na strīśūdre 
praśasyata iti | sarvastrīṇāṃ śūdratulyatā jñeyā ||

tathāgastyasaṃhitāyām-

yadi home na śaktaḥ syātpūjāyāṃ tarpaṇepi vā |
tāvatsaṃkhyājapenaiva brāhmaṇārādhanena vā ||

p. 61) bhavedaṃgadvayenaiva puraścaraṇakarmaṇi |
gurave dakṣiṇāṃ dattvā bhojanācchādanānapi ||
gurusantoṣamātreṇa mantrasiddhirbhaveddhruvam |
gurumūlamidaṃ sarvamityāhustantravedinaḥ ||
gurureva parabrahma tasmādādau tamarcayet |
tadante mahatīṃ pūjāṃ kuryātsādhakasattamaḥ ||
suvāsinīṃ kumārīñca bhūṣaṇairapi bhūṣayet |
miṣṭānnaphalamūlaiśca bhojayettāṃ yathāvidhi ||
evaṃ siddhamanurmantrī sādhayetsakalepsitān ||
	atha grahaṇakālikapuraścaraṇavidhiḥ |

tatraiva-

athavānyaprakāreṇa puraścaraṇamiṣyate |
grahaṇe'rkasya cendorvā śuciḥ pūrvamupoṣitaḥ ||
nadyāṃ samudragāminyāṃ nābhimātrodake sthitaḥ |
sparśādvimuktiparyantaṃ japenmantramananyadhīḥ ||

nadyabhāve tu tatraiva-

yadvā puṇyodake snātvā śuciḥ pūrvamupoṣitaḥ |
grahaṇādivimokṣāntaṃ japenmantraṃ samāhitaḥ ||

muṇḍamālāyām-

dṛṣṭvā snātvā susaṅkalpo vimokṣāntaṃ japaṃ caret |
tāvaddhomādikaṃ kuryādgrahaṇānte śuciḥ pumān ||
evaṃ japānmantrasiddhirbhavetyeva na saṃśayaḥ | iti |

grahaṇe puraścaraṇasya mukhyatāmāha-

śrāddhādāvanurodhena tyajejjāpyaṃ naro yadi |
sa bhaveddevatādrohī pitṝn sapta nayatyadhaḥ ||
japayajñaṃ parityajya pitṛyajñaṃ karoti yaḥ |
sa bhaveddevatādrohī pitṝn sapta nayatyadhaḥ ||

p. 62) sanatkumārīyavacanājjapasyāvaśyakatvam | vastutastu 
ārabdhapuraścaraṇaviṣayakamidam ||

pauraścarye samārabdhe tatra cedgrahaṇaṃ bhavet |
tadā śraddhānurodhena japaṃ naiva parityajet | ityukteḥ |

atha puraścaraṇamāha |

japahomau tarpaṇañcābhiṣeko viprabhojanam |
pañcāṅgopāsanaṃ loke puraścaraṇamiṣyate ||

	grahaṇādau tu japamātrameva puraścaraṇam | ata eva 
paṃcāṅgasvarūpapuraścaraṇe kṛte mukhyaprayoge'pyadhikāra iti | 
daśakaraṇe kevalajapamātre puraścaraṇe nādhikāra iti tattvam | atha 
puraścaraṇakālobhihito vārāhīye-

candratārānukūle ca śuklapakṣe śubhe'hani |
ārabheta puraścaryāṃ harau supte na cācaret ||
grahaṇe ca mahātīrthe na kālamavadhārayet |

atha puraścaraṇaprayogaḥ |

	tatra tāvadbhūmeḥ parigrahaṃ kṛtvā puraścaraṇakṛnnaraḥ 
prāktṛtīyadivase kṣaurādikaṃ vidhāya vedikācaturdikṣu krośamātraṃ 
krośadvayaṃ vā yāvadiṣṭaṃ vā kṣetraṃ caturasram 
āhārādivihārārthaṃ parikalpya kūrmacakrānuguṇaṃ maṇḍapaṃ 
vidhāya ekabhuktañca kuryāt | tataḥ paradine snānādikaṃ vidhāya 
śuddhaḥ san aśvatthodumbaraplakṣāṇāmanyatamasya vitastimātrān 
daśakīlakān oṃ astrāya phaḍiti mantreṇāṣṭottaraśatābhimantritān 
daśadikṣu nikhanet | oṃ tatsat adyetyādi amukagotrasyāmukaśarmaṇe 
mamaitatkṣetrasya sakalavighnoccāṭanapūrvakasveṣṭamantrasiddhikāmaḥ 
pūrvādidaśadikṣu kīlāropaṇaṃ 
gaṇapativāstupuruṣakṣetrapālapūjanaṃ cāhiṅkaraṣye |

yathā-

oṃ ye cātra vighnakartāro divi bhuvyantarikṣagāḥ |

p. 63) vighnabhūtāśca ye cānye mama mantrasya siddhiṣu ||
mayaitatkīlitaṃ kṣetraṃ parityajya vidūrataḥ |
apasarpantu te sarve nirvighnaṃ siddhirastu me ||

	ityanena nikhanya teṣu oṃ namaḥ sudarśanāya astrāya phaḍiti 
mantreṇāstraṃ sampūjya pūrvādikrameṇa indrādilokapālānpūjayet | 
yathā | oṃ bhūrbhuvaḥ svarindralokapāla ihāgacchetyāvāhya 
pādyādibhiḥ sampūjya svasvadikṣu vahnyādilokapālānpūjayedyathā | 
tato madhyasthāne kṣetrapālaṃ vāstvīśañca sampūjya 
sarvavighnavināśārthaṃ gaṇapatiṃ pūjayet | yathā oṃ 
adyetyādyamukagotro'mukadevaśarmā 
matkattavyāmukamantrapuraścaraṇakarmaṇi sarvavighnavināśārthaṃ 
gaṇeśapūjanamahaṅkariṣye | iti saṅkalpya vedikāmadhye 
pañcopacārairgaṇeśaṃ pūjayet |

uktañca-

grāma krośamitaṃ sthāna nadyādau svecchayā punaḥ |
nagarādāvapi krośakrośayugmamathāpi vā ||
āhārādivihārārthantāvatīṃ bhūmimākramet |
kṣetraṃ vā yāvadiṣṭantu bihārārthaṃ prakalpayet ||
kṣīravṛkṣodbhavān kīlānastramantrābhimantritān |
nikhaneddaśadigbhāge teṣvastraṃ ca prapūjayet ||
lokapālānpunasteṣu gandhādyaiḥ pūjayetsudhīḥ |
kṣetrapālādikaṃ tatra pūjayedvidhivattataḥ ||
kṣetreśaṃ vāstunāmānaṃ vighnarājaṃ samarcayet |
dikpatibhyo baliṃ dadyāttataḥ kṣetraṃ samāviśet ||

indra 1 agniḥ 2 yamaḥ 3 nir-ṛtiḥ 4 varuṇaḥ 5 vāyuḥ 6 somaḥ 7 
īśānaḥ 8 brahmā 9 ūrdhvam | anantaḥ 10 adhaḥ r-indreśānayormadhye 
brahmāṇaṃ paścimanir- ṛtikoṇayormadhye anantam |
atha tato māṣabhaktādinā pūjitadevatābhyo baliṃ dadyāt |
oṃ raudrāraudrakarmāṇau raudrasthānanivāsinaḥ |

p. 64) māratopyugrarūpāśca gaṇādhipatayaśca ye ||
vighnabhūtāśca ye cānye digvidikṣu samāśritāḥ |
sarve te prītamanasaḥ pratigṛhṇatvime balim ||

ityanenāṣṭadikṣu bhūtebhyo baliṃ dadyāt | tato bhūtabalidānaṃ |
bhūtāni yānīha vasanti loke baliṃ gṛhītvā vidhivatpratyuktam |
anyatra vāsaṃ parikalpayantu kṣamantu tānyatra namo'stu tebhyaḥ ||
iti paṭhitvā bhūtabaliṃ dadyāt | tato gāyatrīṃ japet |
prātaḥ snātvā tu gāyatryāḥ sahasraṃ prayato japet |
jñātājñātasya pāpasya kṣayārthaṃ prathamantataḥ || iti 
vidyādharācāryaḥ |

yattu-

prātaḥ snātvā tu sāvitryā ayutaṃ prayato japet |

	tatpunaratyantapāpaśaṅkayā | saṅkalpo yathā | oṃ adyetyādyamuka-
gotro'mukaśarmā jñātājñātapāpakṣayakāmo'ṣṭottarasahasra-
gāyatrījapamahaṃ kariṣye | tata upavāsaṃ haviṣyānna vā kuryāt | tataḥ 
paradine prātaḥ snātvā svastivācanapūrvakaṃ saṃkalpaṃ kuryāt | oṃ 
tatsat adyetyādyamukagotro'mukaśarmā amukadevatāyā 
amukamantrasiddhipratibandhakāśeṣaduritakṣayapūrvakatattanmantrasiddhi-
kāmodyādiyathākālamamukamantrasya yatsaṃkhyakaṃjapaṃ 
taddaśāṃśahomataddaśāṃśatarpaṇataddaśāṃśābhiṣekataddaśāṃ-
śabrāhmaṇabhojanarūpapuraścaraṇamahaṃ kariṣye | iti saṅkalpya 
bhūtaśuddhiprāṇāyāmādikaṃ kuryāt | svasvapaddhatyuktakrameṇa 
devatāṃ sampūjya dīpe prajvalite devatāṃ hṛdaye kṛtvā 
prātaḥkālamārabhya madhyadina yāvajjapaṃ kuryāt | tato 
homastatastarpaṇaṃ tatobhiṣekastato brāhmaṇabhojanaṃ kuryāt |

uktañca kulārṇave-

tarpaṇaṃ ca tataḥ kuryāttīrthodaiścendumiśritaiḥ |
jalaṃ devaṃ samāvāhya pādyādyairupacārakaiḥ ||
sampūjya vidhivadbhaktyā parivārasamanvitam |

p. 65) ekaikāñjalitoyena parivārānpratarparyet ||
tato homaṃ daśāṃśena tarpayetparadevatām |

tathā ca-

tarpaṇavākyaṃ tu | mūlaṃ namaḥ amukadevatā tarpayāmi namaḥ iti 
| abhiṣekavakyantu mūlaṃ namaḥ amukadevatāmahamabhiṣiṃcayāmi | iti 
kalaśamudrayā mūrdhni abhiṣiṃcet |

namonte mūlamuccārya tadante devatābhidhām | 
dvitīyāntamahaṃ pañcādabhiṣaṃcāmyanena tu ||
abhiṣiṃcetsvamūrddhāmantoye kumbhāmayamudrayāna ||

	śaktiviṣaye tu mantrāntenāma coccārya siṃcayāminamaḥ padamiti 
nīlatantrāt | mūlamamukadevatāṃ tarpayāmi namaḥ iti vākyam | tato 
brāhmaṇabhojanam | tato dakṣiṇā | adya kṛtaitadamukadevatāyā 
amukatantrasya puraścaraṇakarmaṇaḥ sāṃgatāsiddhyarthaṃ 
dakṣiṇāmidaṃ kāṃcanamagnidaivataṃ yathānāmagotrāya gurave 
tubhyamahaṃ sampradade | tadabhāve tatputrādibhyo deyā | 
tataśchidrāvadhāraṇaṃ kuryāt |

atha grahaṇakālikapuraścaraṇasaṅkalpaḥ |

	adyetyādyamukagotro'mukaśarmā rāhugraste divākare niśākare 
vā amukadevatāyā amukamantrasiddhikāmo grāsādvimuktiparyantaṃ 
japarūpapuraścaraṇamahaṃ kariṣye | iti saṃkalpya japet | taddine tatparadine 
vā snānādikaṃ vidhāya | oṃ adyetyādyamukamantrasya kṛtaitadgrahaṇa-
kālīye yatsaṃkhyākajapataddaśāṃśahomataddaśāṃśatarpaṇatadda-
śāṃśābhiṣekataddaśāṃśabrāhmaṇabhojanakarmāṇyahaṃ kariṣye | iti 
saṃkalpa homādikaṃ kuryāt | dakṣiṇādānantu pūrvavat |

iti dīkṣāprakāśe puraścaraṇaprakāraḥ |

atha puraścaraṇe kṛtepi yadi mantrasiddhirna syāttadā mantrasya 

p. 66) daśa saṃskārāḥ kartavyāḥ | tathā kṛtepi yadi mantrasiddhirna 
bhavati tadā drāvaṇādikaṃ kuryāt |

taduktaṃ mahātantre-

drāvaṇaṃ bodhanaṃ vaśyaṃ pīḍanaṃ śoṣaṇapoṣaṇe |
dahanañca budhaḥ kuryāttadā siddho bhavenmanuḥ ||

athaiṣāṃ prayogo yathā-

	tatrādau drāvaṇaprayogaḥ || vamiti varuṇavījagrathitaṃ 
mūlamantraṃ karpūrakuṃkumagorocanamanaḥśilośīraiḥ 
cakrarūpayantramadhye vilikhya kasmiṃścitpātre 
dugdhaghṛtamadhujalānyekīkṛtya tatra yantraṃ nidhāya 
pūjājapahomādikaṃ kuryāt | tato mantraḥ siddho bhavati |

atha granthanaprakāraḥ |

eko mantrasya varṇaḥ syāttato nāmākṣaraṃ punaḥ |
mantrārṇo nāma varṇaścetyevaṃ granthanamīritam |
evaṃ kṛtvā jape 108 tataḥ siddho bhavati |

atha bodhanam |

	vāgbhavabījena aimiti bījena sampuṭitaṃ mūlamantraṃ japet | yathā 
aiṃ rāṃ rāmāya namaḥ aiṃ || iti bodhanam ||

atha vaśīkaraṇam |

	bhūrjapatre 
raktacandanadāruharidrākastūrīmanaḥśilābhirmūlamantramālikhya 
kaṇṭhe sandhārya japet |

atha pīḍanam |

tatrādharottarabhāvena mantrapadāni mantrākṣarāṇi prajapya 
adharottarabhāvena devaṃ dhyātvā arkadugdhena yantramālikhya 
tatpādenākramyādharottarapaṭhitamantreṇa juhuyāt |

atha poṣaṇam |

	bālātṛtīyabījena sauḥ iti bījena puṭitaṃ mantraṃ 
madhugodugdhābhyāṃ bhūrjādau vilikhya dhārayet |

p. 67) 				atha śoṣaṇam |

	tatra yajñabhasmanā vāyubījavidarbhitaṃ mūlaṃ vilikhya kaṇṭhe 
dharayet | vidarbhaṇaṃ yathā | yaṃ yaṃ rāṃ yaṃ yaṃ rāṃ yaṃ yaṃ 
mā yaṃ yaṃ yaṃ yaṃ yaṃ naṃ yaṃ yaṃ maḥ yaṃ yaṃ iti |

ādau mantrākṣaradvandvamekaṃ nāmākṣaraṃ tataḥ |
evaṃ punaḥ punaḥ prokto vidarbho mantravittamairiti ||

atha dahanam |

	pālāśabījatailena bahnibījasampuṭitamantrasya ekaikamakṣaraṃ 
mātṛkāvarṇaiḥ puṭīkṛtya mantravidyāṃ viśeṣataḥ |

śatamaṣṭottaraṃ pūrvaṃ prajapetkulasiddhaye |
mātṛkāpuṭitaṃ kṛtvā madhye varṇaṃ nidhāya ca ||
mantravarṇāstataḥ kuryādbodhanaṃ mantrasammatam |
tārasampuṭito vāpi duṣṭamantropi siddhyati ||

ityanyaḥ prakāraḥ |

taduktañcamahātantre-

drāvaṇaṃ bodhanaṃ vaśyaṃ pīḍanaṃ poṣaṇaśoṣaṇe |
dahanañca budhaḥ kuryāttataḥ siddho bhavenmanuḥ ||
drāvaṇaṃ varuṇabījena granthanaṃ kramayogataḥ |
tanmantraṃ yantra ālikhya śilākarpūrakuṅkumaiḥ ||
uśīragorocanābhyāṃ ca mantraṃ saṃgrathitaṃ likhet |
kṣīrājyamadhutoyānāṃ madhye taṃ likhitaṃ kṣipet ||
pūjanājjapanāddhomāddrāvitaḥ phalado bhavet |
dravitopi na siddhaścedbodhanaṃ tasya kārayet ||
bālātṛtīyabījena sampuṭīkṛtya taṃ japet |
evaṃ buddho bhavetsiddho no cettasya vaśaṃ kuru ||

p. 68) kucandanaṃ tathā dāruharidrāmadanaṃ śilā |
etaistu likhito mantro bhūrjapatre suśobhane ||
kaṇṭhe dhṛto bhavetsiddho nocetsantāḍanaṃ kuru |
adharottararūpeṇa padāni parijapya vai ||
dhyāyīta devatāṃ tadvadadharottararūpiṇīm |
vidhyāyādityadugdhena likhitvākramya cāṃghṛṇā |
tathābhūtena mantreṇa homaḥ kāryo dine dine |
pīḍito lajjayāviṣṭaḥ siddhaścenna hi śoṣayet ||
bālātṛtīyabījena puṭitaṃ dugdhamadhyataḥ |
dhārayellikhitaṃ mantramathavā śoṣaṇaṃ caret ||
dvābhyāñca vāyubījābhyāṃ likhenmantraṃ vidarbhitam |
tadyajñabhasmanā kaṇṭhe dhārayedapi sādhakaḥ ||
nocedvāhyognibījena siddhyarthaṃ parameśvari |
mantrārṇān vahnibījena caturdikṣu samāvṛtān |
likhetpālāśatailena dhārayetkaṇṭhadeśataḥ |
siddhaḥ syānnātra sandeho mantra ityāha śaṃkaraḥ ||

iti śrīmanmaithilajīvanāthakṛte dīkṣāprakāśe 
sveṣṭamantrasiddhikathanaṃ sampūrṇam |

atha pañcāyatanī pūjā |

yāmale-

bhavānīṃ tu yadā madhye aiśānyāmacyutaṃ yajet |
āgneyyāṃ pañcavaktraṃ ca nair-ṛte gaṇanāyakam ||
vāyavyāṃ tapanaṃ caivaṃ pūjākrama udāhṛtaḥ || 1 ||

madhye yadā tu govindamaiśānyāṃ śaṃkaraṃ yajet |
āgneyyāṃ gaṇanāthaṃ ca nair-ṛtyāntapanaṃ tathā ||

p. 69) vāyavyāmambikāṃ caiva bhogamokṣapradāyikām || 2 ||

śaṃkarantu yadā madhye aiśānyāmacyutaṃ yajet |
āgneyyāntapanaṃ caiva nair-ṛtyāṃ gaṇanāyakam ||
vāyavyāmambikāñcaiva svargamokṣapradāyinīm || 3 ||

ādityantu yadā madhye aiśānyāṃ śaṃkaraṃ yajet |
āgneyyāmambikāṃ devīṃ svargasādhanabhūmikām || 4 ||

gaṇanāthaṃ yadā madhye aiśānyāṃ keśavaṃ yajet |
āgneyyāmīśvaraṃ caiva nair-ṛtyāṃ tapanaṃ tathā |
vāyavyāṃ pārvatīñcaiva pūjayenmokṣasādhinīm || 5 ||

atra pūjyapūjakayormadhye prācī jñeyā | devatāpṛṣṭhe paścimā 
dik devatādakṣe dakṣiṇā devatā vāme uttarā jñeyā |

pūjyapūjakayormadhye prācī jñeyā vicakṣaṇaiḥ |
paścimā devatāpṛṣṭhe taddakṣe dakṣiṇā bhavet ||
uttarā ca bhavedvāme devatāyāḥ sureśvari |

tathā ca gaṇeśavimarśinyām-

śambhau madhyagate harīnaharabhūdevyo harau śaṃkare-
bhāsye nāgasutā ravau haragaṇeśājāmbikāḥ sthāpitāḥ |
devyāṃ viṣṇuharaikadantaravayo lambodare'jeśvare |
nāryāḥ śaṃkarabhāgatopi śubhadā vyastāśca te hānidā iti ||
atha ṣoḍaśopacāradaśopacārapañcopacārāḥ yathā | tatrādau 
ṣoḍaśopacāraniyamaḥ-

āsanaṃ svāgataṃ pādyamarghyamācamanīyakam |
madhuparkācamanasnānaṃ vasanābharaṇāni ca ||
sugandhisumanodhūpadīpanaivedyacandanam |
prayojayedarccanāyāmupacārāstu ṣoḍaśa ||

kālikāpurāṇe ṣodaśopacāraniyamaḥ-

āsanaṃ pādyamarghyaṃ ca tata ācamanīyakam |

p. 70) madhuparkaṃ snānajalaṃ vastraṃ candanabhūṣaṇam ||
puṣpaṃ dhūpaṃ ca dīpañca netrāñjanamataḥ param |
naivedyācamanīye ca pradakṣiṇanamaskriye ||
ete ṣoḍaśa nirdiṣṭā upacārāstu pūrvataḥ |

			atha daśopacāraniyamaḥ |

pādyamardhyamācanīyaṃ gandhapuṣpaṃ ca dhūpakam |
dīpaṃ naivedyamityāhuḥ punarācamanīyakam || 
tāmbūlaṃ ca tato dadyātpūjāyāṃ sarvataḥ sadā |

			atha pañcopacārāḥ |

arghyo gandhaṃ ca puṣpaṃ ca naivedyācamanaṃ tathā |
dravyābhāve tṛṣavāraṃ puṣpaiḥ sarvaṃ prakalpayet |
atra ṣoḍaśopacārābhāve daśopacārāḥ tadabhāve pañcopacārāḥ |
tadabhāve puṣpaiḥ sarvaṃ prakalpayet |

puṣpadānavidhiḥ |

puṣpaṃ vā yadi vā patraṃ phalaṃ naṣṭamadhomukham ||
duḥkhadantatsamākhyātaṃ yathotpannaṃ tathārpayediti ||
puṣpāñjalau na doṣaḥ syādityuktaṃ śambhunārcane |

arghyādipātraniyamamāha gautamaḥ-

arghyasya trīṇi pātrāṇi pādyasyāpi trayaṃ bhavet |
tathaivācamanīyasya pātrāṇi ca vibhāgaśaḥ ||
tathā karaṇadaurbalyādekameva praśasyate |
kintu sāmānyaviśeṣārghyadvayasyāvaśyakatvam |

tathā ca tantrāntare-

ekapātraṃ na kartavyaṃ yadi sākṣānmaheśvaraḥ |
mantrāḥ parāṃmukhā yānti āpadaśca pade pade ||
iha loke daridraḥ syānmṛtaśca narakaṃ vrajet |
athārghyajale'ṣṭagandhadānasyāvaśyakatvam ||

p. 71) uktaṃ ca śāradāyām-

gandhāṣṭakamidaṃ dadyādarghyapātraṃ prayatnataḥ |
gandhāṣṭakantat trividhaṃ śaktiviṣṇuśivātmakam ||
candanāgurukarpūracorakuṅkumarocanāḥ |
jaṭāmāṃsīkapiyutā śaktergandhāṣṭakaṃ viduḥ ||
candanāguruhrīverakuṣṭhakuṅkumasevyakāḥ |
jaṭāmāsīsuramiti viṣṇorgaṃdhāṣṭakaṃ smṛtam ||
candanāgurukarpūratamālajalakuṅkumam |
uśīraṃ kuṣṭhasaṃyuktaṃ śaivaṃ gandhāṣṭakaṃ viduriti ||
arghyaṃ diśettato mūrdhni śiromantreṇa deśikaḥ |
vāruṇena ca mantreṇa dadyādācamanīyakam ||
svadhetyācamanīyañca trivāraṃ mukhapañkaje |
svadheti madhuparkaṃ ca punarācamanīyakam ||

atha ṣaḍaṅgaṃ dhūpamāha |

tantrāntare-

sitājyamadhusaṃmiśraṃ guggulvagurucandanam |
ṣaḍaṅgo dhūpa eṣo'sti sarvadevapriyaḥ sadā ||

atha daśāṅgo dhūpaḥ |

madhumustāghṛtaṃ gandho guggulvaguruśailajam |
saralaṃ siṃhasiddhārthau daśāṅgo dhūpa iṣyate ||

atha yāmale gandhapuṣpabhūṣaṇadīpadānaprakāraḥ-

nivedayetpurobhāge gandhaṃ puṣpaṃ ca bhūṣaṇam |
dīpaṃ dakṣiṇato dadyātpurato vā na pṛṣṭhata iti ||
vāmatastu tathā dhūpamagre vā na ca dakṣiṇe |
naivedyaṃ dakṣiṇe bhāge purato vā na pṛṣṭhataḥ ||
atra vāmadakṣiṇabhāgo devatāyā na tu sādhakasyeti |
dhūpadīpau subhojyaṃ ca devatāgre nivedayediti darśanāt ||

p. 72) atha ghṛtayuktaṃ dakṣiṇe tailayuktaṃ vāme | evaṃ sitāvartiśca 
dakṣiṇe raktā cedvāme praśastā bhavati |

tathā yāmale-

dīpaṃ dakṣe ghṛtayuktaṃ tailayuktaṃ ca vāmataḥ ||
dakṣiṇe ca sitāvartirvāmato raktavartikā ||
pakvāpakvavibhāgena naivedheṣviti saṃsthitiḥ |
purato niyamo nāsti dīpanaivedyayoḥ kvacit ||

	pakvaṃ dugdhaṃ ca devatāvāme sthāpayet | apakvamāmānnaṃ 
dakṣiṇe sthāpayet || tato daṇḍavatpraṇamediti || atha ṣoḍaśopacārāṇāṃ 
paurāṇiko mantro yathā | tatrādāvāsanamantraḥ |

bhavāni tvaṃ mahādevi sarvasaubhāgyadāyike |
anekaratnasaṃyuktamāsanaṃ pratigṛhyatām ||

atha pādyasya-

sucāru śītalaṃ divyaṃ nānāgandhasamanvitam ||
gṛhāṇa devadeveśi mahādevi namostu te ||

athārghyasya-

śivapriye mahābhāge śaṃkarapriyavādini |
arghyaṃ gṛhāṇa kalyāṇi kṛpayā parameśvari ||

tata ācamanamantraḥ |

pavitraṃ jalamānītaṃ suvarṇakalaśe sthitam |
ācamyatāṃ mahābhāge hareṇa sahite'naghe ||

atha snānamantraḥ |

gaṅgāsarasvatīrevākāverīnarmadājalaiḥ |
snāpitāsi mayā devi tathā śāntiṃ prayaccha me ||

atha vastrasya-

sarvasatvādhike ramye lokalajjānivāraṇe |
mayopapādite tubhyaṃ vāsasī pratigṛhyatām ||

p. 73) atha candanasya-

karpūrāgurusaṃghṛṣṭaṃ kuṅkumādisamanvitam |
kastūrikāsamāyuktaṃ candanaṃ pratigṛhyatām ||

athākṣatamantraḥ |

rañjitāḥ kusumaughena akṣatāścātiśobhanāḥ |
mamaiṣāṃ devi dānena prasannā bhava pārvati ||

atha bhūṣaṇamantraḥ |

bhūṣaṇāni vicitrāṇi maṇimuktāmayāni vai |
gṛhāṇa parayā prītyā sarvakāmapradāyini ||

atha puṣpasya-

sanmallikāvakulacampakapāṭalābjapunnāgajātikaravīrarasāla-
puṣpaiḥ | bilvapravālavividhāmalamālatībhistvāṃ pūjayāmi jagadīśvari 
me prasīda ||

atha dhūpamantraḥ |

devadrumarasodbhūtaḥ kālāgurusamanvitaḥ |
ānīto'yaṃ mayā dhūpo bhavāni pratigṛhyatām ||

atha dīpasya |

tva jyotiḥ sarvadevānāṃ tejasāṃ teja uttamam | 
ātmajyotiḥ paraṃ dhāma dīpo'yaṃ pratigṛhyatām

aṃjanadānamantraḥ |

netradvayaṃ mahādevi bhūṣayāmalakajjalaiḥ |
surāsurakṛpārāśe māṃ pāhi parameśvari ||

atha naivedyasya |

annaṃ ca vividhaṃ svādu rasaiḥ ṣaḍbhiḥ samanvitam |
bhakṣyabhojyasamāyuktaṃ naivedyaṃ pratigṛhyatām ||
tata ācamanīyaṃ pūrvavat |

p. 74)				atha tāmbūlamantraḥ |

karpūrailālavaṅgāditāmbūladalasaṃyutam |
kramukasya phalañcaiva tāmbūlaṃ pratigṛhyatām ||

tata ārārtikaṃ dadyāt |

candrādityau ca dharaṇī vidyudagnistathaiva ca |
tvameva sarvatejāṃsi ārtikyaṃ pratigṛhyatām ||

iti paurāṇikaṣoḍaśopacārāḥ |

atha tāntrikaśoḍaśopacāraniyamaḥ | tatrādau svāgatavākyam-

kṛtārtho'nugṛhīto'smi saphalaṃ jīvitaṃ mama |
āgatā devadeveśi svasvāgatamidaṃ punaḥ ||

atha pādyam |

śyāmākaviṣṇukrāntābjadūrvāḥ pādyajale kṣipet |
mūlaślokairnamomantraiḥ pādyaṃ pādāmbuje'rpayet ||
yadbhaktileśasamparkātparamānandasambhavaḥ |
tasmai te caraṇābjāya pādyaṃ saṅkalpayāmyaham ||
athā camanam | tatra lavaṅgajātikaṅkolaṃ prakṣipya mukhe dadyāt |

mantraḥ |

vedānāmapi vedāya devānāṃ devatātmane |
ācāmaṃ kalpayāmīśe śuddhānāṃ śuddhihetave ||

athārghapātre dūrvātiladarbhasarṣapākṣatapuṣpāṇi datvārghyaṃ 
prakalpayet mūrddhni | mantro yathā-

tāpatrayaharaṃ divyaṃ paramānandalakṣaṇam |
tāpatrayavinirmuktaṃ tavārghyaṃ kalpayāmyaham ||

atha madhuparkamantraḥ |

sarvakāluṣyahīnāyai paripūrṇasukhātmane |
madhuparkamidaṃ devi kalpayāmi prasīda me ||

p. 75) 				punarācamanamantraḥ |

ucchiṣṭopyaśucirvāpi yasya smaraṇamātrataḥ |
śucimāpnoti tasyai te punarācamanīyakam ||
snānavastropavītānte naivedyāntepi tatsmṛtam |
pādyādidravyābhāve tu tatsmarannakṣatān kṣipet ||

atha gandhatailadānamantraḥ |

snehaṃ gṛhāṇa snehena bhaktānāmarthasiddhide |
surāsurārcite devi dadāmi snehamuttamam ||

atha snānam |

paramānandabodhābdhe dadyārdre surapūjite |
sāṃgopāṃgamidaṃ snānaṃ kalpayāmi harapriye ||
tataḥ sahasramukhyena śataṃ vā śaktitopi vā |
gandhayuktajalairdevīmabhiṣiñcenmanuṃ japan || tataḥ ||
paṭhan mūlaṃ tataḥ ślokau dadyādvastropavītake |
nānārāgavicitraṃ tu muktāmaṇisamākulam ||
nirāvaraṇavijñānaṃ vāsaste kalpayāmyaham |
yamāśritya mahāmāyā jagatsammohinī sadā ||
tasyai te paramaiśvaryai kalpayāmyuttarīyakam |

atha bhūṣaṇamantraḥ |

nānāśaktyāśraye devi sundarāṅgi sureśvari |
bhūṣaṇāni vicitrāṇi kalpayāmyamarārcite ||
atha gandhamantraḥ | pātrasthaṃ gandhaṃ kaniṣṭhayārpayet |

anena-

paramānandasaurabhyaparipūrṇadigantare |
gṛhāṇa paramaṃ gandhaṃ kṛpayā parameśvari ||

	ayaṃ gandho'mukadevatāyai namaḥ || tataḥ 
kaniṣṭhāṅguṣṭhābhyāṃ gandhamudrāṃ pradarśayet || tataḥ 
puṣpamantraḥ | mūlamuccārya puṣpāṇyādāya ||

p. 76) turīyavanasambhūtaṃ nānāguṇamanoharam | 
amandasaurabhaṃ puṣpaṃ gṛhyatāmidamadbhutam ||

etāni puṣpāṇi amukadevatāyai vauṣaṭ | 
tatastarjanyaṅguṣṭhābhyāṃ puṣpamudrāṃ darśayet | atha viśeṣaḥ-

akṣatānarkadhattūrau viṣṇau naivārpayetsudhīḥ |
bandhūkaṃ ketakīṃ kundaṃ kesaraṃ kuṭajaṃ japām ||
śaṅkare nārpayedvidvān mālatīṃ yūthikāmapi |
śaktau dūrvārkamandārānmālūrantagaraṃ ravau ||
vināyake tu tulasīṃ nārpayejjātucidbudhaḥ |

	evaṃ puṣpapūjāṃ vidhāyāvaraṇadevatārcanaṃ kuryāt | tata 
āvaraṇadevatārcanaṃ samarpayedanena |

abhīṣṭasiddhiṃ me dehi śaraṇāgatavatsale |
bhaktyā samarpaye tubhyamidamāvaraṇārcanamiti ||

	tataḥ karaṃ prakṣālya upacārāntaraṃ kalpayet | tato devatāvāma 
bhāge dhūpapātraṃ sthāpayet || tato dhūpapātraṃ phaḍiti prakṣālya 
nama iti puṣpaṃ dattvā vāmatarjanyā saṃśpṛśya mūlaṃ ślokaṃ ca |

vanaspatiraso divyo gandhāḍhyo gandha uttamaḥ |
āghreyaḥ sarvadevānāṃ dhūpo'yaṃ pratigṛhyatām ||

	iti paṭhitvā sāṅgāyaisaparivārāyai sveṣṭadevatāyai dhūpaṃ 
samarpayāmi nama iti śaṅkhajalaṃ bhūmau prakṣipya 
tarjanyaṃguṣṭhayogena dhūpamudrāṃ pradarśya | 

jayadhvanimantraḥ |

	mātaḥ svāheti ghaṇṭāṃ sampūjya vāmahastena tāṃ vādayan 
devatāguṇānkīrtayan dakṣiṇahastena devatāṃ nābhideśato dhūpayet |

atha dīpavidhiḥ |

devasya dakṣiṇe bhāge ghṛtadīpaḥ || devatāvāmabhāe tailadīpaḥ 
sthāpyaḥ || tato dīpapātraṃ phaḍiti samprokṣya nama iti puṣpaṃ datvā 
vāmamadhyamayā spṛśan mūlamuccārya-

p. 77) svaprakāśo mahāndīpaḥ sarvatra timirāpahaḥ |
sabāhyābhyantaraṃ jyotirdipo'yaṃ pratigṛhyatām ||

	sāṅgāyai saparivārāyai amukadevatāyai dīpaṃ darśayāmi namaḥ 
śaṅkhasthajalaṃ bhūmau prakṣipya madhyamāṅguṣṭhayogana 
dīpamudrāṃ pradarśya pūrvavadghaṇṭāṃ vādayan | netrādipādāntaṃ 
dīpaṃ pradarśayet ||

atha naivedyadānam-

	naivedyaṃ pātre nidhāya mūlena samprokṣya 
cakramudrayābhirakṣya dvādaśavāraṃ vāyubījābhimantritairjalaiḥ 
prokṣya tadutthavāyunā taddoṣaṃ saṃśoṣya dakṣiṇakaratale vahnibījaṃ 
vicintya tatpṛṣṭhalagnaṃ vāmakaratalaṃ kṛtvā tadutthāgninā 
naivedyadoṣaṃ saṃdahya vāmakare amṛtaṃ dhyātvā vāmapṛṣṭhe 
dakṣiṇakaraṃ kṛtvā pradarśya tadakhilamamṛtātmakaṃ dhyātvā 
tatspṛṣṭvā mūlamantramaṣṭadhā japtvā dhenumudrāṃ pradarśya 
jalagandhapuṣpairabhyarcya devatāyai puṣpāṃjaliṃ samarpya 
tanmukhāttejovinirgataṃ dhyātvā vāmāṅguṣṭhena naivedyapātraṃ 
spṛṣṭvā dakṣiṇena gṛhītvā mūlamuccārya-

satpātrasiddhaṃ suhavirvidhānekabhakṣaṇam |
nivedayāmi deveśi sanugā tvaṃ gṛhāṇa tamiti |

	ślokaṃ saṃjapya sāṅgāyai saparivārāyai śrīmadamukadevatāyai 
naivedyaṃ samarpayāmi nama iti jalamṛtyusṛjya aṅguṣṭhānāmikābhyāṃ 
naivedyamudrāṃ darśayet |
	tataḥ amṛtopastaraṇamasi svāheti devyā haste jalaṃ dadyāt | tato 
vāmakareṇa vikacotpalasadṛśīṃ grāsamudrāṃ pradarśya dakṣiṇakareṇa 
samantrakaṃ prāṇādimudrāṃ pradarśayet | yathā oṃ prāṇāya svāhā | 
aṅguṣṭhaniṣṭhānāmikābhiḥ | oṃ apānāya svāhā | 
aṅguṣṭhatarjanīmadhyamābhiḥ | oṃ vyānāya svāhā | 
anāmāmadhyamāṅguṣṭhaiḥ | oṃ vyānāya svāhā | 
anāmāmadhyamāṅguṣṭhaiḥ | oṃ udānāya svāhā | 
aṅguṣṭhatarjanīmadhyamānāmābhiḥ | oṃ samānāya svāhā | 
sarvābhiriti | atha brahmeśādyaiḥ stutāṃ devakanyābhiḥ 
sudhāṃśucāmaraiḥ parivījyamānāṃ

p. 78) bhoktṛgaṇān hāsayantīṃ devīṃ dhyāyet | atrāvāsare 
pūjāṅgahomaṃ kuryāt | atha homasya nityatā somabhujagāvalyām-

nājaptaḥ sidhyate mantro nāhutaśca phalapradaḥ |
nāniṣṭo yacchate kāmān tasmāt tritayamarcayet ||
pūjāyāṃ labhyate pūjā japātsiddhirna saṃśayaḥ |
vibhūtiścāgnikāryeṇa sarvasiddhirna saṃśayaḥ ||

taduktam-

sthaṇḍile'gnimupādhāya vaiśvadevakriyāṃ caret |
mūlena vīkṣya cāstreṇa kṛtvā prokṣaṇatāḍanam ||
kuśaistadvarmaṇābhyukṣya pūrvavatsthāpayecchucim |
tanmantreṇa tamabhyarcyāhūya tatreṣṭadevatām ||
pūjayedgandhapuṣpaistāṃ mahāvyāhṛtibhistataḥ |
hutvā vyastasamastābhirāhutīnāṃ catuṣṭayam ||
annairmūlena juhuyātpañcaviṃśatisaṃkhyayā |
punarvyāhṛtibhirhutvā mūrtau devīṃ niyojayet ||
vahniṃ visṛjya devāya dadyādācamanīyakamiti |

	atha tatrātmadakṣiṇahastapramāṇaṃ caturaṅgulamunnataṃ 
sthaṇḍilaṃ kṛtvā mūlena vīkṣya phaḍiti prokṣya taireva kuśaiḥ santāḍya 
humiti mantreṇābhyukṣya mūlenāgnimādāya tadaṃśaṃ gṛhītvā nair-
ṛtyakoṇe oṃ kravyādebhyo nama iti kṣipet | tato mūlenāgniṃ sthāpayitvā 
vamityamṛtīkṛtya humityavaguṇṭhya phaḍiti saṃrakṣya | oṃ 
vaiśvānarajātaveda ihāvaha lohitākṣa sarvakarmāṇi sādhaya svāheti 
vahniṃ sampūjya tatreṣṭadevatāmāvāhya sampūjya ājyena | oṃ bhūḥ 
svāhā | oṃ bhuvaḥ svāhā | oṃ svaḥ svāhā | oṃ bhūrbhuvaḥ svaḥ 
svāhā | iti hutvā mūlamantreṇa pañcaviṃśatisaṃkhyākaṃ hutvā 
punarvyāhṛtibhirhutvā mūrtau devatāṃ saṃyojyāgniṃ visarjayediti |

p. 79) bho bho vahne mahāśakte sarvakarmaprasādhaka |
karmāntarepi samprāpte sānnidhyaṃ kuru sādaramiti ||

	visarjana kṛtvā devatāyai ācamanaṃ dattvā tanmukhāgatantejaḥ 
punastanmukhe nirgatamiti vicintya puṣpāñjaliṃ datvā praṇamya japaṃ 
kuryāt | tadyathā | ṛṣyādinyāsaṃ vidhāya mūrdhni kulakāṃ vicintya 
daśadhā prajapya sampūjya japamālāmānīya 
kvacitpātre'rghyodakenābhyukṣya |

oṃ māle māle mahāmāle sarvaśaktisvarūpiṇi |
caturvargastvayi nyastastasmānme siddhidā bhaveti ||

	gandhādinā sampūjya | avighnaṃ kuru māle tvamiti mantreṇa 
dakṣiṇahastena gṛhītvā śirasi guruṃ hṛdaye sveṣṭadevatāṃ dhyāyan 
hṛdayasamīpaṃ mālāmānīya dakṣahastamadhyamāmadhyaparvaṇi 
saṃsthāpya praṇavamuccārya mālāmantraṃ japet | punaromityuccārya 
japaṃ samāpya |

tvaṃ māle sarvadevānāṃ prītidā śubhadā matā |
śubhaṃ kuruṣva me bhadre yaśovīryañca sarvadā ||

	hrīṃ siddhyai nama iti svaśirasi mālāṃ saṃsthāpya punaḥ 
prāṇāyāmatrayaṃ kṛtvā ṛṣyādiṣaḍaṅganyāsaṃ vidhāya devyā 
vāmahaste arghyodakena |

guhyātiguhyagoptrī tvaṃ gṛhāṇāsmakṛtaṃ japam | 
siddhirbhavatu me devi tvatprasādānmaheśvarīti ||

	japaṃ samarpya devyagre puṣpāñjaliṃ prakṣipet | tato mālāṃ 
sthāpayet | tataḥ kavacādikaṃ paṭhitvā puṣpāñjaliṃ dattvā | itaḥ 
pūrvaṃ yatkṛtaṃ yaduktaṃ tatsarvamamukadevatā brahmārpaṇamastu | 
tataḥ śāntipāṭhaṃ vidhāya vāmabhāge nirmālyavāsinā nirmālyena 
sampūjya yantralepaṃ śirasi dattvā nirmālyaṃ viharediti ||

iti śrījīvanāthakṛte dīkṣāprakāśe ṣoḍaśopacāraḥ samāptaḥ ||

p. 80) 				atha kulukā |

śrīdevyuvāca |

kukulā kīdṛśī deva seturvā nātha kīdṛśaḥ |
kīdṛśo vā mahāseturnnirvāṇamapi kīdṛśam ||
anyadvā kathitaṃ deva kīdṛśaṃ tadvada prabho |

īśvara uvāca |

guhyādguhyataraṃ devi tava snehena kathyate |
vinā yena maheśāni niṣphalantu japādikam ||
tārāyāḥ kulukā devi mahānīlasarasvatī | 
pañcākṣarī kālikāyāḥ kulukā parikathyate ||
kālīkūrcaṃ vadhūrmāyā phaḍantaṃ parameśvari |
chinnāyāḥ parameśāni kulukā tryakṣarī bhavet ||
vajravairocanīye ca ante varma prakīrtayet |
sampatpradāyāḥ prathamaṃ bhairavyāḥ kulukā matā ||
śrīmattripurasundaryāḥ kulukā dvādaśākṣarī |
vāgbhavaṃ prathamaṃ bījaṃ kāmarājamataḥ param ||
lajjābījaṃ tataḥ paścāttripureśī tataḥ param |
bhagavatīti padaṃ dadyādante ṭhadvayamuccaret ||
anyāsāntu mahādevi tryakṣarī kulukā matā |
tāraṃ kūrcaṃ samuccārya nārasiṃhaṃ samuccaret ||
ityeṣā kathitā devi saṃkṣepātkulukā mayā |
ajñātvā kulukāṃ devi prajapedyo narādhamaḥ ||
pañcatvamāśu labhate siddhihāniśca jāyate |
mantrāḥ parāṅmukhā yānti mantrahāniḥ prajāyate ||
tathā japādikaṃ sarvaṃ niṣphalaṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ |
tasmātsarvaprayatnena yo japenmūrdhni kullakām ||
tasyāśu mantrasiddhiḥ syānnānyathā parameśvari |

p. 81) tārāyāḥ | hrīṃ strīṃ hūṃ | kālikāyāḥ krīṃ haṃ strīṃ hrīṃ 
phaṭ | chinnamastāyāḥ | oṃ hūṃ kṣrauṃ | tripurabhairavyāḥ | 
vajravairocanīye hūṃ | śrītripurasundaryāḥ aiṃ klīṃ hrīṃ tripureśi 
bhagavati svāhā || anyāsāṃ vidyānām | oṃ hraṃ kṣrauṃ iti kulukā 
jñeyā | sarvāsāṃ vidyānāṃ setuḥ praṇava eva hṛdaye cintyaḥ | 
mahāsetustu mūlamantraḥ | sa ca kaṇṭhadeśe japtavyaḥ | nirvāṇastu | oṃ 
aṃ mūlaṃ aiṃ aṃ āṃ iṃ īṃ uṃ ūṃ ṛṃ ṝṃ ḷṃ ḹṃ eṃ aiṃ oṃ 
auṃ aṃ aḥ kaṃ khaṃ gaṃ ghaṃ ṅaṃ caṃ chaṃ jaṃ jhaṃ ñaṃ ṭaṃ 
ṭhaṃ ḍaṃ ḍhaṃ ṇaṃ taṃ thaṃ daṃ dhaṃ naṃ paṃ phaṃ baṃ bhaṃ 
maṃ yaṃ raṃ laṃ vaṃ śaṃ ṣaṃ saṃ haṃ jñaṃ kṣaṃ oṃ iti 
nirvāṇaṃ nābhideśe cintyamiti |

uktaṃ ca mahātantre-

oṃ setuḥ sarvavidyānāṃ mahāsetuśca mūlakam |
praṇavaṃ pūrvamuddhṛtya svarādyaṃ tadanantaram ||
vāgbhavañca tato devi mātṛkāṃ tadanantaram |
oṃkāramantre nirvāṇaṃ sureśvari vicintayet ||
hṛdaye setumājapya kaṇṭhadeśe sureśvari |
mahāsetuṃ tato nābhau nirvāṇaṃ paricintayet ||
tataḥ ṣaḍaṅganyāsañca kṛtvā dhyātvā manuṃ japet |
evaṃ kṛte sureśāni mantrasiddhiḥ prajāyate ||

iti śrīdīkṣāprakāśe rahasyaprakāraḥ |

atha śivābaliprakāraḥ |

tathā ca kulacūḍāmaṇau-

avaśyamannadānena niyataṃ toṣayecchivām |
nityaśrāddhaṃ yathā sandhyā candanaṃ pitṛtarpaṇam ||
tathaiva kuladevīnāṃ nityatā kulapūjane |
paśu pāṃ śivāṃ devīṃ yo nārcayati nirjane ||

p. 82) śivārāveṇa tasyāśu sarve naśyanti niścitam |
japapūjāvidhānena yatkiñcitsukṛtāni vai ||
gṛhītvā śāpakaṃ datvā śivā roditi nirjane |
ekayā bhujyate yatra śivayā yadi bhairavi ||
tadaiva sarvaśaktīnāṃ prītiḥ paramadurlabhā |
rājyādibhayamāpanne deśāntarabhayādike ||
śubhāśubhāni karmāṇi vicintya balimāharet |
bilvamūle prāntare vā śmaśāne nagarādbahiḥ ||
māsapradhānaṃ naivedyaṃ sandhyākāle nivedayet |
kāli kālīti vatavye tatromā śivarūpiṇī ||
paśurūpadharā yāti parivāragaṇaiḥ saha |

tathottarābhimukho bhūtvā saṅkalpaṃ kuryāt-

	oṃ asyāṃ sandhyāyāmamukagotraḥ 
śrīmadamukakāryaśubhāśubhārthaṃ śivābalimahaṅkariṣye iti 
saṅkalpya prāṇāyāmaṃ kṛtvā ṣaḍaṅganyāsañca kṛtvārghyaṃ 
sthāpayitvā bhakṣyānnaṃ gṛhītvā muktakeśaḥ śanaiḥ śivā āvāhayet 
|| kālikālīti mantreṇa | tataḥ-

gṛhṇa devi mahābhāge śive kālāgnirūpiṇi |
śubhāśubhaphalaṃ vyaktaṃ brūhi gṛhṇa balintava ||

	ityarghyodakena utsṛjya tataḥ kiñcadapasṛtya śivāsu bhakṣayantīṣu | 
oṃ saṃhārabhairavāya namaḥ 1 | oṃ baṭukebhyo namaḥ 2 | oṃ 
vināyakebhyo namaḥ 3 | oṃ mātṛbhyo namaḥ 4 | oṃ kṣauṃ 
kṣetrapālebhyo namaḥ 5 | oṃ yoginībhyo namaḥ 6 | oṃ ḍākinībhyo namaḥ 
7 | oṃ śivadūtībhyo namaḥ 8 | iti balyaṣṭakaṃ datvā 
gandhapuṣpāñjalimādāya muktakeśī digambara utthāya nimīlitākṣaḥ 
stutiṃ paṭhet |

śivārūpadhare devi guhyakāli namostu te |
ulkāmukhi lalajjihve ghorarāve śṛgālini ||

p. 83) śmaśānavāsini prete śavamāṃsapriye'naghe ||
araṇyacāriṇyanaghe śive jambūkarūpiṇi |
namo'stu te mahāmāye jagattāriṇi kālike ||
mātaṅgi kukkuṭe raudri mahākāli namostu te |
sarvasiddhiprade bhīme bhayaṅkari bhayāpahe ||
prasannā bhava deveśi mama bhaktasya caṇḍike |
saṃsāratāraṇapare jaya sarvaśubhaṅkari ||
vidhvastacikure caṇḍi cāmuṇḍe muṇḍamālini |
saṃhārakāriṇi kruddhe sarvasiddhiṃ prayaccha me ||
durge kirāti śabari pretāsanagate'bhaye |
anugrahaṃ kuru sadā kṛpayā māṃ vilokaya ||
rājyaṃ prayaccha vikaṭe vittamāyuḥ sutāṃ striyam |
śivābalipradānena tvaṃ prasannā bhaveśvari ||
namastestu namastestu namastestu namostu te |

iti paṭhitvā bhuktaśeṣānnaṃ tadbhājanaṃ ca bhūmau nikhanet | 
atha bhojanakāle śubhāśubhaphalaṃ śivāśabdānusāreṇa jñātavyam ||

taduktaṃ tatraiva-

bhuktā rauti yadaiśānyāṃ mukhamuttolya susvaram |
tadaiva maṅgalaṃ vidyānnānyathā kulapūjanam ||
yadā na bhujyate vastu tadā naiva śubhaṃ bhavet |
śubhaṃ yadi bhavettatra bhujyate tadaśeṣataḥ ||

	atra sātviko brāhmaṇaḥ māṃsasthāne māṣavaṭakaṃ dadyāt | 
sāttviko jīvahatyāñca na kadācitprakārayedityukteḥ ||

tato gahamāgatya devīṃ pūjayet |

athānukalpadravyāṇi- yathā śaktisaṅgamatantre-

māhiṣatvena kūṣmāṇḍaṃ chāgatvena ca karkaṭīm |
vṛntākaṃ kukkuṭatvena meṣatvena ca tumbikām |

p. 84) mānuṣatvena panasaṃ matsyatvekṣudaṇḍakam |
śūraṇañca kharatvena dātavyaṃ kośakaṃ mṛge ||
pāyasaṃ tu gajatvena mārjāratve kulutthakam |
paṭolaṃ śūkaratvena śarkarāṃ bālukantathā ||
khaḍgatvena maheśāni matsyatve kadalīphalam |
mūṣakatve mūlakaṃ ca sārasatve kaliṃgakam ||
kīratve nārikelañca matsyatve kadalīṃ ca vā |
māṣāḥ sarvabalitvena kṛśarānnantathā matamiti ||

iti śrījīvanāthakṛte dīkṣāprakāśe śivābaliprakāraḥ samāptaḥ |

atha viṣṇorvaidikaṣoḍaśopacāraprakāraḥ |

tatrādāvāsanamantraḥ-

	oṃ sahasraśīrṣāpuruṣaḥ sahasrākṣaḥ sahasrapāt sabhūmi/ sarvata 
spṛtvātyatiṣṭhaddaśāṅgulam | upaveśanamantraḥ | puruṣa eveda/ sarvaṃ 
yadbhūtaṃ yacca bhāvyam | utāmṛtatvasyeśāno yadannenātirohati |

atha pādyamantraḥ-

	oṃ etāvānasya mahimāto jyāyā/śca puruṣaḥ pādosya 
viśvābhūtāni tripādasyāmṛtaṃ divi ||

arghyamantraḥ |

	tripādūrdhva udaitpuruṣaḥ pādosyehābhavatpunaḥ | tato 
viṣvaṅvyakrāmatsāśanānaśane abhi ||

ācamanamantraḥ |

	tato virāḍajāyata virājo adhipuruṣaḥ | sa jāto atyaricyata | 
paścādbhūmimathopuraḥ |

snānamantraḥ |

	tasmādyajñātsarvahutaḥ sambhṛtaṃ pṛṣadājyam || 
paśū/stā/ścakre vāyavyā nārāṇyā grāmyāśca ye |

p. 85) 			athācchādanamantraḥ |

	tasmādyajñātsarvahuta ṛcaḥ sāmāni jajñire | chandā/si jajñire 
tasmādyajustasmādajāyata |

yajñopavītamantraḥ |

	tasmādaśvā ajāyanta ye ke cobhayādataḥ | gāvo ha jajñire tasmāt 
tasmājjātā ajāvayaḥ |

gandhamantraḥ |

	ta yyajñambarhiṣi praukṣanpuruṣaṃ jātamagrataḥ | tena devā 
ayajanta sādhyā ṛṣayaśca ye |

atha puṣpamantraḥ |

	yatpuruṣaṃ byadadhuḥ katidhā vyakalpayan | mukhaṃ 
kimasyāsītkimbāhū kimūrū pādā ucyete |

dhūpamantraḥ |

	brāhmaṇosya mukhamāsīdbāhū rājanyaḥkṛtaḥ ūrū tadasya 
yadvaiśyaḥ padbhyāṃ śūdro ajāyata |

atha dīpamantraḥ |

	candramā manaso jātaścakṣoḥ sūryo ajāyata | śrotrādvāyuśca 
prāṇaśca mukhadagnirajāyata |

naivedyamantraḥ |

	nābhyā āsīdantarīkṣaṃ śīrṣṇo dyauḥ samavartata | padbhyāṃ 
bhūmirdiśaḥ śrītrāttathā lokānakalpayan |

añjalikaraṇamantraḥ |

	yatpuruṣeṇa haviṣā devā yajñamatanvata | vasantosyāsīdājyaṃ 
grīṣma idhmaḥ śaraddhaviḥ |

atha pradakṣiṇāmantraḥ |

	saptāsyāsana paridhayaḥ triḥsapta samidhaḥ kṛtāḥ | devā 
yadyajñantavānā abadhnan puruṣaṃ paśum |

p. 86) 				atha visarjanamantraḥ |

	yajñena yajñamayajanta devāstāni dharmāṇi prathamānyāsan 
tehanākaṃ mahimānaḥ sacanta yatra pūrve sādhyāḥ santi devāḥ ||

iti dīkṣāprakāśe viṣṇoḥ ṣoḍaśopacāraprakāraḥ |

atha saptaśatīpāṭhavidhimāha |

vāsavītantre-

ādhāraṃ sthāpayitvā tu pustakaṃ vācayettataḥ |
hastasaṃsthāpanāddevi bhavedarddhaphalaṃ yataḥ ||
yāvanna pūryate'dhyāyaḥ tāvanna virametpaṭhan |
yadi prāmādādadhyāye virāmo bhavati priye ||
punaradhyāyamārabhya paṭhetsarvaṃ muhurmuhuḥ |
anukramātpaṭheddevi śiraḥ kampādikantyajet ||

athānyatrāpi |

praṇavaṃ pūrvamuccārya stotraṃ vā saṃhitāṃ paṭhet |
ante ca praṇavaṃ dadyādityuvācādipūruṣaḥ ||
na mānasaṃ paṭhetstotraṃ vācikantu praśasyate |
na svayaṃ likhitaṃ stotraṃ nābrāhmaṇalipiṃ paṭhet |
na ca svayaṃ kṛtaṃ stotraṃ tathānyena ca yatkṛtam |
yataḥ kalau praśaṃsanti munibhirbhāvitaṃ ca yat ||

anyacca |

svayantu likhitaṃ yacca kṛtinā likhitantu yat |
abrāhmaṇena likhitaṃ tatsarvaṃ niṣphalaṃ bhavet ||
ṛṣiścchando devatādi nyasya stotraṃ paṭhedbudha iti |

kīlakagranthe tu |

aṣṭamyāṃ vā caturdaśyāṃ kṛṣṇāyāntu samāhitaḥ |
dadāti pratigṛhṇāti nānyathaiṣā prasīdatīti ||

p. 87) itthaṃ rūpeṇa kīlena mahādevena kīlitamiti |
argalaṃ kīlakaṃ cādau japitvā kavacaṃ paṭhet ||
japetsaptaśatīṃ paścātkrama eṣa śivodita iti |

oṃ asya prathamacaritrasya brahmā ṛṣirgāyatrīchando mahākālī 
devatā nandajā śaktiḥraktadantikā bījamagnistattvaṃ 
mahākālīprītyarthaṃ jape viniyogaḥ | ityuktvā prathamādhyāyaṃ paṭhet | 
tataḥ | oṃ madhyamacaritrasya viṣṇur-ṛṣiruṣṇikchando lakṣmīrdevatā 
śākambharī śakti durgābījaṃ vāyustattvaṃ mahālakṣmīprītyarthaṃ 
jape viniyogaḥ | iti paṭhitvā dvitīyādhyāyaṃ tṛtīyādhyāyaṃ 
caturthādhyāyaṃ paṭhet | tata uttaracaritrasya rudra-ṛṣiḥ triṣṭupchandaḥ 
mahāsarasvatī devatā klīṃ bījaṃ ravistattvaṃ mahāsarasvatīprītyarthe 
jape viniyogaḥ |

uktañca-

saptasatyāścaritre tu prathame padmabhūrmuniḥ |
chando gāyatramuditaṃ mahākālī tu devatā ||
vāgbījaṃ pāvakastattvaṃ dharmārthe viniyojanam |
vāgaibījaṃ pāvakaṃ ramiti |
madhyame tu caritre tu munivaṣṇurudāhṛtaḥ |
uṣṇikchando mahālakṣmīrdevatā bījabhadrikā ||
vāyustatvaṃ dhanaprāptyai viniyoga udāhṛtaḥ |
adrijā hrīṃ vāyubījaṃ yamiti ||
uttarasya caritrasya ṛṣiḥ śaṅkara īritaḥ |
uṣṇikchando devatāsya mahāpūrvā sarasvatī |
kāmo bījaṃ ravistatvaṃ kāmāptyai viniyojanam |
kāmaḥ klīmiti | ravirvījaṃ praṇavamiti mantravidaḥ ||
evaṃ saṃsmṛtya ṛṣyādīndhyātvā pūrvoktamārgataḥ |
smarannarthaṃ paṭheccaṇḍīstavaṃ spaṣṭavadakṣaram ||
samāptau tu mahālakṣmīṃ dhyātvā kṛtvā ṣaḍaṅgakam |

p. 88) japedaṣṭaśataṃ mūlaṃ devatāyai nivedayet ||
ādāvapyaṣṭaśataṃ prajapenmūlamatra tu |

mūlamantrastu | aiṃ hrīṃ klīṃ cāmuṇḍāyai bicce || rudrayāmale-

prathamaṃ caritaṃ proktaṃ madhukaiṭabhaghātanam |
dvitīyaṃ viddhi caritaṃ mahiṣāsuraghātanam ||
uttaraṃ caritaṃ jñeyaṃ śumbhadaityavadhānvitam |
caritāni japettrīṇi sarahasyānyatandritaḥ ||

anyatrāpi-

śatamādau śatañcānte japenmantraṃ navākṣaram | 
caṇḍīsaptaśatīmadhye sampuṭo'yamudāhṛtaḥ ||
sakāmaḥ sampuṭo jāpyo niṣkāmaḥ sampuṭaṃ vinā ||

atha mārīcakalpe-

rātrisūktaṃ japedādau madhye saptaśatīstavam |
prānte tu japanīyaṃ vai devīsūktamiti kramaḥ ||
evaṃ sampuṭitaṃ stotraṃ sarvakāmārthasiddhidam |

	atha rātrisūktaṃ devīsūktaṃ ca vaidikamiti | vastutastu 
saptaśatīstotrāntargatameveti prāmāṇikāḥ | brahmaṇā tu kṛtaṃ stotraṃ 
viśveśvarīsūktaṃ tadeva rātrisūktamiti vyavahriyate | atha namo devyai 
mahādevyai śivāyai satataṃ nama ityādi sarvāpado 
bhaktivinamramūrtibhirityantaṃ devīsūktamiti | śakrādayaḥ suragaṇā 
ityārabhyāsmākamunmadasurāribhavannamaste ityantaṃ lakṣmīsūktamiti | 
devyā hate tatra mahāsurendre ityārabhya trailokyavāsināmīḍye lokānāṃ 
varadā bhava ityantaṃ sarasvatīsūktamiti |

atha vārāhītantre-

saṃskṛtya bhūmiṃ saṃsthāpya kalaśaṃ tīrthavāriṇā |
pūrayitvā svarṇagarbhaṃ kṣipedaṣṭamṛdaṃ tataḥ ||
dharmakāmaḥ kṣipetsvarṇaṃ dhanakāmastu mauktikam |

p. 89) śrīkāmaḥ kamalaṃ nyasyetkāmārthī rocanaṃ nyaset ||
mokṣakāmo nyasedratnaṃ japakāmoparājitām ||
uccāṭanārthaṃ vyāghrīṃ ca vaśyārthaṃ śikhimūlikām ||
māraṇārthaṃ marīcaṃ ca ketakīṃ mohanāya ca |
ākarṣaṇārthaṃ dhattūraṃ prakṣipetkalaśodare ||
japenmantraṃ maheśāni śṛṇuṣva gadato mama |
ādimārabhya prajapetsṛṣṭikrama ihocyate ||
japecchakrādimārabhya śumbhadaityavadhāvadhiḥ |
ādyācchakrādiparyantaṃ sthitikrama udāhṛtaḥ ||
śeṣamārabhya ādyantaṃ saṃhārakrama īritaḥ |
sthitipāṭhaḥ sarvakārye muktikāme na saṃhṛtiḥ ||
śrīkāme puṣṭikāme ca sṛṣṭikrama udāhṛtaḥ |

	sāvarṇiḥ sūryatanaya ityārabhya sāvarṇirbhavitā manurityantaṃ 
pāṭhaḥ sṛṣṭikramaḥ | purā śumbhetyādinavādhyāyānpaṭhitvā 
śakrādhyāyāntaṃ paṭhediti sthitikramaḥ |
	evaṃ devyāṃ varaṃ labdhvā ityādi saṃhārakrameṇa sāvarṇiḥ 
sūryatanaya iti ślokāntaṃ paṭhanīyamiti saṃhārakramaḥ | ayaṃ pāṭhaḥ 
saṅkaṭe praśasya iti dākṣiṇātyāḥ |

atha viśeṣamāha-

dharmārthakāmamokṣāṇāṃ kāmaḥ pūrvamukho hunet |
mohane māraṇe caiva dakṣiṇāśāmukho hunet ||
uccāṭane tūttarāsyo diśāmevaṃ vyavasthitiḥ |
dharmārthakāmamokṣāṇāṃ saṃsiddhau pāyasaṃ hunet ||
māraṇe mokṣaṇe caiva tathoccāṭanakarmaṇi |
hunenmāṣaṃ trimadhvāktaṃ mohane madhupāyasam ||
stambhane tu māttulaphalaṃ vaśye tu śvetasarṣapam |

p. 90) atha devīprayogasāre-

saṅkaṭe samanuprāpte duscikitsye bhaye sadā |
jātidhvaṃse kulacchede āyuṣo nāśa āgate ||
vairivṛddhau vyādhivṛddhau dhananāśe tathā kṣaye |
tathaiva trividhotpāte tathā caivātipātake ||
kuryādyatnācchatāvṛttiṃ tataḥ sampadyate śubham |
vipadastasya naśyanti ante yāti parāṅgatim ||
śriyo vṛddhiḥ śatāvṛttyā rājyavṛddhistathā parā |
manasā cintitaṃ devi siddhedaṣṭottaraṃ śatam ||
śatāśvamedhayajñānāṃ phalaṃ prāpnoti suvrate |
sahasrāvarttanāllakṣmīrāvṛṇoti svayaṃ sadā ||
kāmānmanogatānprāpya naro mokṣamavāpnuyāt |
yathāśvamedhaḥ ṛtuṣu devānāṃ ca yathā hariḥ ||
stavānāmapi sarveṣāṃ tathā saptaśatīstavaḥ |
bhuktimuktī na dūrasthe śuddhapāṭhavatāṃ nṛṇām ||
nātaḥ parataraṃ kiñcitstotramasti varānane ||

atha navarātrividhānam |

athātaḥ sampravakṣyāmi navarātravidhānakam |
japedekottarāṃ bṛddhiṃ dināni navasaṃkhyayā ||
navākṣarīvidhānena sampūjyārthaṃ vicakṣaṇaḥ |
sahasraṃ prajapenmantraṃ pratyahaṃ ca yathāvidhi ||
hutvā daśāṃśato homaṃ tatra siddhiṃ samāpnuyāt |

aparaśca-

bilvamūle samāsīno māsamekaṃ japetsudhīḥ |
madhuratrayasaṃyuktairbilvapatraiśca sādhakaḥ ||
māsaṃ homavidhānena kuryādvā taddaśāṃśataḥ |
kamalaiḥ kṣīrasaṃmiśrairlakṣmīprāptirna saṃśayaḥ ||

p. 91) atra tu namo devyai ityādimantraireva daśāṃśena homaḥ kārya iti 
sampradāyaḥ | brāhmaṇakaraṇe dakṣiṇāniyamastatraiva kathitaḥ |

pañcāvṛte svarṇamekaṃ trirāvṛtte tadarddhakam |
ekāvṛtte pādamekaṃ dadyādvā śaktito budha iti ||

gāyatrī yathā yajurvede-

	kātyāyanyai vidmahe kanyākumāri dhīmahi tanno durgā 
pracodayāditi |

atha prayogāntaraṃ kātyāyanītantre-

	pūrvāhṇe ṣoḍaśāṅgulamitaṃ caturaṅgulonnataṃ sthaṇḍilaṃ 
kṛtvā tatra śuddhagoghṛtena ekasminpātre rudravartiṣu trayodaśa 
dīpānprajvālya dīpeṣu prathamadīpe 
prathamādhyāyadevatāmāvāhyāsanapādyādigandhadānāntaṃ 
sampūjya puṣpasamarpaṇasamaye vakṣyamāṇanavabhiḥ ślokaiḥ 
puṣpāṃjaliṃ samarpya dhūpadīpau datvā nīrājanam | ekaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ 
kumār-rī suvāsinīṃ ca bhojayet | saṅkalpayedvā | evaṃ 
tadadhyāyadevatāvāhanādibrāhmaṇabhojanāntaṃ pratyadhyāyaṃ 
kuryāt | ante prabhūtanaivedyādibhirmahāpūjā anugrahe pūrvāhṇe 
samāptiḥ | nigrahe'parāhṇe samāptiḥ | ślokāstu viśveśvarīṃ 
jagaddhātrīm 1 stutā suraiḥ pūrvamabhīṣṭasaṃśrayā 2 yā sāmprataṃ 
coddhata 3 yā ca smṛtā tatkṣaṇameva hanti naḥ 4 sarvāvādhāpraśamanaṃ 
5 atra vairivināśanamityatra roganāśanamityāhyūhyam | evaṃ 
daityatāpitairityatrāpi | sarvamaṅgalamāṅgalye 6 sṛṣṭisthitivināśānāṃ 
7 śaraṇagatadīnārta 8 sarvasvarūpe sarveśe 9 ete'nugrahe nava ślokāḥ 9 
vaśye tu viśeṣaḥ | oṃ hrīṃ raktacāmuṇḍe turu turu amukaṃ me 
vaśamānaya svāhā | anena pratyadhyāyamādyantayoḥ pūjā sarvānte 
ayutamantrajapaḥ ayutahomaśca |

homayetkaṭutailena raktacandanarājikāḥ |
sahasrāhutimātreṇa rājānaṃ vaśamānayet ||
madhunāśokapuṣpaṃ ca rātrau hutvā tu pūrvavat |

p. 92) cakavartī bhavedvaśyaṃ caṇḍīmantraprabhāvataḥ |
ante śataṃ brāhmaṇāḥ kumāryaśca bhojanīyāḥ |
dhanārthaṃ bilvapatraiḥ pūjā vaśyārthaṃ karavīrajaiḥ ||

	japākusumairvā putrārthaṃ tulasīdalaiḥ | tuṣṭyarthaṃ paṅkajaiḥ | 
mahākāryārthaṃ prayoge tu ṣoḍaśamāṣaiḥ svarṇamūrtiṃ kṛtvā 
kalaśasthāpanaṃ ca kṛtvā tadagre ghaṭārgalayantre ṣaṭkoṇayantre vā 
dīpaṃ sthāpayet | mahati kārye ūnapañcāśa49 ddināni madhye 
caturviṃśatidināni 24 tadardhamalpe 12 | evameva mūrteḥ 
svarṇaparimāṇamapi bodhyam | mahati kārye brāhmaṇāḥ 3 
prayogakartāraḥ | alpe eka eva | nigrahe tu | 
sarvābādhāpraśamanamityasyāgre evamuktvā samutpatyeti paṭhet | 
asminprayoge tarpaṇamapi kuryāt | trayodaśa pātrāṇi 
dugdhenāpūryādyantayoḥ puṣpāṃjaliṃ datvā pratiślokaṃ 
prathamādhyāyadevatāṃ tarpayāmi iti prayogeṇa tarpayet | tathā 
trayodaśasvagniṣu kramāttadadhyāyadevatā āvāhya sampūjya 
pratimantramoṃ prathamādhyāyadevatāyai namaḥ svāheti mantreṇa 
pāyasena juhuyāt pratyadhyāyaṃ vā paṭhet | dīpeṣu akṣatapuñjeṣu 
jalapātreṣu agniṣu kumārīṣu vā kārya ityupadeśaḥ | athaitatstotrapāṭhe 
ḍāmarakalpoktanavārṇasampuṭīkararṇaprayogavidhiḥ | tatrādau 
taduddhāraḥ-
aiṃ bījamādīndusamānadīptiṃ hrīṃ sūryatejodyutimadvitīyam | 
klīṃ cāpi vaiśvānaratulyarūpaṃ tṛtīyamānantyasukhāya cintyam | 
cāśuddhajāmbūnadakāntisūryaṃ muṃ pañcamaṃ vestutaraṃ 
prakalpyam |
ḍāṃ ṣaṣṭhamugrārtiharaṃ sunīlaṃ yai saptamaṃ kṛṣṇataraṃ 
ripughnam ||
vipāṇḍuraṃ tvaṣṭamamādisiddhaṃ cce dhūmravarṇaṃnavamaṃ 
viśālamiti ||

aiṃ hrīṃ klīṃ cāmuṇḍāyai vicce | iti navākṣaro mantraḥ | asya 
prayogaḥ | nābhimātrejale sahasrajapātkavitāsiddhiḥ | ayutajape 
rājabandhanasaṃkaṭādibhyo mokṣaḥ |

asminnavākṣare mantre mahālakṣmīrvyavasthitā |

p. 93) tasmātsusiddhaḥ sarveṣāṃ sarvadikṣu pradīpakaḥ ||
ityetenāsya siddhādicakraśodhanaṃ nāstīti sūcitam |
navākṣarasya ṛṣayo brahmaviṣṇumaheśvarāḥ |
chandāṃsyuktāni munibhirgāyatryuṣṇiganuṣṭubhaḥ ||
devyaḥ proktā mahāpūrvā kālī lakṣmī sarasvatī |
nandā śākambharī bhīmāḥ śaktayo'sya manoḥ smṛtāḥ ||
syū raktadantikā durgābhrāmaryo bījasañcayāḥ |
agnivāyubhagāstattvaṃ phalaṃ vedatrayodbhavam ||
sarvābhīṣṭaprasidhyarthaṃ viniyoga udāhṛtaḥ |

atha prayogaḥ |

	asya śrīnavārṇamantrasya brahmaviṣṇumaheśvarā ṛṣayaḥ 
gāyatryuṣṇiganuṣṭubhaśchandāṃsi | 
mahākālīmahālakṣmīmahāsarasvatyo devatāḥ 
nandāśākambharībhīmāḥ śaktayaḥ | raktadantikādurgābhrāmaryo 
bījāni agnirvāyubhagāstattvāni sarvābhīṣṭasiddhyarthaṃ jape viniyogaḥ | 
tataḥ ṛṣyādinyāsaṃ kuryāt | yathā- brahmaviṣṇumaheśvarebhyo namaḥ 
śirasi | gāyatryuṣṇiganuṣṭupchandobhyo namo hṛdi | 
nandāśākambharībhīmāśaktibhyo namaḥ dakṣiṇastane | 
śrīraktadantikādurgā bhrāmarībījebhyo namaḥ vāmastane | 
agnivāyusūryatatvebhyo namaḥ hṛdaye |

uktaṃ ca-

ṛṣiśchandodevatāni śiromukhahṛdi nyaset |
stanayoḥ śaktibījāni tattvāni hṛdaye punaḥ ||

atha viśeṣamāha-

mantrāṇāṃ pallavo vāso mantrāṇāṃ pallavaḥ śiraḥ |
śiraḥpallavasaṃyukto mantraḥ puṣṭādisādhakaḥ ||
huṃkārapallavopeto māraṇe brāhmaṇaṃ vinā |
yantrabhañjanakāryeṣu sughorabhayanāśane ||

p. 94) vaṣaḍantaḥ prakalpayastu grahavādhāvināśakaḥ |
uccāṭane tu samprokto mantraḥ phaṭpallavānvitaḥ ||

ete pallavāstu tattatkarmaṇi caṇḍīpāṭhepi ślokādau yojyāḥ | tathā

nyāsahīno bhanenmūke mṛtaḥ syācchirasā binā |
apāstavāso nagnaḥ syāt suptaḥ syādāsanaṃ vinā ||
guruṃ vinā vithā mantraḥ savyajāpastu śūnyakaḥ |
nirvīryo duṣṭadattaḥ syādanyabījastu kīlitaḥ ||
duṣṭadattaḥ duṣṭāya datta ityarthaḥ |
devachandarṣihīno yaḥ sa tu supto bhujaṅgamaḥ |

	mūkādayo mantrā varjyāḥ | vaidike na pallavādivicāraḥ | hrīṃ 
caṇḍikāyai ityanenāsya mantrasya stotrasya ca ṣaḍaṅgakaranyāsau | 
hṛdayāya namaḥ | śirase svāhā | śikhāyai vaṣaṭ | kavacāya hum | 
netratrayāya vauṣaṭ | astrāya phaḍiti kramaḥ | astraṃ sarvadikṣu bodhyam | 
navabījamantrānte caṇḍikā tṛptyatāmiti mantreṇa tarpaṇam | evaṃ 
homādiṣu jñeyam |

atha yantramāha |

likhedaṣṭadalaṃ padmaṃ kuṅkumāgurucandanaiḥ |
tataścaturviṃśadalaṃ nūpureṇa samanvitam ||
padmamadhye likheccakraṃ ṣaṭkoṇaṃ caṇḍikāmayam |
ṣaṭkoṇacakramadhyasthamādyaṃ bījatrayaṃ nyaset ||

	tatra madhyabīje mahālakṣmīḥ | taddakṣiṇe mahākālī vāme 
sarasvatī | pūrvādiṣu ṣaṭsu koṇeṣu bījānyanyāni vinyaset | idaṃ 
pūjāyantram | padmāsanādyanyatamamāsanam | tathā 
saptaśatīślokairnyāsaḥ | 

khaṅginīśūlinītyādi paṭhitvā ślokapaṃcakam |

ādyaṃ kṛṣṇataraṃ bījaṃ dhyātvā sarvāṃgakaṃ nyaset | 
ādyātmaikamiti |

śūlena pāhi no devītyādiślokacatuṣṭayam |
paṭhitvā sūryasadṛśaṃ dvitīyaṃ sarvato nyaset |

p. 95) dvitīyaṃ | grīmiti | sarvasvarūpa ityādiślokānāṃ pañcakaṃ punaḥ 
paṭhitvā sphaṭikābhāsaṃ tṛtīyaṃ sarvato nyaset | tṛtīyaṃ klīṃ miti | 
uktaireva ślokairāvṛttidvayena ṣaḍaṃganyāsopi kvaciduktaḥ | 

tataḥ ṣaḍaṃgaṃ kurvīta vibhaktairmūlavarṇakaiḥ |
ekenaikena vaikena caturbhiryugalena ca ||
samastena ca mantreṇa kuryādaṃgāni ṣaṭ sudhīḥ |
evaṃ nyāsavidhiṃ kṛtvā sṛṣṭimudrāṃ vilokayet ||
tataḥ pariṣiñcya pūjārthaṃ dravyāṇi navabījataḥ |
talapṛṣṭhodbhavāṃ mudrāṃ datvāstreṇāvaguṇṭhyet ||
arghapātraṃ pratiṣṭhāpya pūrayecchubhavāriṇā |
gandhapuṣpākṣatatāṃstatra vinyasya nṛpanandana ||
dhyātvā sarvāṇi tīrthāni hastau datvābhimantrayet |
rakṣārthaṃ tasya kartavyā cakramudrāstasaṃyutā ||
arghodakena saṃskāraṃ pūjādravyasya pūrvavat |

atha mahākālyādidevatātrayadhyānam |

khaḍgaṃ cakragadeṣucāpaparighān śūlaṃ bhuśuṇḍīṃ śiraḥ
śaṃkhaṃ saṃdadhatīṃ karaistrinayanāṃ sarvāṃgabhūṣāvṛtām |
nīlāśmadyutimāsyapādadaśakāṃ seve mahākālikāṃ
yāmastaucchayite ddarau kamalajo haṃtuṃ madhuṃ kaiṭabham || 1 ||

akṣasrakparaśuṃ gadeṣukuliśaṃ padmaṃ dhanuḥ kuṇḍikāṃ
 daṃḍaṃ śaktimasiṃ ca carma jalajaṃ ghaṃṭāṃ surābhājanam |
śūlaṃ pāśasudarśane ca dadhatīṃ hastaiḥ pravālaprabhāṃ
seve sairibhamardinīmiha mahālakṣmīṃ sarojasthitām || 2 ||

ghaṃṭāṃśūlahalāni śaṃkhamuśale dhanuḥ sāyakaṃ |
hastābjairdadhatīṃ ghanāṃtaralasacchītāṃśutulyaprabhām |
gaurīdehasamudbhavāṃ trijagatāmādhārabhūtāṃ mahā
pūrvāmatra sarasvatīmanubhaje śumbhādidatyārdinīm

p. 96) tata uktamaṃtraiḥ pañcopacāraiḥ pūjāṃ kṛtvā 
japellakṣacatuṣṭayam | taddaśāṃśataḥ pāyasānnena juhuyāt | pūjite 
hemaretasi etadevāsya puraścaraṇam-

mārkaṃḍeyapurāṇoktaṃ nityaṃ caṃḍīstavaṃ paṭhan |
puṭitaṃ mūlamantrasya japenāpnoti vāṃchitamiti ||

atha śatacaṇḍīvidhiḥ |

śaṅkarasya bhavānyā vā prāsādanikaṭe śubham |
maṇḍapaṃ dvāravedyāḍhyaṃ kuryātsudhvajatoraṇam ||
tatra kuṇḍaṃ prakurvīta pratīcyāṃ madhyatopi vā |
snātvā nityakriyāṃ kṛtvā vuṇuyāddaśa vāḍavān ||
jitendriyānsadācārānkulīnānsatyavādinaḥ |
vāḍavān brāhmaṇān |
vyutpannāṃ caṇḍikāpāṭharatān lajjādayāvataḥ |
madhuparkavidhānena svarṇavastrādidānataḥ |
japārthamāsanaṃ mālāṃ dadyāttebhyopi bhojanam ||
te haviṣyānnamaśnantī mantrārthagatamānasāḥ |
bhūmau śayānāḥ pratyekaṃ japeyuścaṇḍikāstavam ||
mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇoktaṃ daśakṛtvaḥ sucetasaḥ |
navārṇaṃ caṃḍikāmantraṃ japeyuścāyutaṃ pṛthak ||

	pṛthak sampuṭīkaraṇajapāditi śeṣaḥ | pratyekaṃ 
brāhmaṇairayutajapaḥ kāryaḥ |
	athāṣṭamīnavamīcaturdaśīpaurṇamāsīṣu yathā 
śatāvṛttisamāptirbhavati tathā prārambhaḥ kartavya iti sāmpradāyikāḥ |

yajamānaḥ pūjayecca kanyānāṃ navakaṃ śubham |
dvivarṣādyā daśāṃtāstāḥ kumārīḥ paripūjayet |
ekābdāyāḥ prītyabhāvādrudrābdā tu vivarjitā |

p. 97) tāsāṃ krameṇa nāmāni- kumārī 1|2 trimūrtiḥ 2|3 kalyāṇī 3|4  
ropiṇī 3|4 rohiṇī 4|5 kālikā 5|6 caṇḍikā 6|7 śāmbhavī 7|8 durgā 8|9 
subhadrā 9|10 etaireva nāmamaṃtraiḥ tāsāṃ pūjā |

tāsāmāvāhane maṃtraḥ procyate śaṃkaroditaḥ |

yathā-

maṃtrākṣaramayīṃ lakṣmīṃ mātaṇāṃ rūpadhāriṇīm |
navadurgātmikāṃ sākṣātkanyāmāvāhayāmyaham |

ityāvāhya pūjayet | maṃtro yathā-

jagatpūjye jagadvaṃdye sarvaśaktisvarūpiṇi |
pūjāṃ gṛhāṇa kaumāri jaganmātarnamostu te || 1 ||

tripurāṃ tripurādhāraṃ trivargajñānarūpiṇīm |
trailokyavaditāṃ devīṃ trimūrtiṃ pūjayāmyaham || 2 ||

kalātmikāṃ kalātītāṃ kāruṇyahṛdayāṃ śivām |
kalyāṇajananīṃ devīṃ kalyāṇīṃ pūjayāmyaham || 3 ||

aṇimādiguṇādhārāmākārādyakṣarātmikām |
anaṃtaśaktikāṃ lakṣmīṃ rohiṇīṃ pūjayāmyaham || 4 ||

kāmacārīṃ śubhāṃ kāṃtā kālacakrasvarūpiṇīm |
kāmadāṃ karuṇodārāṃ kālikāṃ pūjayāmyaham || 5 ||

caḍavīrāṃ caṃḍamāyāṃ caṃḍamuṃḍaprabhañjanīm |
pūjayāmi sadā devīṃ caṃḍikāṃ caḍavikramām || 6 ||

sadānaṃdakarīṃ śāṃtāṃ sarvadevanamaskṛtām |
sarvabhūtātmikāṃ lakṣmīṃ śāmbhavīṃ pūjayāmyaham || 7 ||

durgame dustare ghore bhavaduḥkhavināśinīm |
pūjayāmi sadā bhaktyā durgāṃ durgārtināśinīm || 8 ||

suṃdarīṃ svaṇavarṇābhāṃ sukhasaubhāgyadāyinīm |
subhadrajananīṃ devīṃ subhadrāṃ pūjayāmyaham || 9 ||

p. 98) etairmantraiḥ purāṇoktaistāṃ tāṃ kanyāṃ samarcayet |

	tatra hīnādhikāṅgī kuṣṭhavraṇayutā andhā kāṇā kurūpā 
kekarā romayugdehā dāsījā rogiṇya ityevamādyā varjyāḥ |

viprāṃ sarveṣṭasaṃsiddhyai yaśase kṣatriyodbhavām |
vaiśyajāṃ dhanalābhāya putrāptyai śūdrajāṃ yajediti ||

gandhapuṣpadhūpadīpabhakṣyabhojyairyathāśaktivastrābharaṇaiśca 
pūjayet |

atha pūjanaprakāraḥ |

vedyāṃ viracite ramye sarvatobhadramaṇḍale |
ghaṭaṃ saṃsthāpya vidhivattatrāvāhyārcayecchivām ||
tadagre kanyakāścāpi pūjayedbrāhmaṇānapi |
upacāraistu vividhairnavārṇavarṇānapi ||

oṃ kāraḥ prathamaṃ pīṭhaṃ pūrṇapīṭhamataḥ param |
tṛtīyaṃ kāmapīṭhaṃ ca pūjayetsampradāyataḥ ||
pūrvādidikṣu pīṭhasya gaṇeśādicatuṣṭayam |
gaṇeśakṣetrapālau ca pāduke vaṭukāstrayaḥ ||
āgneyādicaturdikṣu pūjya devīcatuṣṭayam |
jayā ca vijayā caiva jayantī cāparājitā ||

	pūrvoktayantre pūrvakoṇe sarasvatīsahito brahmā | śrīsahito 
viṣṇurnai-ṛtye umayā sahitaḥ śivo vāyavye |

p. 100) tebhyopi dakṣiṇāndatvā gṛhṇīyādāśiṣastataḥ |
evaṃ kṛte jagadvaśyaṃ sarvaṃ naśyantyupadravā iti ||
etaddaśaguṇaṃ kuryāccaṇḍīsāhasrake vidhim |
vidyāvataḥ sadācārān brāhmaṇānvṛṇuyācchatam ||

caturdinairuktarītyeti śeṣaḥ |
ayutaṃ prajapeyuste pratyekaṃ navavarṇakam ||
pūrvoktāḥ kanyakāḥ pūjyāḥ pūrvamantraiḥ śataṃ śubhāḥ |
vedāhamevaṃ sampādya homaṃ kuryuḥ prayatnataḥ ||
saptaśatyāśśatāvṛttyā pratimantravidhānataḥ |
lakṣasaṅkhyānavārṇena pūrvoktairdravyasañcayaiḥ ||
hotṛbhyo dakṣiṇāṃ dattvā pūrvoktānbhojayeddvijān |
sahasrasammitān sādhūndevyārādhanatatparān ||
evaṃ sahasrasaṃkhyāke kṛte caṇḍīvidhau nṛṇām |
siddhatyabhīpsitaṃ sarvaṃ duḥkhaughaśca vinaśyati ||
mārīdurbhikṣarogādyā naśyanti vyasanoccayāḥ |

andhakaśca mahādaityo durgāhomaparāyaṇaḥ |
kavacāhutimātreṇa maheśena nipātita iti ||

atha prayogakramaḥ |

	tantroktavidhinā śivālaye vā maṇḍapaṃ vidhāya tanmadhye vedīṃ

p. 101) ca nirmāya pratīcyāṃ kuṇḍamadhye vā kṛtvā | kṛtanityakriyaḥ

p. 102) saptaśatīśatāvṛttyā pratiślokaṃ lakṣasaṃkhyo navārṇairhomaḥ | 
śeṣaṃ pūrvavat | iti sahasracaṇḍīvidhānam | evaṃ 
sahasracaṇḍīdaśaguṇo'yutacaṇḍīvidhiḥ | taddaśaguṇo 
lakṣacaṇḍīvidhiriti | jape home dakṣiṇāyāṃ kumārībhojane 
brāhmaṇabhojane ca daśaguṇatvamiti |
	atha prayogāntarāṇi kātyāyanītantroktāni | 
pratimantramādyantayoḥ praṇavaṃ japenmantrasiddhiḥ | agre sarvatra 
ślokapadaṃ mantropalakṣaṇam | 
sapraṇavamanulomavyāhṛtitrayamādāvante tu vilomaṃ tadityevaṃ 
pratiślokaṃ kṛtvā śatāvṛttipāṭhe'tiśīghraṃ siddhiḥ || 1 ||

	pratiślokamādau jātavedasa iti ṛcaṃ paṭhetsarvakāmasiddhiḥ || 2 ||
	
	ṛcamapi likhyate | jātavedase sunavāmasomamarātīyatonidahāti 
vedaḥ | sanaḥ parṣadatidurgāṇi viśvānāveva sindhuṃ duritātyagniḥ |
iti ṛk | apamṛtyuvāraṇāya ādāvante śataṃ tryambakamantraṃ japet || 4 ||

pratiślokaṃ tanmantrajapa ityanyat || 5 ||

pratiślokaṃ śaraṇāgatadīnārttaślokaṃ paṭhet sarvakāryasiddhiḥ || 6 ||

pratiślokaṃ karotu sā naḥ śubhaheturīśvarītyarddhaṃ 
paṭhetsarvakāmāptiḥ || 7 ||

svābhīṣṭavaraprāptyai evaṃ devyā varaṃ labdhveti ślokaṃ pratiślokaṃ 
paṭhet || 8 ||

sarvāpannivāraṇārthaṃ pratiślokaṃ durge smṛteti paṭhet || 9 ||

asya kevalasyāpi ślokasya kāryānusāreṇa lakṣamayutaṃ sahasraṃ śataṃ 
vā japaḥ || 10 ||

sarvā bādhetyasya lakṣajape ślokoktaṃ phalam || 11 ||

itthaṃ yadā yadeti ślokasya lakṣajape mahāmārīśāntiḥ || 12 ||

tato vabre nṛpo rājyamiti mantrasya lakṣajape punaḥ svarājyalābhaḥ || 13 ||

hinasti daityatejāṃsītyanena sadīpabalidāne ghaṇṭābandhane ca 
bālagrahaśāntiḥ || 14 ||

ādyāvṛttimanulomena paṭhitvā tato viparītakrameṇa dvitīyāṃ

p. 103) kṛtvā punaranulomenetyevamāvṛttitrayeṇa ukteṣu prakāreṣu 
śīghraṃ kāryasiddhiḥ || 15 ||
	sarvāpannivāraṇāya durge smṛtetyardham | tataḥ yadantike yacca 
dūrake bhayaṃ vindati māmiha | pavasānarītañjahi iti ṛcaṃ tadante 
dāridryaduḥkhetyarddhamevaṃ kāryānusāreṇa lakṣamayutaṃ sahasraṃ 
vā śataṃ japaḥ || 16 ||
	kāṃsosmi tāṃ hiraṇyaprākārāmārdrāṃ jvalantīṃ tṛptāṃ 
tarpayantīṃ padme sthitāṃ padmavarṇāṃ tāmihopahvaye śriyam || iti 
ṛcaṃ pratiślokaṃ paṭhellakṣmīprāptiḥ || 17 ||
	pratiślokamanṛṇā asminniti mantraṃ paṭhedṛḍhaparihāraḥ || 18 ||
māraṇārthamevamuktvā samutpatyeti pratiślokaṃ paṭhet || 19 ||
māraṇoktavṛttibhiḥ phalasiddhiḥ || 20 ||
jñānināmapi cetāṃsīti ślokajapamātreṇa 
sadyomohanamityanubhavasiddham || 21 ||
pratimaṃtraṃ tat ślokapāṭhe tvavaśyaṃ mohanam || 22 ||
rogānaśeṣāniti ślokasya pratiśloke pāṭhe sakalaroganāśaḥ || 23 ||
tanmātrajapepi saḥ || 24 ||
ityuktā sā tadā devī gambhīreti ślokasya pratiślokapāṭhe 
pṛthagjape vā vidyāprāptiḥ || 25 ||
vāgvaikṛtanāśaśca || 26 ||
bhagavatyā kṛtaṃ sarvamityādi dvādaśottaraśatākṣaro mantraḥ 
sarvakāmadaḥ sarvāpannivārakaśca || 27 ||
devi prapannārtihare iti ślokasya yathākāryaṃ 
lakṣāyutasahasraśatānyatame japepratiślokapāṭhe vā sarvāpannivṛttiḥ 
sarvakāmāvāptiśca || 28 ||
eṣu prayogeṣu pratiślokaṃ kevaladīpanamaskāreṇa vā siddhiḥ || 29 ||
pratiślokaṃ kāmabījasaṃpuṭitasyaikacatvāriṃśaddinaṃ 
trirāvṛttau sarvakāmasiddhiḥ || 30 ||

p. 104) ekaviṃśatidinamuktarītyā pratyahaṃdvādaśāvṛttau vaśīkaraṇam 
|| 31 ||
	māyābījasampuṭitasya kūṭapallavasahitasya saptadinaparyantaṃ 
trayodaśāvṛttāvuccāṭanasiddhiḥ || 32 ||
	ekonapañcāśaddinaparyantaṃ pratiślokaṃ śrībījasampuṭitasya 
pañcadaśāvṛttyā lakṣmīprāptiḥ || 33 ||
	atha kāmānābhedena dhyānabhedaḥ | vaśye raktaṃ pauṣṭike 
karburam | uccāṭane dhūmraṃ māraṇe kṛṣṇaṃ santānārthaṃ 
nīlotpaladalaśyāmam | ratnākāradhyānaṃ sarvakāmadaṃ yuddhe 
kālāgnisamaṃ jalabhayemahāśoṣayuktaṃ viṣādibhaye 
pīyūṣavṛṣṭiyuktapūrṇacandrasadṛśaṃ dhyāyet ||
	iti śrījīvanāthakṛte dīkṣāprakāśe saptaśatīpāṭhavidhiḥ |

atha pārthivapūjāprakāraḥ |

	oṃ sadyojātaṃ prapadyāmi sadyojātāya vai namaḥ | bhave 
bhavenātibhave bhavasya māṃ bhavodbhavāya namo namaḥ | iti mantreṇa 
mṛttikāgrahaṇam | oṃ vāmadevāya namo jyeṣṭhāya namaḥ śreṣṭhāya 
namo rudrāya namaḥ | kālāya namaḥ kalavikaraṇāya namo balavikaraṇāya 
namo balāya namo balapramathanāya namaḥ sarvabhūtadamanāya namo 
manonmanāya namaḥ | iti mantreṇa jalakṣepaḥ | tataḥ oṃ aghorebhyotha 
ghorebhyo'ghoraghoratarebhyaḥ sarvebhyaḥ sarvasarvebhyo namastestu 
rudrarūpebhyaḥ |

iti liṅgakaraṇam |

	oṃ tatpuruṣāya vidmahe mahādevāya dhīmahi tanno rudraḥ 
pracodayāditi bāṇaṃ kuryāt | oṃ īśānaḥ sarvavidyānāmīśvaraḥ 
sarvabhūtānāṃ brahmādhipatirbrahmaṇodhipatirbrahmā śivo me'stu 
sadā śivom | iti vedikāyāṃ bāṇaṃ saṃyojya dhyāyet |

tadyathā-

oṃ dhyāyennityaṃ maheśaṃ rajatagirinibhaṃ cārucandrāvataṃsaṃ
ratnākalpojjvalāṅgaṃ paraśumṛgavarābhītihastaṃ prasannam |

p. 105) padmāsīnaṃ samantātstutamamaragaṇairvyāghrakṛttiṃ vasānaṃ
viśvādyaṃ viśvabījaṃ nikhilabhayaharaṃ pañcavaktraṃ trinetram ||
karpūragauraṃ karuṇāvatāraṃ saṃsārasāraṃ bhujagendrahāram | 
sadā ramantaṃ hṛdayāravinde bhavaṃ bhavānīsahitaṃ namāmi ||
kailāsapīṭhāsanamadhyasaṃsthaṃ bhaktaiśca nandyādibhirarcyamānam |
bhaktārtidāvānalamaprameyaṃ dhyāyedumāliṅgitamindubhūṣam ||
iti dhyātvā bilvapatraṃ sagandhapuṣpākṣataṃ gṛhītvā oṃ 
manojyotiriti mantreṇa pratiṣṭhāṃ kuryāt |

mantro yathā-

	oṃ manojyotirjuṣatāmājyasya bṛhaspatiryajñamimaṃ tanotvariṣṭaṃ 
yajña/ samimaṃ dadhātu viśve devā sa iha mādayantāmoma pratiṣṭha |

tata imaṃ paṭhet-

svāmin sarvajagannātha yāvatpūjāvasānakam |
tāvattvaṃ prītibhāvena liṅge'smin sannidhiṃ kuru ||

tataḥ pādyam |

	oṃ bhavāya bhavanāśāya mahādevāya dhīmahi | ugrāya 
cogranāśāya sarvāya śaśimauline namaḥ | śivāya idaṃ pādyam | oṃ 
sāmbaśivāya namaḥ | idamarghyaṃ svāhā | idamācamanīyaṃ svadhā | 
imaṃ madhuparkaṃ svadhā | idamanulepanam | etāni puṣpāṇi | oṃ 
sāmbaśivāya vauṣaṭ || bilvapatrāṇi dattvā | oṃ śarvāya kṣitimūrtaye 
namaḥ | aiśānyām | oṃ bhavāya jalamūrtaye namaḥ prācyām | oṃ 
rudrāyāgnimūrtaye namaḥ | āgneyyām | oṃ ugrāya vāyumūrtaye namaḥ 
dakṣiṇasyām | oṃ bhīmāyākāśamūrtaye namaḥ | nair-ṛtyām | oṃ 
paśupataye yajamānamūrtaye namaḥ | vāruṇyām | oṃ mahādevāya 
somamūrtaye namaḥ | vāyavyām | oṃ īśānāya sūryamūrtaye namaḥ | 
uttarasyām | oṃ namaḥ śivāyeti puṣpāñjaliṃ dadyāt | eṣa

p. 106) dhūpaḥ namaḥ | eṣa dīpaḥ namaḥ | etāni naivedyāni nivedayāmi | 
idaṃ tāmbūlaṃ namaḥ | tataḥ | namaḥ |

oṃ kārarūpāya namo namokṣaravapurbhṛte |
namo nādātmane tubhyaṃ namo bindukalātmane |
aliṅgaliṅgarūpāya rūpātīyāya te namaḥ |
tvaṃ mātā sarvalokānāṃ tvameva jagataḥ pitā ||
tvaṃ bhrātā tvaṃ suhṛnmitraṃ tvaṃ priyaḥ priyarūpadhṛk |
tvaṃ gurutvaṃ gatiḥ sākṣāt tvaṃ pitā tvaṃ pitāmahaḥ ||
namaste bhagavan rudra bhāskarāmitatejase |
namo bhavāya rudrāya umayā sahitāya ca ||
sarvāya kṣitipālāya sadāsurabhide namaḥ |
paśūnāṃ pataye caiva pāvakāmitatejase ||
bhīmāya vyomarūpāya śabdamātrāya te namaḥ |
mahādevāya somāya amṛtāya namo namaḥ ||
ugrāya yajamānāya namaste karmayogine |

	oṃ harāya namaḥ | oṃ maheśvarāya namaḥ | oṃ śūlapāṇaye namaḥ | 
mṛttikāgrahaṇam | nirmāṇaṃ kuryāt | prāṇapratiṣṭhā | oṃ śūlapāṇe iha 
supratiṣṭhito bhava | oṃ manojyotiriti paṭhet oṃ pinākadhṛṣe namaḥ || oṃ 
paśupataye namaḥ | oṃ śivāya namaḥ | āvāhanaṃ ihāgaccha | iti snapanaṃ 
kuryāt | pūjanam | oṃ mahādevāya namaḥ | visarjanam |

pārthivasya ca liṅgasya yanmayā pūjanaṃ kṛtam |
anena bhagavān rudro vāñchitārthaṃ prayacchatu || 
iti praṇamya liṃgāni visarjayet |
namo bhavāya rudrāya śivāya ca namo namaḥ |
īśānāya vṛṣāṃkāya bhīmogrābhyāṃ namo namaḥ ||
mahādevāya guptāya vāsudevapriyāya ca |

p. 107) vāsudevāya śāntāya tathomāpataye namaḥ ||
namaśca nīlakaṃṭhāya śitikaṃṭhāya te namaḥ ||
gopate śaṃkarāyaiva sadā sarvamayāya ca ||
prātassampūjya sāyaṃ ca kṛtāñjalipuṭaḥ sthitaḥ |
ūnaviṃśatināmāni paṭhate yo narottamaḥ ||
sa pāpairmucyate sarvairdivi devaiśca pūjyate |
tasya stotrasya paṭhanāddahetpāpaṃ śivaṃ vrajet ||
vāraṃ vāraṃ praṇamet | arddhacandrākāraṃ pradakṣiṇāṃ kuryāt |
gaccha gaccha paraṃ snānaṃ svasthānaṃ parameśvara |
pūjārādhanakāle ca punarāgamanāya ca ||
oṃ sāmba śiva pūjitosi prasīda śiva svasthānaṃ gaccheti vadet |

atha śivarātrau praharacatuṣṭaye pūjāvidhiḥ |

na snānena na vastreṇa na ca dhūpena nārcayā |
tuṣyāmi na tathā puṣpairyathā rātryupavāsataḥ ||
tato rātrau prakartavyaṃ śivaprīṇanatatparaiḥ |
prahare prahare snānaṃ pūjā caiva viśeṣataḥ ||
atra vīpsayā praharacatuṣṭayapūjanaṃ pratīyata iti |

narasiṃhacaryāyām-

śaivo vā vaiṣṇavo vāpi yadi syādanyapūjakaḥ | 
sarvaṃ pūjāphalaṃ hanti śivarātribahirmukhaḥ ||

saṃvatsarapradīpe-

dugdhena prathamaṃ snānaṃ dadhnā caiva dvitīyake |
tṛtīye tu tathājyena caturthe madhunā tathā ||

īśānasaṃhitāyām-

phālgune kṛṣṇacaturdaśyāṃ ravivāro yadā bhavet |
bhaumo vāpi bhaveddevi kartavyaṃ vratamuttamam ||

p. 108) śivayogasya yogena tadbhaveduttamottamam |
śivarātrivrataṃ nāma sarvapāpavināśanam ||
ācāṇḍālamanuṣyāṇāṃ bhuktimuktipradāyakam |

nāgarakhaṇḍe-

upavāsaprabhāveṇa balādapi ca jāgarāt |
śivarātrestathā tasya liṅgasyāpi prapūjanāt ||
akṣayān labhate kāmānśivasāyujyamāpnuyāt ||

padmapurāṇe-

varṣe varṣe mahādevi naro nārī pativratā |
śivarātrau mahādevaṃ kāmabhaktyā prapūjayet ||

īśānasaṃhitāyām-

evameva vrataṃ kuryātpratisaṃvatsaraṃ vratī |
caturdaśābdikaṃ taddhi caturviṃśābdikaṃ tathā ||
sarvān kāmānavāpnoti sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate |

atha prayogaḥ |

	prātarudaṅmukhaḥ | oṃ tatsadityuccārya sūryasoma iti paṭhitvā 
jalādīnyādāya |

śivarātrivrataṃ hyetatkariṣye'haṃ mahāphalam |
nirvighnamastu me cātra tvatprasādājjagatpate ||

	ityanena vrataniyamagrahaṇaṃ kuryāt | punarjalamādāya | oṃ 
saubhāgyādisamastabhogamokṣāvāptikāmaḥ śivarātrivratamahaṃ 
kariṣye || iti ||

tato mantraṃ paṭhet |

caturdaśyāṃ nirāhāro bhūtvā śambhoḥ pare'hani |
bhokṣyehaṃ bhuktimuktyarthaṃ śaraṇaṃ me bhaveśvara ||

	tato rātrau prathamaprahare pratiṣṭhite liṅge apratiṣṭhite vā 
pratiṣṭhāṃ vidhāya pūjāṃ kuryāt | haḥ astrāya phaḍiti 
pādaghātatrayeṇa bhūmiṃ niḥsārya tenaiva tālatrayeṇa karatruṭikayā 
digbandhanaṃ bhūtaśuddhyādikaṃ

p. 109) ca vidhāya | hāṃ hṛdayāya namaḥ | hīṃ śirase svāhā | hrūṃ 
śikhāyai vaṣaṭ | hauṃ kavacāya huṃ | hauṃ netratrayāya vauṣaṭ | haḥ 
astrāya phaṭ iti tālatrayam | chotikayā daśadigbandhanaṃ kṛtvā haumiti 
mantreṇa prāṇāyāmaṃ vidhāya pārthivaśivaliṃge uktavidhinā śivaṃ 
pūjayet | tatrāyaṃ viśeṣaḥ | hauṃ īśānāya namaḥ |

prathamaprahara dugdhena snapayitvā punarjalena snapayet |

tataḥ-

śivarātrivrataṃ deva pūjājapaparāyaṇaḥ |
karomi vidhivaddānaṃ gṛhāṇārghaṃ maheśvara ||

	ityanenārghyaṃ dattvā gandhādibhiḥ pūjayitvā praṇamya 
gītavādyastavapāṭhatatkathāśravaṇādibhiḥ praharaṃ nayet |

taddhyānaṃ tajjapaḥ snānaṃ tatkathāśravaṇādikam |
upavāsakṛto hyete guṇāḥ proktāḥ purātanaiḥ ||
atha dvitīyaprahare | hauṃ oṃ aghorāya namaḥ | iti gandhasnānam ||
tataḥ oṃ namaḥ śivāya śāntāya sarvapāpaharāya ca |
śivarātrau dadamyarghyaṃ prasīda umayā saha ||

	ityarghyaṃ dattvā pūjādikaṃ pūrvavat | tatastṛtīyaprahare | oṃ 
hauṃ sadyojātāya namaḥ | iti ghṛtena snānam | arghyamantrastu | 

duḥkhadāridryaśokena dagdhohaṃ pārvatīpriya |
śivarātrau dadāmyarghyamumākānta prasīda me ||

	anyatprathapraharavatkuryāt | caturthaprahare | oṃ hauṃ tatpuruṣāya 
namaḥ | iti madhunā snānam |

arghyamantrastu-

mayā kṛtānyanekāni pāpāni hara śaṅkara |
śivarātrau dadāmyarghyamumākānta gṛhāṇa me ||

	anyatpūrvavat | tataḥ oṃ namaḥ śivāyeti mūlamantraṃ japtvā 
avighneneti mantraṃ paṭhet |

p. 110) mantro yathā-

oṃ avighnena vrataṃ devaṃ tvatprasādātsamarpitam |
kṣamasva jagatāṃ nātha trailokyādhipate hara ||
yanmayātra kṛtaṃ puṇyaṃ tacca tubhyaṃ niveditam |
tvatprasādānmahādeva vratamadya samarpitam ||
prasanno bhava me deva sadgatiṃ dehi śaṃkara |
tvadālokanamātreṇa pavitrosmi na śaṃsayaḥ ||
tataḥ paradine brāhmaṇān bhojayitvā mantraṃ paṭhet |

yathā-

saṃsārakleśadagdhasya vratenānena śaṅkara |
prasīda sumukho bhūtvā jñānadṛṣṭiprado bhava ||
ityuktvā smaraṇaṃ kuryāt |

iti śivarātripūjāvidhānaṃ sampūrṇam |

atha śivapūjāyāṃ viśeṣaḥ |

rātrāvudaṅmukhaḥ kūryātpūjāṃ pūrvamukho divā |
śivācanaṃ sadāpyevaṃ śuciḥ kuryādudaṅmukhaḥ ||
sadeti divārātrau |

atra hetuṃ darśayati rudrayāmale-

na prācīmagrataḥ śambhornodocīṃ śaktisaṃsthitām |
na pratīcīṃ yataḥ pṛṣṭhamato dakṣaṃ samāśrayet || yajamānaḥ |
śambhoḥ prācīmavasthito yajanaṃ na samāśrayet |

	śambhorjagatsaṃhārakasyāgrataḥ sāmmukhyāt | pañcavaktrapakṣe 
pradhānaṃ vaktraṃ prācāntu sthitam | ekavaktrapakṣe 
tadrūpamāhādityapurāṇe-

saumyamaulīndusūcyakṣamekavaktraṃ caturbhujam |
śūlapaṃkajahastaṃ ca varadābhayapāṇikam ||
āyatākṣaṃ surārādhyaṃ sarvābharaṇabhūṣitam |

p. 111) śivarūpaṃ gṛhe kuryātprāsāde vāpyaninditam ||

	atāgre pūjāniṣedhāt | devāgre svasya cāpyagre prācī proktā 
gurūttamairityasya na viṣaya iti kintvabhidhānādiprasiddhaprācī parā 
grāhyā etadanusārādeva vakṣyamāṇapūrvādyāgneyāntapūjā |

yatraiva bhānustu viyatyudeti prācīti tāṃ vedavido vadanti |
ataḥ parāṃ pūjakadevayośca sadā''gamajñāḥ pravadanti tāṃ ca ||
iti vacanāt |

atha viśeṣaṃ darśayati liṅgapurāṇe-

vinā (1) bhasmatripuṇḍreṇa vinā rudrākṣamālayā |
