saṃvatsarapradīpe-

tripurasya vadhe kāle rudrasyākṣṇoḥ patanti ye |
aśrūṇāṃ bindavaste tu rudrākṣā abhavan bhuvi ||
ekadvitrica turyañca ṣaṭ sapta vasavo nava |
daśaikādaśa dvādaśa trayodaśa caturdaśa ||
eteṣāṃ tu mukhānāṃ hi devatā sa ca śaṅkara |
guhyaṃ guhyataraṃ deva kathayasva yathārthataḥ ||

śrīśaṅkara uvāca |

śṛṇu ṣaṇmukha tattvena vaktre vaktre yathākramam |
ekavaktraḥ śivaḥ sākṣādbrahmahatyāṃ vyapohati ||
dvivaktro devadevaśca gobadhādivināśakaḥ |
trivaktro dahanaḥ sākṣādbhrūṇahatyāṃ vyapohati ||
caturvaktraḥ svayaṃ brahmā narahatyāṃ vyapohati |
pañcavaktraḥ svayaṃ rudraḥ kālāgnirnāma nāmataḥ ||
agamyāgamane caiva abhakṣyasyāpi bhakṣaṇe |
mucyate sarvapāpebhyaḥ pañcavaktrasya dhāraṇāt ||
ṣaḍvaktraḥ kārtikeyastu dhartavyo dakṣiṇe bhuje |
brahmahatyādibhiḥ pāpairmucyate nātra saṃśayaḥ ||
saptavaktro mahāsena ananto nāma nāgarāṭ |
gurutalpādibhiḥ pāpairmucyate nātra saṃśayaḥ ||
aṣṭavaktro mahāsenaḥ sākṣāddevo vināyakaḥ |
pṛṣṭhodakareṇāpi saṃspṛśedvā gurustriyam ||
evamādīni pāpāni hyātipāpāni sarvaśaḥ |
vighnāstasya vinaśyanti mukto yāti parāṃ gatim ||
ete guṇā bhavaṃtyasya aṣṭavaktrasya dhāraṇāt |

p. 115) navāsyo bhairavaḥ sākṣāddhārayedvāmake bhuje ||
kāpilo muktidaḥ sākṣānmama tulyaphalaṃ labhet |
lakṣakoṭisahasrāṇi brahmahatyāṃ karoti yaḥ ||
tatsarvaṃ dahate śīghraṃ navavaktrasya dhāraṇāt |
daśavaktro mahāsenaḥ sākṣāddevo janārdanaḥ ||
grahāścaiva piśācādyā vetālā brahmarākṣasāḥ |
sarpāśca sarve naśyanti daśavaktrasya dhāraṇāt ||
ekādaśāsyarudrāṇāṃ rudrā ekādaśa smṛtāḥ |
śikhāyāṃ dhārayennityaṃ tasya puṇyaphalaṃ śṛṇu ||
aśvamedhasahasrasya vājapeyaśatasya ca |
hemaśṛṅgasya lakṣasya samyagdattasya yatphalam ||
tatphalaṃ samavāpnoti rudravaktrasya dhāraṇāt |
rudrākṣo dvādaśāsyastu kaṇṭhadeśe ca dhārayet ||
ādityastuṣyate nityaṃ dvādaśārkavyavasthitam |
evaṃ trayodaśāsyaśca caturdaśamukhastathā ||
anantaphaladāvetau guṇaṃ vaktuṃ na śakyate |
caturdaśamukhaṃ caivaṃ śilāyāṃ dhārayedbudhaḥ ||
vinā mantreṇa rudrākṣaṃ yo dhatte bhuvi mānavaḥ |
sa yāti narakaṃ ghoraṃ yāvadindrāścaturdaśa ||
rudrākṣasya pratiṣṭhāyāṃ mantraḥ pañcākṣaraḥ smṛtaḥ |
oṃ tryambakaṃ vā aghoramantraṃ viśeṣeṇa prayojayet |
pañcāmṛtaṃ pañcagavyaṃ snānakāle prayojayet |
alpenāpi ca mantreṇa rudrākṣasya dvijottama ||
pratiṣṭhāṃ vidhivatkuryāttatodhikaphalaṃ labhet |
tato yathā sumantreṇa dhārayedbhaktisaṃyutaḥ ||

athātra mantro yathā-

oṃ oṃ bhṛ/ śa/ namaḥ 1 oṃ oṃ namaḥ 2 oṃ hrīṃ namaḥ 3 oṃ hraṃ 
namaḥ 4

p. 116) oṃ huṃ namaḥ 5 oṃ oṃ hu/ huṃ namaḥ 6 oṃ sa hraṃ namaḥ 7 oṃ 
haṃ namaḥ 8 oṃ hrīṃ namaḥ 9 oṃ śrīṃ namaḥ 10 oṃ hrīṃ hūṃ 
namaḥ 11 oṃ kṣāṃ kṣauṃ namaḥ 12 oṃ namaḥ 13 oṃ namo namaḥ 14

atha prakārāntaroktamantrāḥ-

	oṃaiṃ 1 oṃ śrīṃ 2 oṃ kūṃ 3 oṃ draḥ 4 oṃ hrīṃ 5 oṃ hrīṃ 6 
oṃ hrīṃ 7 oṃ brūṃ rāṃ 8 oṃ hrāṃ 9 oṃ hrīṃ 10 oṃ hrīṃ 11 oṃ 
kṣauṃ strīṃ 12 oṃ umāṃ 13 oṃ namo namaḥ 14 yathāpyekādi 
caturdaśamukharudrākṣeṣu phalamantraviśeṣāḥ santi tathāpi 
sulabhatvātpañca vaktrarudrākṣaṃ vidhipūrvakaṃ dhārayet |

pañcavaktraḥ svayaṃ rudraḥ kālāgnirnāma || nāmataḥ |
āgamyāgamane caiva abhakṣyasyāpi bhakṣaṇe |
mucyate sarvapāpebhyaḥ pañcavaktrasya dhāraṇāt || ityukteḥ |
om. huṃ nama iti pratyekamaṣṭottaraśataṃjaptvāmbhasā prakṣālya 
dhārayet |

liṃgapurāṇe-

āyuṣmān balavān śrīmān putravāndhanavān sadhīḥ |
varamiṣṭaṃ labhelliṅgaṃ pārthivaṃ yaḥ samarcayet ||
tasmāttatpārthivaṃ liṅgaṃ jñeyaṃ sarvārthasādhanam |
vārkṣaṃ vittapradaṃ jñeyaṃ sphāṭikaṃ sarvakāmadam ||
nārmadaṃ girijaṃ śreṣṭhamanyadapīha liṃgavat | 

kālakaumudyām-

akṣādalpapramāṇaṃ hi na liṅgaṃ kutracinnaraḥ |
kurvītāṅguṣṭhato hrasvaṃ na kadācitsamāśrayediti ||

akṣo'śītiraktikā | brahmapurāṇe-

yāvanna dīyate cārgho bhāskarāya niveditaḥ |
tāvanna pūjayedviṣṇuṃ śaṅkaraṃ ca maheśvarīm ||
sarvatraiva praśasto'bjaḥ śivasūryārcanaṃ vinā |

p. 117) abjaḥ śaṃkhaḥ |
talliṃge pūjayenmantrī sarvadevān pṛthak pṛthak |

devīpurāṇe-

mṛdāharaṇasaṅghaṭṭau pratiṣṭhāhvānameva ca |
snapanaṃ pūjanaṃ caiva visarjanamataḥ param ||
haro maheśvaraścaiva śūlapāṇiḥ pinākadhṛk |
śivaḥ paśupatiścaiva mahādeva iti kramāt ||

	atra pūrvoktasaptakarmaṇi paravacanoktasaptanāmabhiḥ 
kriyāsvarūpavibhaktimabhidhāya yathāyathaṃ kāryāṇi | 
adṛṣṭārthayorarthakramāsambhave pāṭhakramādevāvāhanātprāk 
pratiṣṭhā śrāddhe kuśāsanadānavaditi tatrānuṣṭhānam | yathā oṃ 
harāya nama iti mṛdāharaṇam | oṃ maheśvarāya nama iti saṅghaṭṭanam | 
oṃ śūlapāṇaye nama iti prāṇapratiṣṭhā |

tato dhyānam-

	dhyāyennityaṃ maheśamityādinā | tataḥ oṃ pinākadhṛṣe nama 
ityāvāhya | oṃ paśupataye nama iti snapanaṃ vidhāya | oṃ śivāya nama 
iti pūjayet | visarjanātpūrvaṃ prācyaiśānyādyaṣṭadikṣu vāmāvartena 
pūjayet | oṃ śarvāya kṣitimūrtaye namaḥ pūrve 1 | oṃ bhavāya 
jalamūrtaye namaḥ aiśānyām 2 | oṃ rudrāyāgnimūrtaye nama uttare 3 | 
oṃ ugrāya vāyumūrtaye namaḥ vāyavyām 4 | oṃ 
bhīmāyākāśamūrtaye namaḥ paścime 5 | oṃ paśupataye 
yajamānamūrtaye namo nair-ṛtyām 6 | oṃ mahādevāya somamūrtaye 
namaḥ dakṣiṇe 7 | oṃ īśānāya sūryamūrtaye namaḥ āgneyyām |

mūrtayoṣṭau śivasyaitāḥ pūrvādikramayogataḥ |
āgneyyāntāḥ prayojyāstu vedyāṃ liṃge śivaṃ yajet ||

nāndīpurāṇe-

gobhūvastrahiraṇyādibalipuṣpānnivedayet |
jñeyo namaḥ śivāyeti mantraḥ sarvārthasādhakaḥ ||

p. 118) sarvamantrādhikaścāyamoṅkārādyaḥ ṣaḍakṣaraḥ |

bhaviṣyapurāṇe pañcārakṣaramupakramya-

apavitraḥ pavitro vā sarvāvasthāṃ gatopi vā |
mahāpātakayuktopi mantrasyāsya japāttathā ||
adhikārī bhavetsarva iti devo'vravīcchivaḥ |

tathā-

sarveṣāmeva pātrāṇāṃ paraṃ nātra maheśvaraḥ ||
patantaṃ dhārayedyasmādatīva narakārṇavāt |
śivamuddiśya yaddattaṃ sarvakāraṇakāraṇam ||
tadanantaphalaṃ dāturbhavatīha kimadbhutam |

śivadharme-

tasmātpuṣpaphalaistoyaiḥ patrairapi ca yatphalam |
tadantaphalaṃ jñeyaṃ bhaktirevātra kāraṇam ||

bhaviṣye-

liṃgānulepanaṃ kāryaṃ divyagandhaistu gandhibhiḥ |
varṣe koṭiśataṃ divyaṃ śivaloke mahīyate ||

skandapurāṇe-

śuṣkāṇyapi ca patrāṇi śrīvṛkṣasya sadaiva tu |
dātavyāni narairbhaktyā śivabhaktimabhīpsubhiḥ ||

bhaviṣye-

dhattūrairapi yo nityaṃ sakṛtpūjayate śivam |
golakṣaphalaṃ prāpya śivaloke mahīyate ||
bilvapatrairanartaiśca yo liṃgaṃ pūjayet sakṛt |
sarvapāpavinirmuktaḥ śivaloke mahīyate ||

yathā ca-

sarvakāmapradaṃ bilvaṃ mahādāridryanāśanam ||
bilvapatrātparaṃ nāsti yena tuṣyati śaṅkaraḥ |
keśakīṭāpaviddhāni niśi paryuṣitāni ca ||

p. 119) svayaṃ patitapuṣpāṇi tyajedupahatānyapi |
devadārusametaṃ ca sarvaśrīvāsakundurum |
śrīphalaṃ cājyamiśraṃ ca datvāpnoti parāṃ gatim ||

sarjaḥ śālarasaḥ kunduruśaileyam |

ebhyaḥ saugandhikaṃ dhūpaṃ ṣaṭsahasraguṇottaram |
aguruṃ śatasāhasraṃ dviguṇaṃ cāsitāgrurum ||
guggulaṃ ghṛtasaṃyuktaṃ sākṣādgṛhṇāti śaṅkaraḥ |
tailenāpi hi yo dadyādghṛtābhāvena mānavaḥ ||
tena dīpapradānena śivavadrājate divi |

nandikeśvarapurāṇe-

śālitaṇḍulaprasthasya kuryādannaṃ susaṃskṛtam |
śivāyataṃ caruṃ dadyāccaturdaśyāṃ viśeṣataḥ ||

śivasarvasve ca-

ekamāmraphalaṃ pakvaṃ yaḥ śivāya nivedayet |
varṣāṇāmayutaṃ bhogaiḥ krīḍate śivamandire ||
ekaṃ mocāphalaṃ pakvaṃ yaḥ śivāya nivedayet |
varṣalakṣaṃ mahābhogaiḥ śivaloke mahīyate ||
naivedyaṃ ghṛtasaṃsaktaṃ madhupakvaṃ nivedayet |
agniṣṭomasya yajñasya phalaṃ prāpnoti mānavaḥ ||

śivadharme-

liṅgavedī bhaveddevī liṃgaḥ sakṣānmaheśvaraḥ |
tayoḥ sampūjanātsyātāṃ devī devaśca pūjitau ||

devīpurāṇe-

davyaṃ vratettato'savyaṃ praṇālīṃ naiva laṃghayet |
ekībhūtamato rudraṃ yaḥ kuryāttriḥ pradakṣiṇam ||
kṣaṇāttasya bhavenmuktirna tasya punarudbhavaḥ |

bhaviṣye-

jānubhyāṃ caiva pāṇibhyāṃ śirasā ca vicakṣaṇaḥ |

p. 120) kṛtvā praṇāmaṃ deveśaṃ sarvānkāmānavāpnuyāt |

mahābhārate-

sarvalakṣaṇahīnopi yukto vā sarvapātakaiḥ |
sarvaṃ tarati tatpāpaṃ bhavāya śirasā naman ||

	tato vāraṃ vāraṃ praṇamya oṃ vāmadevāya namaḥ iti mantreṇa 
saṃhāramudrayā visṛjya taducchiṣṭena caṇḍograśūlapāṇiṃ sampūjya 
viṣṇunirmālyayuktaśivanirmālyaṃ śirasā dhārayet |

agrāhyaṃ śivanirmālyaṃ patraṃ puṣpaṃ phalaṃ jalam |
śālāgrāmaśilāsparśātsarvaṃ yāti pavitratāmiti ||

atha pārthivaliṅgānāṃ viśeṣapūjāprakāraḥ |

śrīmantramahādadhau yathā-

	sādhakaḥ snātvā nityakarma kṛtvā śuci bhūmiṃ gatvā 
uparisthāṃ mṛttikāmapāhṛtya | hū/ pṛthivyai nama ityabhimantrya 
mṛttikāṃ gṛhītvā tālamavalokya niḥśarkarāṃ kṛtvā śuddhapātre 
nityaśivapūjārthaṃ nidadhyāt | tataḥ śubhadine 
bālagaṇeśakumārayormantro harādikāt saptamanūnapi 
pūjāsiddhyarthaṃ sadgurossakāśādgṛhṇīyāt | hrīṃ gaṃ glauṃ 
gaṇapataye glauṃ gaṃ hrīṃ iti kumāratantraḥ | aiṃ kṣuṃ kṣuṃ klīṃ 
kumārāya namaḥ iti kumāramantraḥ | oṃ namo harāya maheśvarāya | oṃ 
namaḥ śūlapāṇaye | oṃ namaḥ pinākine | oṃ namaḥ paśupataye | oṃ 
namaḥ śivāya | oṃ namo mahādevāya iti saptamanūnapi saṅgṛhya 
śubhadine sveṣṭasiddhyarthaṃ śivārcanamārabhet | 
	tadyathā | kṛtanityakriyaḥ sūryāyārghyaṃ datvā mṛttikāmādāya | 
vamityabhimantritena jalenābhiṣicya sveṣṭapramāṇāṃ pratimāṃ piṇḍayet 
| tāṃ pātre nidhāya saṅkalpaṃ kuryāt |

p. 121) sa yathā | amukagotrasyāmukaśarmaṇo mama 
manobhilaṣitakāryasiddhyarthaṃ 
yathāsaṃkhyakapārthivaliṃgapūjanamahaṃ kariṣye |
	iti saṃkalpya mṛtpiṇḍān mṛdaṃ gṛhītvā gaṇapatimantreṇa 
varābhayalasatpāṇikamalaṃ bālagaṇeśaṃ nirmāya pīṭhe saṃsthāpya 
svamantreṇa sampūjya haramantreṇa oṃ namo harāya iti mantreṇa | 
śatamātrādhikāṃ mṛdaṃ gṛhītvā | oṃ namo maheśvarāya iti mantreṇa 
aṅguṣṭhamānādadhikaṃ vitastyavadhi manoharaṃ liṅgaṃ 
tulyapramāṇaṃ tulyarūpaṃ racayet | tataḥ oṃ namaḥ śūlapāṇaye iti 
liṅgaṃ pīṭhe saṃsthāpeyt | tato'vaśiṣṭamṛdā kumāramantreṇa 
kumāraṃ kṛtvā liṃgapaṃktyante saṃsthāpya svamantreṇa pūjayet | tataḥ 
pratiliṅgasthaṃ haram | oṃ namaḥ pinākine iti mantreṇa samāvāhya 
dhyāyet |

tadyathā-

dakṣāṃgasthaṃ gajapatimukhaṃ prāmṛśan dakṣadoṣṇā
vāmorusthādripatitanayāṃke guhaṃ cāpareṇa |
iṣṭābhītiparakarayuge dhārayannindukānti-
ravyādgrastatribhuvanamado nīlakaṇṭhastrinetraḥ ||

	evaṃ dhyātvā oṃ namaḥ paśupataye iti mantreṇa śivaṃ snāpayet | 
tataḥ | oṃ namaḥ śivāyeti mantreṇa gandhādīn samarpya 
pūrvādivāmāvartena daśadikṣu śarvādīn pūjayet | tadyathā | oṃ 
śarvāya kṣitimūrtaye namaḥ pūrve | oṃ bhavāya jalamūrtaye namaḥ 
aiśānyāṃ | oṃ rudrāyāgnimūrtaye namaḥ uttare | oṃ ugrāya 
vāyumūrtaye namaḥ vāyavyām oṃ bhīmāyākāśamūrtaye namaḥ 
paścime | oṃ paśupataye yajamānamūrtaye namaḥ nair-ṛtyām | oṃ 
mahādevāya somamūrtaye namaḥ dakṣiṇe | oṃ īśānāya sūryamūrtaye 
namaḥ agnikoṇe | tata indrādīn vjrādīnapi pūrvādidakṣiṇāvartena 
pūjayet | tato dhūpaṃ dīpaṃ naivedyaṃ datvā vāraṃ vāraṃ natvā 
pradakṣiṇāmarddhacandrākṛtiṃ kṛtvā mantrajapaṃ

p. 122) kṛtvā stavādikaṃ paṭhitvā saṃhāramudrayā visṛjya 
gaṇeśakārtikeyāvapi visṛjet atha kāmanābhedena dhyānamuktam | tatraiva | 
tadyathā-

dhanaputrādikāmaistu śivorcyaḥ proktalakṣaṇaḥ 
vidyākāmaiścintanīyaḥ paraśuṃ hariṇaṃ varam |
jñānamudrāṃ dadhaddhastairvaṭamūlamupāśritaḥ ||
atha sandhikaraṇārthaṃ dhyānam |
puṃsorviruddhayoḥ sandhau kuryālliṃgāni sādhakaḥ |
nadītīrādapānītamṛdā tāni ca pūjayet ||
tatra dhyeyo hariharaḥ śaṃkhapadmāhiśūlabhṛt |
indranīlaśaraccandranibho bhūṣaṇapuñjavān |

atha dampatyorvirodhaśamanārthaṃ dhyānamāha-

dampayoravirodhārthamarddhanārīśvaraḥ smṛtaḥ |
pīyūṣapūrṇakalaśaṃ dadhatpāśāṃkuśāvapi ||

athoccāṭanamāraṇavidveṣaṇārthaṃ dhyānam-

uccāṭe māraṇe dveṣe dhyātavyaḥ punarīdṛśaḥ |
kālīhastāmbujālambaḥ śūlaprotastviṣāṃ cayaḥ ||
muṇḍamālālasatkaṇṭho rāvavitrāsitākhilaḥ ||

atha kāryavaśātprayogavidhānamāha-

pūjayetkāryavaśato lakṣāvadhi sahasrataḥ |
lakṣaṃ pārthivaliṃgānāṃ pūjanādbhaktimuktibhāk ||
lakṣaṃ tu guṇaliṃgānāṃ pūjanātpārthivo bhavet |
yā nārī guṇaliṃgāni sahasraṃ pūjayetsatī ||
bhartuḥ sukhamakhaṃḍaṃ sā prāpyānte pārvatī bhavet |
navanītasya liṅgāni sampūjyeṣṭamavāpnuyāt ||
prātargomayaliṃgāni nityaṃ vastrāṇi pūjayet ||
vṛhatībilvayoḥ patrairnaivedyaṃ guḍamarpayet ||
iṣṭaphalāvāptiḥ |
bhasmanā gomayasyāpi bālikāyāstathā phalam ||

p. 123) krīḍanti bālakā bhūmau liṃgaṃ kṛtvā rajomayam ||
pūjayanti vinodena te'pi syuḥ kṣitināyakāḥ ||
evaṃ māsatrayaṃ kurvannanalpaṃ labhate dhanam |
ekādaśaiva liṃgāni gomayotthāni pūjayet ||
prātarmadhyāhnayossāyaṃ niśīthe prativāsaram |
sa sarvāḥ sampado yāyātṣaṇmāsādevamācaran ||
ekādaśa yajennityaṃ śālipiṣṭamayāni yaḥ |
liṅgāni māsamātreṇa sa kalmaṣacayaṃ dahet |
sphāṭikaṃ pūjitaṃ liṃgaṃ manonikaranāśanam |
sarvakāmapradaṃ puṃsāmudumbarasamudbhavam ||
revāmadhyāśmajaṃ sarvasiddhidaṃ duḥkhanāśanam ||
yathākathañcilliṃgasya pūjā nityaṃ kṛteṣṭadā ||
yo yajetpicumandotthaiḥ patrairgomayajaṃ śivam |
kruddhaṃ maheśvaraṃ dhyāyan sa parājayate ripūn ||
yo liṃgaṃ pūjayennityaṃ śivabhaktiparāyaṇaḥ |
merutulyopi tasyāśu pāparāśirlayaṃ brajet ||
dogdhrīrgāsturagāṃlkṣaṃ yo dadyādvedapāṭhine |
pārthivaṃ yor'ccayelliṃgaṃ tayorliṅgārcako varaḥ ||
caturdaśyāṃ tathāṣṭamyāṃ paurṇamāsyāṃ vidhukṣaye |
payasā snāpayedīśaṃ dharādānaphalaṃ vrajet ||
liṃgapūjāṃ vidhāyāgre stotraṃ vā śatarudriyam |
prajapettanmanā bhūtvā śive svaṃ vinivedayet ||
yatsaṃkhyākaṃ yajelliṃgaṃ tanmitaṃ homamācaret |
ājyānvitaistilairagnau ghṛtairvā pāyasena vā ||
śivamantreṇa tasyānte brāhmaṇānapi bhojayet |
evaṃ kṛte samasteṣṭasiddhirbhavati niścitam ||

p. 124) 
atha pūjāyantrasaṃskāraḥ |

vāmakeśvaratantre-

bhairavyuvāca |

cakrabhedaṃ mahādeva tvatprāsādānmayā śrutam |
idānīṃ śrotumicchāmi pratiṣṭhākarmanirṇayam ||

śrīśaṃkara uvāca |

śṛṇu devi mahābhāge jagatkāriṇi kaulini |
yantrasyodyāpanaṃ kāryaṃ sarvakarmavinirṇayam ||
snātvā saṃkalpayenmantrī gurorarcanamācaran |
paṃcagavyaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā śivamantreṇa mantritam ||
taccakraṃ niḥkṣipenmantrī praṇavena samākulam |
taduddhṛtya tataścakraṃ sthāpayetsvarṇapātrake ||
pañcāmṛtena dugdhena śītalena jalena ca |
candanena sugandhena kastūrīkuṅkumena ca ||
payodadhighṛtakṣaudraśarkarādyairanukramāt |
toyadhūpāntaraiḥ kuryātpañcāmṛtavidhiṃ punaḥ ||
hāṭakaiḥ kalaśairdevīmaṣṭabhirvāripūritaiḥ |
kaṣāyajalasampūrṇaiḥ kārayetsnānamuttamam ||
snānaṃ samāpya tāṃ devīṃ svāpayetsvarṇapātrake |
spṛṣṭvā pātraṃ kuśāgreṇa gāyatryā cābhimantrayet ||
aṣṭottaraśataṃ devi devatābhāvasiddhaye |
ātmaśuddhiṃ tataḥ kṛtvā ṣaḍaṃgairyajanaṃ caret ||
tatrāvāhya mahādevīṃ jīvanyāsaṃ ca kārayet |
upacāraiḥ ṣoḍaśabhirmahāmudrādibhiḥ sadā ||
phalatāmbūlanaivedyairdevīṃ tatra samarcayet |
paṭṭasūtrādikaṃ dadyādvastrālaṅkārameva ca ||

p. 125) mukuraṃ cāmaraṃ ghaṇṭāṃ yathāyogyaṃ maheśvari |
sarvametatprayatnena dadyādātmahite rataḥ ||
tato japetsahasraṃ ca sakalepsitasiddhaye ||
balidānaṃ tataḥ kuryātpraṇameccakrarājakam |
aṣṭottaraśataṃ hutvā sampātājyaṃ viniḥkṣipet |
homakarmaṇyaśaktaśceddviguṇaṃ japamācaret ||
dhenumekāṃ samānīya svarṇaśṛṃgādyalaṃkṛtām |
gurave dakṣiṇāṃ dadyāt tato devyā visarjanamiti ||

atha pūjāyantrasaṃskāraprakāraḥ |

p. 126) tataḥ paṭṭasūtrādikaṃ dattvā'ṣṭottarasahasraṃ japet |

tathā brahmapurāṇe-

māghakṛṣṇacaturdaśyāmādidevo mahāniśi |
śivaliṃgasamudbhūtaḥ koṭisūryasamaprabhaḥ ||

p. 127) tathā saurāgame-

prahare prahare snānaṃ pūjāṃ caiva vidhānataḥ |
śivaliṅgasya kurvīta arghadānaṃ ca bhaktitaḥ 
ekenaivopavāsena kṛtenātra tithau śivaḥ |
prīyate bhagavān devo bhuktimuktipradāyakaḥ ||

tathā nandikeśvaraḥ |

dṛṣṭvā liṃgaṃ maheśasya sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate |
janmajanmakṛtairdehī manovākkāyakarmajaiḥ ||

tathoktaṃ skandapurāṇe-

madhyandinakare prāpte yo liṅgaṃ paripūjayet |
sampūrṇāṃ pṛthivīṃ dattvā yatphalaṃ tadavāpnuyāt ||
trisandhya kurute yastu pratyahaṃ śivapūjanam |
vadennāmāni tasyaiva sa śivo nātra saṃśayaḥ ||
śatamaṣṭottaraṃ yastu pratyahaṃ śivamarcayet |
ekakālaṃ trikālaṃ vā sa punāti kulatrayam ||
varaṃ prāṇaparityāgaḥ śiraso vāpi kartanam |
na caivāpūjya bhuñjīta śivaliṅgaṃ maheśvaram ||
aśvamedhasahasrasya sahasraguṇitasya ca |
phalaṃ prāpnoti vacanānmahādeveti nāmataḥ ||
pāṭhenāpi narā nityaṃ ye smaranti maheśvaram |
tepi yānti tanuṃ tyaktvā śivalokamanāmayam ||
dravyamāyuḥ śriyaṃ putrān yaccānyanmanasepsitam |
sphāṭikaṃ liṅgamāsādya japamānaḥ samaśnute ||
prātharutthāya yo liṃgaṃ bhaktyā sampūjayetsakṛt |
kapilāyāḥ śataṃ dattvā yat phalaṃ tadavāpnuyāt ||
sūtake mṛtake caiva na tyajecchivapūjanam |
nairantaryeṇa ṣaṇmāsaṃ pūjayedvidhinā śivam ||
puṇyaṃ tadeva labhate sakalaṃ viṣuvercanāt |

p. 128) etadeva ca vijñeyaṃ grahaṇe cottarāyaṇe ||
kārtikyāṃ ca yugādyāyāṃ ṣaḍaśītyāṃ viśeṣataḥ |
yastu kṛṣṇacaturdaśyāṃ snātvā devaṃ pinākinam ||
ārādhayeddvijaśreṣṭha tasya nāsti punarbhavaḥ |
phalānāṃ dve sahasre tu mahāsnānaṃ prakīrtitam |
mṛttāmrahemaraupyaiśca koṭikoṭiguṇaṃ kramāt ||

atrānyadapi-

jalajānāṃ tu puṣpāṇāṃ bilvapatrasya caiva hi |
eṣāṃ paryuṣitā śaṅkā kāryā pañcadinordhvataḥ ||
kaṇakāni kadambāni rātrau deyāni śaṅkare |
divāśeṣāṇi cānyāni divārātrau ca mallikā ||
alābhe caiva puṣpāṇāṃ patrāṇyapi nivedayet |
phalānāmapyabhāve tu tṛṇagulmau nivedayet ||
eteṣāmapyabhāve tu bhaktyā bhavati pūjitaḥ |
svayaṃ patitapuṣpāṇi tyajedupahatāni ca ||
snānaṃ kṛtvā tu ye puṣpaṃ gṛhṇanti jaḍabuddhayaḥ ||
devatāstanna gṛhṇanti na cāpi pitarastathā |
mukulenārcayeccaivamapakvaṃ na nivedayet ||
phalaṃ vyathitapakvaṃ tu yatnātpakvaṃ parityajet |
ghṛtena tilatailena tathā caivauṣadhīrasaiḥ |
dīpaṃ prajvālya deveśe bhaktyā svargapatirbhavet |
tadyathā bhakṣyate bhakṣyaṃ tattathaiva nivedayet ||
anyathā tatpradānena na tatphalamavāpnuyāt |
sakṛduccārito yena mantraḥ paṃcākṣaraḥ śubhaḥ ||
saptajanmakṛtātpāpāt mucyate nātra saṃśayaḥ |
apavitraḥ pavitro vā sarvāvasthāṃ gatopi vā ||
mahāpātakayuktopi mantrasyāsya japācchuciḥ |

p. 129) kṛtvā pradakṣiṇaṃ bhaktyā śivasyāyatanaṃ naraḥ ||
aśvamedhasahasrasya śreṣṭhasya labhate phalam |
daṇḍapramāṇavadbhamau namaskāreṇa yo'rcayet ||
sa yāṃ gatimavāpnoti na tāṃ kratuśatairapi |
tīrthakoṭisahasrāṇi yajñakoṭiśatāni ca ||
mahādevapraṇāmasya kalāṃ nārhanti ṣoḍaśīm |
yajñopavītavāsāṃsi ghaṇṭāvyajanacāmarāḥ ||
gobhūmidāsīnagaraṃ grāmodyānadhvajādikam |
datvā kalpakṣayaṃ yāvacchivasyānucaro bhavet ||
mṛddāruṇeṣṭike śaile yo vai kuryācchivālayam |
triḥsaptakulasaṃyuktaṃ śivaloke mahīyate ||
liṅgapratiṣṭhāṃ kṛtvā tu kulakoṭīḥ samuddharet |
śivakṣetrasamīpasthaḥ sarvā nadyo dvijottama ||
vāpīkūpataḍāgāśca śivagaṃgā iti smṛtāḥ |
gaṃgāsnānasamaṃ tatra snānātpuṇyamudāhṛtam ||
tatra deśe tu durbhikṣaṃ na ca māro pravartate |
nākāle mriyate kaścit pūjyate yatra śaṅkaraḥ ||
kaścit kiñcit yadā paśyecchivaliṃgamapūjitam |
tadā sampūjya yo gacchetsa yāti paramāṃ gatim ||
ciraṃ paryuṣitaṃ mālyaṃ śivasyāpanayettu yaḥ |
gosahasraphalaṃ tasya bhavatīha na saṃśayaḥ ||
gobhūhiraṇyavastrādibalipūjānivedane |
jñeyo namaḥ śivāyeti mantraḥ sarvārthasādhakaḥ ||
sarvamantrādhikaścāyamoṅkārādyaḥ ṣaḍakṣaraḥ |
tanmantrajāpī tatkarmaratastadgatamānasaḥ ||
niṣkāmaḥ puruṣo rājansa rudrapadamaśnute ||

iti śivamāhātmyavarṇanam |

samāpto'yaṃ granthaḥ |
